================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot\analyze.py
================================================================================

import pandas as pd
import glob
import os

def analyze_results():
    # Find all SP2L result CSV files in the directory
    result_files = glob.glob("sp2l_simulation_results_*.csv")
    
    if not result_files:
        print("No simulation result files found. Make sure they start with 'sp2l_simulation_results_'")
        return

    print("="*60)
    print("      SP2L MULTI-ASSET PERFORMANCE ANALYZER      ")
    print("="*60)

    for file_path in result_files:
        asset_name = file_path.replace("sp2l_simulation_results_", "").replace(".csv", "")
        print(f"\nAnalyzing File: {file_path}")
        print(f"Asset: {asset_name}")
        print("-" * 40)
        
        try:
            df = pd.read_csv(file_path)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error reading {file_path}: {e}")
            continue
            
        if df.empty:
            print("File is empty (No trades executed).")
            continue

        # Standard 1% Risk assumption (adjust if you ran simulations with a different risk %)
        risk_usd = 100.0 

        # Core Metrics
        total_trades = len(df)
        wins = df[df['PnL'] > 0]
        losses = df[df['PnL'] <= 0]
        
        win_count = len(wins)
        loss_count = len(losses)
        win_rate = (win_count / total_trades) * 100 if total_trades > 0 else 0
        
        total_pnl = df['PnL'].sum()
        total_commissions = df['Commission'].sum()
        
        # Expectancy & Leverage Math
        avg_commission_per_trade = total_commissions / total_trades if total_trades > 0 else 0
        true_win = risk_usd - avg_commission_per_trade
        true_loss = risk_usd + avg_commission_per_trade
        
        # Break-Even Win Rate Calculation
        break_even_wr = (true_loss / (true_win + true_loss)) * 100 if (true_win + true_loss) > 0 else 0
        
        # Average Stop-Loss Distance (to visualize the micro-stop issue)
        avg_sl_distance = df['SL_Distance'].mean()
        
        # Display Results
        print(f"Total Executions:      {total_trades}")
        print(f"Win Rate:              {win_rate:.1f}% ({win_count}W / {loss_count}L)")
        print(f"Net PnL:               ${total_pnl:,.2f}")
        print(f"Total Commissions:     ${total_commissions:,.2f}")
        print(f"Avg Commission/Trade:  ${avg_commission_per_trade:.2f}")
        print(f"Avg SL Distance:       {avg_sl_distance:.5f}")
        print(f"True Win (Risk - Fee): ${true_win:.2f}")
        print(f"True Loss (Risk + Fee):-${true_loss:.2f}")
        print(f"Break-Even Win Rate:   {break_even_wr:.1f}%")
        
        # Verdict
        if win_rate > break_even_wr:
            print("Verdict:               PROFITABLE EDGE DETECTED 🟢")
        else:
            print(f"Verdict:               SYSTEM BLEEDING (-{break_even_wr - win_rate:.1f}% short of break-even) 🔴")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    analyze_results()

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot\engine.py
================================================================================

from SP2LTradingConfig import SP2LTradingConfig
from SP2LSimulator import SP2LSimulator
import os

def main():
    print("Loading environment constraints...")
    try:
        # Load constraints to determine the active symbol
        config = SP2LTradingConfig()
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Failed to load configuration. Check your .env file: {e}")
        return

    # Dynamically target the correct CSV file based on the config symbol
    target_strategy = "sp2l"
    data_file = f"{config.symbol.upper()}5.csv" 
    
    # Pre-flight checks
    if not os.path.exists(data_file):
        print(f"Error: Could not locate the data file '{data_file}'.")
        print("Please ensure your CSV is in the same directory as this script.")
        return

    if target_strategy == "sp2l":
        print(f"Initializing SP2L Engine for {config.symbol} on {data_file}...")
        simulator = SP2LSimulator(config)
        print("Running historical data. Executing trades...")
        simulator.run_simulation()
    else:
        print(f"Strategy '{target_strategy}' is not implemented.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot\sample-maker.py
================================================================================

import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

start = datetime(2025, 1, 15, 9, 0)
rows = []
price = 2680.0

for i in range(12 * 24 * 30):  # 30 days of 5-min bars
    dt = start + timedelta(minutes=5 * i)
    if dt.weekday() >= 5:
        continue  # skip weekends

    # Occasional high-momentum impulse setup
    if i % 180 == 0:
        open_p = price
        close_p = price + 4.5
        high_p = close_p + 0.3
        low_p = open_p - 0.2
    else:
        open_p = price
        change = np.random.normal(0, 0.4)
        close_p = open_p + change
        high_p = max(open_p, close_p) + abs(np.random.normal(0, 0.2))
        low_p = min(open_p, close_p) - abs(np.random.normal(0, 0.2))

    rows.append({
        'date': dt.strftime('%Y.%m.%d'),
        'time': dt.strftime('%H:%M'),
        'open': round(open_p, 2),
        'high': round(high_p, 2),
        'low': round(low_p, 2),
        'close': round(close_p, 2),
        'volume': int(np.random.randint(200, 1500))
    })
    price = close_p

pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv('xauusd_m5_data.csv', index=False)
print("Generated test 'xauusd_m5_data.csv' with 30 days of 5-minute bars.")

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot\SP2LSimulator.py
================================================================================

import pandas as pd
from datetime import datetime
import csv

class SP2LSimulator:
    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config
        
        csv_data_path = f"{self.config.symbol}5.csv"
        column_names = ['date', 'time', 'open', 'high', 'low', 'close', 'volume']
        self.data = pd.read_csv(csv_data_path, header=None, names=column_names)
        
        if str(self.data.iloc[0]['date']).strip().lower() == 'date':
            self.data = self.data.iloc[1:].reset_index(drop=True)
        
        self.data['datetime'] = pd.to_datetime(self.data['date'].astype(str) + ' ' + self.data['time'].astype(str), format='%Y.%m.%d %H:%M')
        self.data['ema20'] = self.data['close'].ewm(span=20, adjust=False).mean()
        
        print("\n" + "="*50)
        print(" ENGINE CONFIGURATION: 1:1 ")
        print("="*50)
        print("--- ACCOUNT & RISK ---")
        print(f"Starting Balance:       ${self.config.starting_balance:,.2f}")
        print(f"Max Daily Loss:         {self.config.max_daily_loss_pct}%")
        print(f"Max Weekly Loss:        {self.config.max_weekly_loss_pct}%")
        print(f"Risk Per Trade:         {self.config.risk_per_trade_pct}%")
        
        print("\n--- ASSET & SESSION ---")
        print(f"Target Asset:           {self.config.symbol}")
        print(f"Timeframe:              {self.config.timeframe}")
        print(f"London Session:         {self.config.session_1_start} - {self.config.session_1_end}")
        print(f"New York Session:       {self.config.session_2_start} - {self.config.session_2_end}")
        
        print("\n--- STRATEGY CONSTANTS ---")
        print(f"Min Spike Body:         {self.config.min_spike_body_pct}%")
        print(f"Risk/Reward Ratio:      {self.config.risk_reward_ratio}")
        print(f"50% Retracement Limit:  {self.config.use_50_pct_retracement_limit}")
        
        print("\n--- VOLATILITY FILTERS ---")
        print(f"ATR Filter:             {self.config.use_atr_filter}")
        print(f"Volume Filter:          {self.config.use_volume_filter}")
        print(f"Structure Filter:       {self.config.use_structure_filter}")
        print(f"Max SL (ADR %):         {self.config.max_sl_adr_percent}%")
        
        print("\n--- DYNAMIC SPECS ---")
        print(f"Contract Size:          {self.config.contract_size}")
        print(f"Min Lot Size:           {self.config.min_lot_size}")
        print(f"Spread:                 {self.config.spread}")
        print(f"Commission (Per Lot):   ${self.config.commission_per_lot:.2f}")
        print(f"Min SL Distance:        {self.config.min_sl_distance_points}")
        print(f"Point Scale:            {self.config.point_scale}")
        
        print("\n--- OUTPUT ---")
        print(f"Log File Path:          {self.config.log_file_path}")
        print("="*50 + "\n")

        self.balance = config.starting_balance
        self.daily_loss = 0.0
        self.weekly_loss = 0.0
        self.current_date = None
        self.current_week = None
        
        self.in_trade = False
        self.active_setup = None 
        self.trade_history = []
        self.historical_buffer = [] 
        
        self.state = 'SEARCHING' 
        self.setup_context = None
        self.daily_ranges = self.calculate_daily_ranges(self.data)

    def calculate_daily_ranges(self, df):
        df['date_only'] = df['datetime'].dt.date
        daily_high = df.groupby('date_only')['high'].max()
        daily_low = df.groupby('date_only')['low'].min()
        return (daily_high - daily_low).to_dict()

    def get_adr(self, current_date, period=20):
        past_dates = sorted([d for d in self.daily_ranges.keys() if d < current_date])
        if len(past_dates) < period:
            return None
        recent_ranges = [self.daily_ranges[d] for d in past_dates[-period:]]
        return sum(recent_ranges) / period

    def calculate_lot_size(self, entry_price, stop_loss):
        risk_amount = self.balance * (self.config.risk_per_trade_pct / 100)
        point_risk = abs(entry_price - stop_loss)
        if point_risk == 0: return 0.0
        raw_lots = risk_amount / (point_risk * self.config.contract_size)
        return max(self.config.min_lot_size, round(raw_lots - 0.005, 2))

    def reset_risk_limits(self, candle_time):
        candle_date = candle_time.date()
        candle_week = candle_time.isocalendar()[1]
        if self.current_date != candle_date:
            self.daily_loss = 0.0
            self.current_date = candle_date
        if self.current_week != candle_week:
            self.weekly_loss = 0.0
            self.current_week = candle_week

    def is_tradable_time(self, candle_time):
        if candle_time.weekday() >= 5: return False
        time_str = candle_time.strftime("%H:%M")
        
        in_session_1 = self.config.session_1_start <= time_str <= self.config.session_1_end
        in_session_2 = self.config.session_2_start <= time_str <= self.config.session_2_end
        
        return in_session_1 or in_session_2

    def calculate_true_range(self, current, previous):
        hl = current['high'] - current['low']
        hc = abs(current['high'] - previous['close'])
        lc = abs(current['low'] - previous['close'])
        return max(hl, hc, lc)

    def close_single_position(self, setup, pos, exit_reason, exit_price, exit_time):
        if setup['type'] == 'LONG':
            pnl = (exit_price - pos['entry_price']) * pos['volume'] * self.config.contract_size
        else:
            pnl = (pos['entry_price'] - exit_price) * pos['volume'] * self.config.contract_size
        
        pnl -= pos['commission']
        self.balance += pnl
        
        if pnl < 0:
            self.daily_loss += abs(pnl)
            self.weekly_loss += abs(pnl)
            
        self.trade_history.append({
            'Setup_Time': setup['setup_time'],
            'Exit_Time': exit_time,
            'Entry_Reason': setup.get('reason', 'N/A'),
            'Spike_Size': round(setup.get('spike_size', 0), self.config.point_scale),
            'SL_Distance': round(abs(pos['entry_price'] - setup['sl']), self.config.point_scale),
            'Wait_Candles': setup.get('wait_count', 0),
            'Exit_Reason': exit_reason,
            'Type': setup['type'],
            'Strategy': setup['tp_strategy'],
            'Volume': pos['volume'],
            'Entry': round(pos['entry_price'], self.config.point_scale),
            'Stop_Loss': round(setup['sl'], self.config.point_scale),
            'Take_Profit': round(pos['tp'], self.config.point_scale),
            'Exit_Price': round(exit_price, self.config.point_scale),
            'Commission': round(pos['commission'], 2),
            'PnL': round(pnl, 2),
            'Balance_After': round(self.balance, 2)
        })

    def execute_trade(self, setup, entry_price, candle_time, reason):
        sl = setup['sl']
        distance = abs(entry_price - sl)
        total_vol = self.calculate_lot_size(entry_price, sl)
        
        if total_vol < self.config.min_lot_size:
            return

        # STRICT 1:1 RISK-TO-REWARD ON 50% LIMIT ENTRY
        tp1 = entry_price + (distance * 1.0) if setup['type'] == 'LONG' else entry_price - (distance * 1.0)
        
        positions = [{
            'entry_price': entry_price, 
            'volume': total_vol, 
            'tp': tp1, 
            'commission': self.config.calculate_commission(total_vol)
        }]

        setup['positions'] = positions
        setup['reason'] = reason
        setup['tp_strategy'] = 'LIMIT_50_1R'
        
        self.in_trade = True
        self.active_setup = setup

    def run_simulation(self):
        for index, row in self.data.iterrows():
            candle_time = row['datetime']
            self.reset_risk_limits(candle_time)
            
            if self.in_trade and self.active_setup:
                setup = self.active_setup
                type_ = setup['type']
                sl = setup['sl']
                
                remaining_positions = []
                for pos in setup['positions']:
                    hit_sl = (type_ == 'LONG' and row['low'] <= sl) or (type_ == 'SHORT' and row['high'] >= sl)
                    hit_tp = (type_ == 'LONG' and row['high'] >= pos['tp']) or (type_ == 'SHORT' and row['low'] <= pos['tp'])

                    if hit_sl:
                        self.close_single_position(setup, pos, "SL", sl, candle_time)
                    elif hit_tp:
                        self.close_single_position(setup, pos, "TP", pos['tp'], candle_time)
                    else:
                        remaining_positions.append(pos)
                
                setup['positions'] = remaining_positions
                if len(setup['positions']) == 0:
                    self.in_trade = False
                    self.active_setup = None
                
                self.historical_buffer.append(row)
                if len(self.historical_buffer) > 60: self.historical_buffer.pop(0)
                continue
            
            self.historical_buffer.append(row)
            if len(self.historical_buffer) > 60: self.historical_buffer.pop(0)

            if len(self.historical_buffer) < 55: continue
            
            if not self.is_tradable_time(candle_time) or self.daily_loss >= self.config.daily_loss_limit_usd or self.weekly_loss >= self.config.weekly_loss_limit_usd:
                self.state = 'SEARCHING'
                continue

            if self.state == 'WAITING_CORRECTION':
                setup = self.setup_context
                setup['wait_count'] += 1
                executed = False
                
                # TIME-STOP: Discard if correction exceeds 4 candles
                if setup['wait_count'] > 4:
                    self.state = 'SEARCHING'
                    continue
                
                # 50% LIMIT EXECUTION
                if setup['type'] == 'LONG':
                    if row['low'] <= setup['sl']:
                        self.state = 'SEARCHING'
                    elif row['low'] <= setup['limit_price']: 
                        entry_price = setup['limit_price'] + self.config.spread
                        executed = True
                            
                elif setup['type'] == 'SHORT':
                    if row['high'] >= setup['sl']:
                        self.state = 'SEARCHING'
                    elif row['high'] >= setup['limit_price']:
                        entry_price = setup['limit_price'] 
                        executed = True

                if executed:
                    direction = "Bullish" if setup['type'] == 'LONG' else "Bearish"
                    reason = f"{direction} Spike -> 50% Limit Hit (1:1 Target)"
                    self.execute_trade(setup, entry_price, candle_time, reason)
                    self.state = 'SEARCHING'
                continue

            if self.state == 'SEARCHING':
                c1 = self.historical_buffer[-3]
                c2 = self.historical_buffer[-2]
                c3 = self.historical_buffer[-1]
                
                filters_passed = True
                
                if self.config.use_structure_filter:
                    past_50 = self.historical_buffer[-53:-3]
                    rolling_high = max([c['high'] for c in past_50])
                    rolling_low = min([c['low'] for c in past_50])
                else:
                    rolling_high, rolling_low = float('inf'), float('-inf')

                c2_range = c2['high'] - c2['low']
                c2_body = abs(c2['close'] - c2['open'])
                c2_mom_pct = (c2_body / c2_range) * 100 if c2_range > 0 else 0

                # P-Gap check integrated into validation
                is_bullish = (c2['close'] > c2['open']) and (c2_mom_pct >= self.config.min_spike_body_pct) and (c1['high'] < c3['low'])
                is_bearish = (c2['close'] < c2['open']) and (c2_mom_pct >= self.config.min_spike_body_pct) and (c1['low'] > c3['high'])
                
                if self.config.use_structure_filter:
                    if c3['high'] <= rolling_high: is_bullish = False
                    if c3['low'] >= rolling_low: is_bearish = False

                if filters_passed and is_bullish:
                    sl_dist = c3['high'] - c1['low']
                    adr = self.get_adr(candle_time.date())
                    max_sl = adr * (self.config.max_sl_adr_percent / 100.0) if adr else float('inf')
                    
                    if sl_dist >= self.config.min_sl_distance_points and sl_dist <= max_sl:
                        self.state = 'WAITING_CORRECTION'
                        limit_price = c1['low'] + (sl_dist * 0.5)
                        self.setup_context = {'type': 'LONG', 'sl': c1['low'], 'trigger': c3['high'], 'limit_price': limit_price, 'wait_count': 0, 'setup_time': candle_time, 'spike_size': sl_dist}
                
                elif filters_passed and is_bearish:
                    sl_dist = c1['high'] - c3['low']
                    adr = self.get_adr(candle_time.date())
                    max_sl = adr * (self.config.max_sl_adr_percent / 100.0) if adr else float('inf')
                    
                    if sl_dist >= self.config.min_sl_distance_points and sl_dist <= max_sl:
                        self.state = 'WAITING_CORRECTION'
                        limit_price = c1['high'] - (sl_dist * 0.5)
                        self.setup_context = {'type': 'SHORT', 'sl': c1['high'], 'trigger': c3['low'], 'limit_price': limit_price, 'wait_count': 0, 'setup_time': candle_time, 'spike_size': sl_dist}

        self.export_results()

    def export_results(self):
        if not self.trade_history:
            print("\nSimulation finished. No valid SP2L trades triggered.")
            return

        keys = self.trade_history[0].keys()
        with open(self.config.log_file_path, 'w', newline='') as f:
            dict_writer = csv.DictWriter(f, keys)
            dict_writer.writeheader()
            dict_writer.writerows(self.trade_history)

        df_trades = pd.DataFrame(self.trade_history)
        total_executions = len(df_trades)
        winning_executions = df_trades[df_trades['PnL'] > 0]
        losing_executions = df_trades[df_trades['PnL'] <= 0]
        
        win_rate = (len(winning_executions) / total_executions) * 100 if total_executions > 0 else 0
        total_pnl = df_trades['PnL'].sum()
        total_commissions = df_trades['Commission'].sum()
        gross_profit = winning_executions['PnL'].sum()
        gross_loss = abs(losing_executions['PnL'].sum())
        profit_factor = round(gross_profit / gross_loss, 2) if gross_loss > 0 else float('inf')

        print("\n" + "="*45)
        print(f"      {self.config.symbol} SP2L SIMULATION REPORT      ")
        print("="*45)
        print(f"Starting Balance:       ${self.config.starting_balance:,.2f}")
        print(f"Final Balance:          ${self.balance:,.2f}")
        print(f"Net Profit/Loss:        ${total_pnl:,.2f} ({((self.balance/self.config.starting_balance)-1)*100:+.2f}%)")
        print(f"Total Pos. Executions:  {total_executions}")
        print(f"Position Win Rate:      {win_rate:.1f}% ({len(winning_executions)}W / {len(losing_executions)}L)")
        print(f"Profit Factor:          {profit_factor}")
        print(f"Total Commissions Paid: ${total_commissions:,.2f}")
        print("="*45)
        print(f"Detailed ledger saved to: {self.config.log_file_path}\n")

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot\SP2LTradingConfig.py
================================================================================

from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict
from pydantic import Field, model_validator

class SP2LTradingConfig(BaseSettings):
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_file='.env', env_file_encoding='utf-8')

    # Account Risk Constraints
    starting_balance: float = Field(..., gt=0)
    max_daily_loss_pct: float = Field(..., gt=0, le=100)
    max_weekly_loss_pct: float = Field(..., gt=0, le=100)
    risk_per_trade_pct: float = Field(..., gt=0, le=100)

    # Core Asset Definition
    symbol: str = Field(...)
    
    # Session Timing (Dual Windows - Updated to 07:00 London Open)
    session_1_start: str = Field(default="07:00", pattern=r"^(?:[01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$")
    session_1_end: str = Field(default="11:30", pattern=r"^(?:[01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$")
    session_2_start: str = Field(default="12:30", pattern=r"^(?:[01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$")
    session_2_end: str = Field(default="16:00", pattern=r"^(?:[01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$")

    # SP2L Strategy Constants
    timeframe: str = Field(pattern="^(M1|M5)$")
    min_spike_body_pct: float = Field(default=65.0, ge=0, le=100)
    risk_reward_ratio: float = Field(default=1.0)
    use_50_pct_retracement_limit: bool = Field(default=False)
    
    # Volatility Filter Toggles
    use_atr_filter: bool = Field(default=False)
    use_volume_filter: bool = Field(default=False)
    use_structure_filter: bool = Field(default=False)
    max_sl_adr_percent: float = Field(default=50.0)
    
    # Output
    log_file_path: str = Field(default="sp2l_simulation_results.csv")

    # Dynamic Fields
    contract_size: float = 100.0
    min_lot_size: float = 0.01
    spread: float = 1.5
    commission_per_lot: float = 7.00
    min_sl_distance_points: float = 3.0
    point_scale: int = 2 

    @model_validator(mode='after')
    def set_dynamic_asset_specs(self) -> 'SP2LTradingConfig':
        sym = self.symbol.upper()
        
        # Auto-name the log file based on the active symbol
        self.log_file_path = f"sp2l_simulation_results_{sym}.csv"
        
        if sym == "XAUUSD":
            self.contract_size = 100.0       
            self.min_lot_size = 0.01
            self.spread = 1.5                
            self.commission_per_lot = 7.00
            
            # Gold: Maintained at 8.0 Points ($8.00)
            self.min_sl_distance_points = 8.0 
            self.point_scale = 2

        # 7$ fee
        # elif sym == "EURUSD":
        #     self.contract_size = 100000.0    
        #     self.min_lot_size = 0.01
        #     self.spread = 0.00002            
        #     self.commission_per_lot = 7.00
            
        #     # EURUSD Volatility Expansion: 15.0 Pips
        #     # This ensures the 50% limit SL is at least 7.5 pips
        #     self.min_sl_distance_points = 0.00150 
        #     self.point_scale = 5
            
        # elif sym == "GBPUSD":
        #     self.contract_size = 100000.0
        #     self.min_lot_size = 0.01
        #     self.spread = 0.00005            
        #     self.commission_per_lot = 7.00
            
        #     # GBPUSD Volatility Expansion: 18.0 Pips
        #     # This ensures the 50% limit SL is at least 9.0 pips
        #     self.min_sl_distance_points = 0.00180 
        #     self.point_scale = 5
        
        # no commission, only spread
        elif sym == "EURUSD":
            self.contract_size = 100000.0    
            self.min_lot_size = 0.01
            # STANDARD ACCOUNT: 1.0 Pip Spread, ZERO Commission
            self.spread = 0.00010            
            self.commission_per_lot = 0.00   
            self.min_sl_distance_points = 0.00060 # Back to optimal 6.0 pips
            self.point_scale = 5
            
        elif sym == "GBPUSD":
            self.contract_size = 100000.0
            self.min_lot_size = 0.01
            # STANDARD ACCOUNT: 1.2 Pip Spread, ZERO Commission
            self.spread = 0.00012            
            self.commission_per_lot = 0.00   
            self.min_sl_distance_points = 0.00085 # Back to optimal 8.5 pips
            self.point_scale = 5

        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Asset specifications for symbol '{sym}' are not mapped.")
            
        return self

    @property
    def daily_loss_limit_usd(self) -> float:
        return self.starting_balance * (self.max_daily_loss_pct / 100)

    @property
    def weekly_loss_limit_usd(self) -> float:
        return self.starting_balance * (self.max_weekly_loss_pct / 100)

    def calculate_commission(self, lot_size: float) -> float:
        return self.commission_per_lot * lot_size

from config.datasets import DATASETS
from config.instrument import XAUUSD
from config.trading import TRADING_CONFIG
from data.loader import load_dataset

from backtest.engine import BacktestEngine
from backtest.metrics import MetricsCalculator
from strategies.builders import build_sp2l_strategy

def main():
    config = DATASETS["A"]

    candles = load_dataset(
        config.path,
        config.name,
    )

    strategy = build_sp2l_strategy()

    engine = BacktestEngine(
        candles=candles,
        instrument=XAUUSD,
        trading_config=TRADING_CONFIG,

        # 5.000 XAUUSD = 5,000 ticks
        stop_loss_distance=5000,

        # 10.000 XAUUSD = 10,000 ticks
        take_profit_distance=10000,
    )

    result = engine.run(strategy)

    metrics = MetricsCalculator().calculate(result)

    print("=" * 60)
    print("BACKTEST RESULTS")
    print("=" * 60)

    print(f"Dataset:           {config.name}")
    print(f"Candles:           {len(candles):,}")

    print("\n--- Account ---")
    print(f"Starting balance:  ${metrics.starting_balance:,.2f}")
    print(f"Ending balance:    ${metrics.ending_balance:,.2f}")
    print(f"Net profit:        ${metrics.net_profit:,.2f}")
    print(f"Return:            {metrics.return_percent:.2f}%")

    print("\n--- Trades ---")
    print(f"Total trades:      {metrics.total_trades:,}")
    print(f"Winning trades:    {metrics.winning_trades:,}")
    print(f"Losing trades:     {metrics.losing_trades:,}")
    print(f"Win rate:          {metrics.win_rate_percent:.2f}%")
    print(f"Profit factor:     {metrics.profit_factor:.2f}")
    print(f"Average trade:     ${metrics.average_trade:,.3f}")
    print(f"Largest win:       ${metrics.largest_win:,.3f}")
    print(f"Largest loss:      ${metrics.largest_loss:,.3f}")
    print(
        f"Max losing streak: "
        f"{metrics.max_consecutive_losses}"
    )

    print("\n--- Risk ---")
    print(f"Max drawdown:      ${metrics.max_drawdown:,.2f}")
    print(f"Max drawdown:      {metrics.max_drawdown_percent:.2f}%")
    print(f"Exposure:          {metrics.exposure_percent:.2f}%")
    print(
        f"Time in market:    "
        f"{metrics.total_time_in_market}"
    )

    print("\n--- Open Position ---")

    if result.open_trade is not None:
        print("Open trade:        YES")
        print(f"Direction:         {result.open_trade.direction}")
        print(f"Lot size:          {result.open_trade.lot_size}")
        print(f"Entry time:        {result.open_trade.entry_time}")
        print(
            f"Final floating P&L: "
            f"${result.final_floating_pnl:,.2f}"
        )
    else:
        print("Open trade:        NO")


    print("=" * 60)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

import os

def concatenate_py_files(output_filename="consolidated_code.txt"):
    parent_dir = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.getcwd(), ".."))
    
    # Directories to completely ignore
    exclude_dirs = {'__pycache__', 'venv', '.venv', 'env', 'Lib', 'site-packages'}
    
    with open(output_filename, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as outfile:
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(parent_dir):
            # Modify dirs in-place to skip excluded directories
            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in exclude_dirs]
                
            for file in files:
                if file.endswith(".py"):
                    file_path = os.path.join(root, file)
                    relative_path = os.path.relpath(file_path, parent_dir)
                    
                    try:
                        with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as infile:
                            content = infile.read()
                            
                            outfile.write(f"{'='*80}\n")
                            outfile.write(f"FILE: {relative_path}\n")
                            outfile.write(f"{'='*80}\n\n")
                            outfile.write(content)
                            outfile.write("\n\n")
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"Skipping {relative_path} due to error: {e}")
                        
    print(f"Done! Merged into '{output_filename}'.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    concatenate_py_files()

FILE: trade-bot-gpt\analysis\data_quality.py

from collections import Counter
from datetime import timedelta

from config.datasets import DATASETS
from data.loader import load_dataset


EXPECTED_INTERVAL = timedelta(minutes=5)


def analyze_dataset(dataset_key: str) -> None:
    config = DATASETS[dataset_key]
    candles = load_dataset(config.path, config.name)

    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print(f"DATASET: {config.name}")
    print("=" * 70)

    print(f"Candles: {len(candles):,}")
    print(f"Start:   {candles[0].timestamp}")
    print(f"End:     {candles[-1].timestamp}")

    analyze_intervals(candles)
    analyze_weekends(candles)
    analyze_suspicious_gaps(candles)
    analyze_duplicates(candles)
    analyze_price_data(candles)


def analyze_intervals(candles) -> None:
    intervals = []

    for previous, current in zip(candles, candles[1:]):
        intervals.append(current.timestamp - previous.timestamp)

    counts = Counter(intervals)

    print("\n--- Candle intervals ---")

    for interval, count in counts.most_common(15):
        print(f"{str(interval):>15} : {count:,}")

    normal = counts.get(EXPECTED_INTERVAL, 0)

    print(f"\nNormal 5-minute transitions: {normal:,}")
    print(f"Non-5-minute transitions:   {len(intervals) - normal:,}")

    print("\nLargest gaps:")

    large_gaps = sorted(
        (
            (interval, index)
            for index, interval in enumerate(intervals)
            if interval > EXPECTED_INTERVAL
        ),
        reverse=True,
    )[:20]

    for interval, index in large_gaps:
        previous = candles[index]
        current = candles[index + 1]

        print(
            f"{previous.timestamp} -> {current.timestamp} "
            f"= {interval}"
        )


def analyze_weekends(candles) -> None:
    print("\n--- Weekend gaps ---")

    weekend_gaps = []

    for previous, current in zip(candles, candles[1:]):
        interval = current.timestamp - previous.timestamp

        if interval <= EXPECTED_INTERVAL:
            continue

        # Python:
        # Monday = 0
        # Saturday = 5
        # Sunday = 6

        if (
            previous.timestamp.weekday() >= 4
            or current.timestamp.weekday() >= 5
        ):
            weekend_gaps.append(
                (
                    previous.timestamp,
                    current.timestamp,
                    interval,
                )
            )

    print(f"Weekend-related gaps: {len(weekend_gaps):,}")

    for previous, current, interval in weekend_gaps[:10]:
        print(
            f"{previous} -> {current} = {interval}"
        )


def analyze_duplicates(candles) -> None:
    print("\n--- Duplicate timestamps ---")

    timestamps = [candle.timestamp for candle in candles]
    counts = Counter(timestamps)

    duplicates = [
        (timestamp, count)
        for timestamp, count in counts.items()
        if count > 1
    ]

    print(f"Duplicate timestamps: {len(duplicates):,}")

    for timestamp, count in duplicates[:20]:
        print(f"{timestamp}: {count} occurrences")


def analyze_price_data(candles) -> None:
    print("\n--- Price data ---")

    invalid = []

    for candle in candles:
        if candle.high < max(candle.open, candle.close):
            invalid.append(candle)
            continue

        if candle.low > min(candle.open, candle.close):
            invalid.append(candle)
            continue

        if candle.low > candle.high:
            invalid.append(candle)

    print(f"Invalid OHLC candles: {len(invalid):,}")

    if invalid:
        for candle in invalid[:10]:
            print(candle)

def analyze_suspicious_gaps(candles) -> None:
    print("\n--- Suspicious gaps ---")

    suspicious = []

    for previous, current in zip(candles, candles[1:]):
        interval = current.timestamp - previous.timestamp

        if interval <= EXPECTED_INTERVAL:
            continue

        previous_weekday = previous.timestamp.weekday()
        current_weekday = current.timestamp.weekday()

        # Ignore obvious weekend closures.
        if previous_weekday == 4 and current_weekday in (6, 0):
            continue

        suspicious.append(
            (previous.timestamp, current.timestamp, interval)
        )

    print(f"Suspicious gaps: {len(suspicious):,}")

    for previous, current, interval in suspicious[:50]:
        print(
            f"{previous} -> {current} = {interval}"
        )
if __name__ == "__main__":
    analyze_dataset("A")
    analyze_dataset("B")

from decimal import Decimal


class Account:
    def __init__(self, starting_balance: str):
        self.starting_balance = Decimal(starting_balance)
        self.balance = self.starting_balance
        self.floating_pnl = Decimal("0")

    @property
    def equity(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.balance + self.floating_pnl

    def apply_pnl(self, pnl: Decimal) -> None:
        self.balance += pnl

    def update_floating_pnl(self, floating_pnl: Decimal) -> None:
        self.floating_pnl = floating_pnl

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\backtest\engine.py
================================================================================

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from decimal import Decimal

from backtest.account import Account
from backtest.risk import RiskManager
from backtest.signal import Signal
from backtest.trade import Trade
from config.instrument import InstrumentConfig
from config.trading import TradingConfig
from models.candle import Candle
from models.price import PriceSpec
from backtest.position_sizing import PositionSizer
from backtest.result import BacktestResult
from backtest.equity import EquityPoint

from strategies.base import Strategy

@dataclass
class PendingOrder:
    signal: Signal
    signal_time: datetime


class BacktestEngine:
    def __init__(
        self,
        candles: list[Candle],
        instrument: InstrumentConfig,
        trading_config: TradingConfig,
        stop_loss_distance: int,
        take_profit_distance: int,
    ):
        self.candles = candles

        self.instrument = instrument
        self.trading_config = trading_config
        self.position_sizer = PositionSizer(trading_config)

        self.price_spec = PriceSpec(
            instrument.tick_size,
            instrument.pip_size,
        )

        self.spread_ticks = self.price_spec.pips_to_ticks(
            trading_config.spread_pips
        )

        self.half_spread_ticks = self.spread_ticks // 2

        self.account = Account(
            str(trading_config.starting_balance)
        )

        self.risk_manager = RiskManager(
            trading_config
        )

        self.stop_loss_distance = stop_loss_distance
        self.take_profit_distance = take_profit_distance

        self.current_trade: Trade | None = None
        self.completed_trades: list[Trade] = []
        self.open_trade_at_end: Trade | None = None
        self.final_floating_pnl = Decimal("0")
        self.equity_curve: list[EquityPoint] = []
        self.pending_order: PendingOrder | None = None

    def run(self, strategy: Strategy) -> BacktestResult:
        for candle in self.candles:

            # 1. CANDLE OPENS
            # Execute the signal generated by the previous
            # completed candle.
            if self.current_trade is None:
                self.process_pending_order(candle)

            # 2. INTRACANDLE MOVEMENT
            # Existing positions can hit SL/TP.
            self.process_open_trade(candle)

            # 4. EQUITY CURVE
            # Record the equity at the close of the candle.
            self.equity_curve.append(
                EquityPoint(
                    timestamp=candle.timestamp,
                    equity=self.account.equity,
                )
            )
            # 4. CANDLE CLOSES
            # Strategy sees only the completed candle.
            signal = strategy.on_candle(candle)

            # 5. QUEUE SIGNAL FOR NEXT CANDLE
            if self.current_trade is None and signal != Signal.NONE:
                self.pending_order = PendingOrder(
                    signal=signal,
                    signal_time=candle.timestamp,
                )
            else:
                self.pending_order = None
        self.finalize_open_trade()
        return BacktestResult(
            starting_balance=self.account.starting_balance,
            ending_balance=self.account.balance,
            completed_trades=self.completed_trades,
            open_trade=self.open_trade_at_end,
            final_floating_pnl=self.final_floating_pnl,
            equity_curve=self.equity_curve,
        )

    def process_pending_order(self, candle: Candle) -> None:
        if self.pending_order is None:
            return

        order = self.pending_order
        self.pending_order = None

        # Risk limits are checked at the moment the order
        # would actually execute.
        if not self.risk_manager.can_open_trade(
            candle.timestamp
        ):
            return

        if order.signal == Signal.BUY:
            self.open_buy(candle, order.signal_time)

        elif order.signal == Signal.SELL:
            self.open_sell(candle, order.signal_time)

    def open_buy(
        self,
        candle: Candle,
        signal_time: datetime,
    ) -> None:
        reference_open_ticks = self.price_spec.to_ticks(
            candle.open
        )

        # BUY executes at ASK.
        entry_ticks = (
            reference_open_ticks + self.half_spread_ticks
        )

        stop_loss_ticks = (
            entry_ticks - self.stop_loss_distance
        )

        take_profit_ticks = (
            entry_ticks + self.take_profit_distance
        )

        lot_size = Decimal(
    str(self.trading_config.default_lot)
)

        self.position_sizer.validate_lot_size(lot_size)

        self.current_trade = Trade(
            direction="BUY",
            lot_size=lot_size,
            signal_time=signal_time,
            entry_time=candle.timestamp,
            entry_ticks=entry_ticks,
            stop_loss_ticks=stop_loss_ticks,
            take_profit_ticks=take_profit_ticks,
        )

    def open_sell(
        self,
        candle: Candle,
        signal_time: datetime,
    ) -> None:
        reference_open_ticks = self.price_spec.to_ticks(
            candle.open
        )

        # SELL executes at BID.
        entry_ticks = (
            reference_open_ticks - self.half_spread_ticks
        )

        stop_loss_ticks = (
            entry_ticks + self.stop_loss_distance
        )

        take_profit_ticks = (
            entry_ticks - self.take_profit_distance
        )

        lot_size = Decimal(
            str(self.trading_config.default_lot)
        )

        self.position_sizer.validate_lot_size(lot_size)

        self.current_trade = Trade(
            direction="SELL",
            lot_size=lot_size,
            signal_time=signal_time,
            entry_time=candle.timestamp,
            entry_ticks=entry_ticks,
            stop_loss_ticks=stop_loss_ticks,
            take_profit_ticks=take_profit_ticks,
        )

    def process_open_trade(self, candle: Candle) -> None:
        if self.current_trade is None:
            self.account.update_floating_pnl(Decimal("0"))
            self.risk_manager.update_floating_pnl(Decimal("0"))
            return

        floating_pnl = self.calculate_floating_pnl(candle)

        self.account.update_floating_pnl(floating_pnl)
        self.risk_manager.update_floating_pnl(floating_pnl)

        trade = self.current_trade

        reference_high_ticks = self.price_spec.to_ticks(candle.high)
        reference_low_ticks = self.price_spec.to_ticks(candle.low)

        if trade.direction == "BUY":
            self.check_buy_exit(
                candle,
                trade,
                reference_low_ticks,
                reference_high_ticks,
            )
        elif trade.direction == "SELL":
            self.check_sell_exit(
                candle,
                trade,
                reference_low_ticks,
                reference_high_ticks,
            )

    def check_buy_exit(
        self,
        candle: Candle,
        trade: Trade,
        reference_low_ticks: int,
        reference_high_ticks: int,
    ) -> None:
        # BUY exits by selling at BID.
        bid_high_ticks = (
            reference_high_ticks - self.half_spread_ticks
        )

        bid_low_ticks = (
            reference_low_ticks - self.half_spread_ticks
        )

        # Conservative rule:
        # If SL and TP are both touched, assume SL first.
        if bid_low_ticks <= trade.stop_loss_ticks:
            self.close_trade(
                candle,
                trade.stop_loss_ticks,
                "stop_loss",
            )
            return

        if bid_high_ticks >= trade.take_profit_ticks:
            self.close_trade(
                candle,
                trade.take_profit_ticks,
                "take_profit",
            )

    def check_sell_exit(
        self,
        candle: Candle,
        trade: Trade,
        reference_low_ticks: int,
        reference_high_ticks: int,
    ) -> None:
        # SELL exits by buying at ASK.
        ask_high_ticks = (
            reference_high_ticks + self.half_spread_ticks
        )

        ask_low_ticks = (
            reference_low_ticks + self.half_spread_ticks
        )

        # Conservative rule:
        # If SL and TP are both touched, assume SL first.
        if ask_high_ticks >= trade.stop_loss_ticks:
            self.close_trade(
                candle,
                trade.stop_loss_ticks,
                "stop_loss",
            )
            return

        if ask_low_ticks <= trade.take_profit_ticks:
            self.close_trade(
                candle,
                trade.take_profit_ticks,
                "take_profit",
            )

    def close_trade(
        self,
        candle: Candle,
        exit_ticks: int,
        exit_reason: str,
    ) -> None:
        trade = self.current_trade

        if trade is None:
            return

        trade.exit_time = candle.timestamp
        trade.exit_ticks = exit_ticks
        trade.exit_reason = exit_reason

        if trade.direction == "BUY":
            trade.profit_ticks = (
                exit_ticks - trade.entry_ticks
            )
        else:
            trade.profit_ticks = (
                trade.entry_ticks - exit_ticks
            )

        price_difference = (
            Decimal(trade.profit_ticks)
            * self.price_spec.tick_size
        )

        gross_profit = (
            price_difference
            * Decimal(
                str(self.instrument.contract_size_oz)
            )
            * trade.lot_size
        )

        commission = (
            Decimal(
                str(
                    self.trading_config.commission_per_lot
                )
            )
            * trade.lot_size
        )

        net_profit = gross_profit - commission

        trade.gross_profit = gross_profit
        trade.commission = commission
        trade.net_profit = net_profit

        self.account.apply_pnl(net_profit)

        self.account.update_floating_pnl(Decimal("0"))
        self.risk_manager.update_floating_pnl(Decimal("0"))

        self.risk_manager.update(trade)

        self.completed_trades.append(trade)

        self.current_trade = None

    def calculate_floating_pnl(self, candle: Candle) -> Decimal:
        if self.current_trade is None:
            return Decimal("0")

        trade = self.current_trade

        reference_price_ticks = self.price_spec.to_ticks(
            candle.close
        )

        if trade.direction == "BUY":
            exit_ticks = (
                reference_price_ticks
                - self.half_spread_ticks
            )
            profit_ticks = (
                exit_ticks - trade.entry_ticks
            )
        else:
            exit_ticks = (
                reference_price_ticks
                + self.half_spread_ticks
            )
            profit_ticks = (
                trade.entry_ticks - exit_ticks
            )

        price_difference = (
            Decimal(profit_ticks)
            * self.price_spec.tick_size
        )

        gross_profit = (
            price_difference
            * Decimal(str(self.instrument.contract_size_oz))
            * trade.lot_size
        )

        return gross_profit

    def finalize_open_trade(self) -> None:
        if self.current_trade is None:
            self.open_trade_at_end = None
            self.final_floating_pnl = Decimal("0")
            return

        self.open_trade_at_end = self.current_trade

        if not self.candles:
            self.final_floating_pnl = Decimal("0")
            return

        final_candle = self.candles[-1]

        self.final_floating_pnl = (
            self.calculate_floating_pnl(final_candle)
        )

        self.account.update_floating_pnl(
            self.final_floating_pnl
        )

        self.risk_manager.update_floating_pnl(
            self.final_floating_pnl
        )

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from decimal import Decimal


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EquityPoint:
    timestamp: datetime
    equity: Decimal

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\backtest\metrics.py
================================================================================

from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_UP
from datetime import timedelta
from backtest.result import BacktestResult


@dataclass
class BacktestMetrics:
    starting_balance: Decimal
    ending_balance: Decimal
    net_profit: Decimal
    return_percent: Decimal

    total_trades: int
    winning_trades: int
    losing_trades: int
    max_consecutive_losses: int
    win_rate_percent: Decimal
    profit_factor: Decimal

    average_trade: Decimal
    largest_win: Decimal
    largest_loss: Decimal

    max_drawdown: Decimal
    max_drawdown_percent: Decimal

    total_time_in_market: timedelta
    exposure_percent: Decimal


class MetricsCalculator:
    def calculate(self, result: BacktestResult) -> BacktestMetrics:
        trades = result.completed_trades
        total_trades = len(trades)

        winning_trades = sum(
            1
            for trade in trades
            if trade.net_profit is not None
            and trade.net_profit > 0
        )

        losing_trades = sum(
            1
            for trade in trades
            if trade.net_profit is not None
            and trade.net_profit < 0
        )

        # Trade profit lists
        profits = [
            trade.net_profit
            for trade in trades
            if trade.net_profit is not None
            and trade.net_profit > 0
        ]

        losses = [
            trade.net_profit
            for trade in trades
            if trade.net_profit is not None
            and trade.net_profit < 0
        ]

        # Win rate
        if total_trades > 0:
            win_rate_percent = (
                Decimal(winning_trades)
                / Decimal(total_trades)
                * Decimal("100")
            ).quantize(
                Decimal("0.01"),
                rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP,
            )
        else:
            win_rate_percent = Decimal("0.00")

        # Net profit and return
        net_profit = result.net_profit

        if result.starting_balance != 0:
            return_percent = (
                net_profit
                / result.starting_balance
                * Decimal("100")
            ).quantize(
                Decimal("0.01"),
                rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP,
            )
        else:
            return_percent = Decimal("0.00")

        # Average trade
        if total_trades > 0:
            total_trade_profit = sum(
                (
                    trade.net_profit
                    for trade in trades
                    if trade.net_profit is not None
                ),
                Decimal("0"),
            )

            average_trade = (
                total_trade_profit
                / Decimal(total_trades)
            ).quantize(
                Decimal("0.001"),
                rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP,
            )
        else:
            average_trade = Decimal("0.000")

        # Profit factor
        if losses:
            gross_profit = sum(profits, Decimal("0"))
            gross_loss = sum(losses, Decimal("0"))

            profit_factor = (
                gross_profit / abs(gross_loss)
            ).quantize(
                Decimal("0.01"),
                rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP,
            )
        else:
            profit_factor = Decimal("0.00")

        # Largest win / loss
        largest_win = max(
            profits,
            default=Decimal("0"),
        )

        largest_loss = min(
            losses,
            default=Decimal("0"),
        )

        # Maximum consecutive losses
        max_consecutive_losses = 0
        current_consecutive_losses = 0

        for trade in trades:
            if (
                trade.net_profit is not None
                and trade.net_profit < 0
            ):
                current_consecutive_losses += 1

                max_consecutive_losses = max(
                    max_consecutive_losses,
                    current_consecutive_losses,
                )
            else:
                current_consecutive_losses = 0

        # Maximum drawdown
        max_drawdown = Decimal("0")
        max_drawdown_percent = Decimal("0.00")

        peak_equity = None

        for point in result.equity_curve:
            if (
                peak_equity is None
                or point.equity > peak_equity
            ):
                peak_equity = point.equity

            drawdown = peak_equity - point.equity

            if drawdown > max_drawdown:
                max_drawdown = drawdown

                if peak_equity != 0:
                    max_drawdown_percent = (
                        drawdown
                        / peak_equity
                        * Decimal("100")
                    ).quantize(
                        Decimal("0.01"),
                        rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP,
                    )
                # Market exposure
        total_time_in_market = timedelta(0)
        exposure_percent = Decimal("0.00")

        if result.equity_curve:
            backtest_start = result.equity_curve[0].timestamp
            backtest_end = result.equity_curve[-1].timestamp
            backtest_duration = backtest_end - backtest_start

            for trade in trades:
                if trade.exit_time is not None:
                    total_time_in_market += (
                        trade.exit_time - trade.entry_time
                    )

            if result.open_trade is not None:
                total_time_in_market += (
                    backtest_end
                    - result.open_trade.entry_time
                )

            if backtest_duration.total_seconds() > 0:
                exposure_percent = (
                    Decimal(str(
                        total_time_in_market.total_seconds()
                    ))
                    / Decimal(str(
                        backtest_duration.total_seconds()
                    ))
                    * Decimal("100")
                ).quantize(
                    Decimal("0.01"),
                    rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP,
                )

        return BacktestMetrics(
            starting_balance=result.starting_balance,
            ending_balance=result.ending_balance,
            net_profit=net_profit,
            return_percent=return_percent,
            total_trades=total_trades,
            winning_trades=winning_trades,
            losing_trades=losing_trades,
            max_consecutive_losses=max_consecutive_losses,
            win_rate_percent=win_rate_percent,
            profit_factor=profit_factor,
            average_trade=average_trade,
            largest_win=largest_win,
            largest_loss=largest_loss,
            max_drawdown=max_drawdown,
            max_drawdown_percent=max_drawdown_percent,
            total_time_in_market=total_time_in_market,
            exposure_percent=exposure_percent,
        )

FILE: trade-bot-gpt\backtest\position_sizing.py

from decimal import Decimal

from config.trading import TradingConfig


class PositionSizer:
    def __init__(self, trading_config: TradingConfig):
        self.minimum_lot = Decimal(str(trading_config.minimum_lot))
        self.lot_step = Decimal(str(trading_config.lot_step))

    def validate_lot_size(self, lot_size: Decimal) -> None:
        if lot_size < self.minimum_lot:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Lot size {lot_size} is below the minimum "
                f"of {self.minimum_lot}"
            )

        offset = lot_size - self.minimum_lot

        if offset % self.lot_step != 0:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Lot size {lot_size} is not a valid lot step. "
                f"Minimum: {self.minimum_lot}, "
                f"step: {self.lot_step}"
            )

    def normalize_lot_size(self, lot_size: Decimal) -> Decimal:
        if lot_size < self.minimum_lot:
            return self.minimum_lot

        steps = (
            (lot_size - self.minimum_lot) / self.lot_step
        ).to_integral_value()

        normalized = (
            self.minimum_lot
            + steps * self.lot_step
        )

        self.validate_lot_size(normalized)
        return normalized

from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal
from backtest.equity import EquityPoint
from backtest.trade import Trade


@dataclass
class BacktestResult:
    starting_balance: Decimal
    ending_balance: Decimal
    completed_trades: list[Trade]
    open_trade: Trade | None
    final_floating_pnl: Decimal
    equity_curve: list[EquityPoint]

    @property
    def net_profit(self) -> Decimal:
        return (
            self.ending_balance
            - self.starting_balance
        )

    @property
    def equity(self) -> Decimal:
        return (
            self.ending_balance
            + self.final_floating_pnl
        )

FILE: trade-bot-gpt\backtest\risk.py

from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal

from backtest.trade import Trade
from config.trading import TradingConfig


class RiskManager:
    def __init__(self, trading_config: TradingConfig):
        self.trading_config = trading_config

        self.daily_realized_pnl = Decimal("0")
        self.weekly_realized_pnl = Decimal("0")

        self.floating_pnl = Decimal("0")

        self.current_day: date | None = None
        self.current_week: tuple[int, int] | None = None

    def update(self, trade: Trade) -> None:
        """
        Add a completed trade's net P&L to the current
        daily and weekly totals.
        """

        if trade.exit_time is None:
            return

        if trade.net_profit is None:
            return

        self._update_periods(trade.exit_time)

        self.daily_realized_pnl += trade.net_profit
        self.weekly_realized_pnl += trade.net_profit

    def can_open_trade(self, timestamp: datetime) -> bool:
        """
        Return True if the daily and weekly loss limits
        have not been reached.
        """

        self._update_periods(timestamp)

        if self.daily_loss_reached():
            return False

        if self.weekly_loss_reached():
            return False

        return True

    def daily_loss_reached(self) -> bool:
        return (
            self.current_daily_pnl()
            <= -Decimal(
                str(self.trading_config.maximum_daily_loss)
            )
        )

    def weekly_loss_reached(self) -> bool:
        return (
            self.current_weekly_pnl()
            <= -Decimal(
                str(self.trading_config.maximum_weekly_loss)
            )
        )

    def _update_periods(self, timestamp: datetime) -> None:
        current_day = timestamp.date()

        iso_calendar = timestamp.isocalendar()
        current_week = (
            iso_calendar.year,
            iso_calendar.week,
        )

        if self.current_day != current_day:
            self.current_day = current_day
            self.daily_realized_pnl = Decimal("0")

        if self.current_week != current_week:
            self.current_week = current_week
            self.weekly_realized_pnl = Decimal("0")

    def update_floating_pnl(self, floating_pnl: Decimal) -> None:
        self.floating_pnl = floating_pnl

    def current_daily_pnl(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.daily_realized_pnl + self.floating_pnl

    def current_weekly_pnl(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.weekly_realized_pnl + self.floating_pnl

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\data\loader.py
================================================================================

import csv
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from models.candle import Candle


class DataLoadError(Exception):
    pass


def load_dataset(path: Path, dataset_name: str) -> list[Candle]:
    if not path.exists():
        raise DataLoadError(f"{dataset_name}: file not found: {path}")

    candles: list[Candle] = []

    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as file:
        reader = csv.reader(file)

        try:
            header = next(reader)
        except StopIteration:
            raise DataLoadError(f"{dataset_name}: file is empty")

        header = [column.strip().lower() for column in header]

        for line_number, row in enumerate(reader, start=2):
            if not row or all(not value.strip() for value in row):
                continue

            try:
                timestamp, open_price, high_price, low_price, close_price = (
                    parse_row(row)
                )

            except (ValueError, IndexError) as exc:
                raise DataLoadError(
                    f"{dataset_name}: invalid data on line "
                    f"{line_number}: {row}"
                ) from exc

            validate_ohlc(
                dataset_name,
                line_number,
                timestamp,
                open_price,
                high_price,
                low_price,
                close_price,
            )

            candles.append(
                Candle(
                    timestamp=timestamp,
                    open=open_price,
                    high=high_price,
                    low=low_price,
                    close=close_price,
                )
            )

    if not candles:
        raise DataLoadError(f"{dataset_name}: no candles loaded")

    validate_order(dataset_name, candles)

    return candles


def parse_row(
    row: list[str],
) -> tuple[datetime, float, float, float, float]:

    # Dataset A
    #
    # date and time, open, high, low, close, volume
    #
    # Example:
    # 2023-11-22 09:20,1999.905,2000.005,1999.015,1999.485,5

    if len(row) == 6:
        timestamp = datetime.strptime(
            row[0].strip(),
            "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M",
        )

        open_price = float(row[1])
        high_price = float(row[2])
        low_price = float(row[3])
        close_price = float(row[4])

        return (
            timestamp,
            open_price,
            high_price,
            low_price,
            close_price,
        )

    # Dataset B
    #
    # date, time, open, high, low, close, volume
    #
    # Example:
    # 2026.09.15,09:05,4291.76,4291.76,4286.10,4288.71,908

    if len(row) == 7:
        timestamp = datetime.strptime(
            f"{row[0].strip()} {row[1].strip()}",
            "%Y.%m.%d %H:%M",
        )

        open_price = float(row[2])
        high_price = float(row[3])
        low_price = float(row[4])
        close_price = float(row[5])

        return (
            timestamp,
            open_price,
            high_price,
            low_price,
            close_price,
        )

    raise ValueError(
        f"Unexpected number of columns: {len(row)}"
    )


def validate_ohlc(
    dataset_name: str,
    line_number: int,
    timestamp: datetime,
    open_price: float,
    high_price: float,
    low_price: float,
    close_price: float,
) -> None:

    if high_price < max(open_price, close_price):
        raise DataLoadError(
            f"{dataset_name}: line {line_number}: "
            f"high is lower than open/close"
        )

    if low_price > min(open_price, close_price):
        raise DataLoadError(
            f"{dataset_name}: line {line_number}: "
            f"low is higher than open/close"
        )

    if low_price > high_price:
        raise DataLoadError(
            f"{dataset_name}: line {line_number}: "
            f"low is greater than high"
        )


def validate_order(
    dataset_name: str,
    candles: list[Candle],
) -> None:

    previous_timestamp = None

    for candle in candles:
        if previous_timestamp is not None:
            if candle.timestamp <= previous_timestamp:
                raise DataLoadError(
                    f"{dataset_name}: timestamps are not strictly "
                    f"increasing around {candle.timestamp}"
                )

        previous_timestamp = candle.timestamp

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\models\price.py
================================================================================

from decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_UP


class PriceSpec:
    def __init__(self, tick_size: str, pip_size: str):
        self.tick_size = Decimal(tick_size)
        self.pip_size = Decimal(pip_size)

    def to_ticks(self, price: float | str | Decimal) -> int:
        value = Decimal(str(price))

        return int(
            (value / self.tick_size).quantize(
                Decimal("1"),
                rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP,
            )
        )

    def from_ticks(self, ticks: int) -> Decimal:
        return Decimal(ticks) * self.tick_size

    def pips_to_ticks(
        self,
        pips: Decimal | float | str,
    ) -> int:
        price_distance = Decimal(str(pips)) * self.pip_size

        return int(
            (price_distance / self.tick_size).quantize(
                Decimal("1"),
                rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP,
            )
        )

    def format_price(self, ticks: int) -> str:
        return format(self.from_ticks(ticks), "f")

FILE: trade-bot-gpt\models\timeframe.py
================================================================================

from collections import deque
from datetime import timedelta

from models.candle import Candle


class M15Builder:
    def __init__(self, max_history: int = 20):
        if max_history <= 0:
            raise ValueError("max_history must be greater than zero")

        self.max_history = max_history
        self.history: deque[Candle] = deque(maxlen=max_history)
        self._buffer: list[Candle] = []

    def update(self, candle: Candle) -> Candle | None:
        # No active group: only an M15-aligned candle can start one.
        if not self._buffer:
            if candle.timestamp.minute % 15 != 0:
                return None

            self._buffer.append(candle)
            return None

        # We already have the first candle of the group.
        expected_timestamp = (
            self._buffer[-1].timestamp + timedelta(minutes=5)
        )

        # Any unexpected timestamp invalidates the current group.
        if candle.timestamp != expected_timestamp:
            self._buffer = []

            # The current candle may itself be a valid new M15 starting point.
            if candle.timestamp.minute % 15 == 0:
                self._buffer.append(candle)

            return None

        self._buffer.append(candle)

        # We need exactly three consecutive M5 candles.
        if len(self._buffer) < 3:
            return None

        candles = self._buffer
        self._buffer = []

        m15 = Candle(
            timestamp=candles[0].timestamp,
            open=candles[0].open,
            high=max(c.high for c in candles),
            low=min(c.low for c in candles),
            close=candles[-1].close,
        )

        self.history.append(m15)

        return m15

    @property
    def candles(self) -> tuple[Candle, ...]:
        return tuple(self.history)
