================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\strategies\key_bar.py
================================================================================

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import timedelta

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.signal_bar import SignalBarEvent

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class KeyBarEvent:
    direction: str
    signal_bar: SignalBarEvent
    key_candle: Candle

class KeyBarDetector:
    """
    Detects the candle immediately following the Signal Bar.

    ```
    Bullish:
        - Key candle breaks Signal Bar high.
        - Key candle closes above Signal Bar high.

    Bearish:
        - Key candle breaks Signal Bar low.
        - Key candle closes below Signal Bar low.

    The detector only confirms the setup.
    It does not execute a trade.
    """

    def detect(
        self,
        signal_bar: SignalBarEvent,
        key_candle: Candle,
    ) -> KeyBarEvent | None:

        signal_candle = signal_bar.signal_candle

        expected_timestamp = (
            signal_candle.timestamp + timedelta(minutes=5)
        )

        # The Key Bar must be the immediately following M5 candle.
        # Any gap or later candle invalidates this Signal Bar.
        if key_candle.timestamp != expected_timestamp:
            return None

        if signal_bar.direction == "buy":
            if (
                key_candle.high > signal_candle.high
                and key_candle.close > signal_candle.high
            ):
                return KeyBarEvent(
                    direction="buy",
                    signal_bar=signal_bar,
                    key_candle=key_candle,
                )

        elif signal_bar.direction == "sell":
            if (
                key_candle.low < signal_candle.low
                and key_candle.close < signal_candle.low
            ):
                return KeyBarEvent(
                    direction="sell",
                    signal_bar=signal_bar,
                    key_candle=key_candle,
                )

        return None


================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\strategies\pullback_structure.py
================================================================================

from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal

from strategies.second_leg import SecondLegEvent
from strategies.structure import StructurePoint
from strategies.swing_history import SwingHistory
from models.candle import Candle


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PullbackStructureEvent:
    direction: str
    second_leg: SecondLegEvent
    anchor_candle: Candle
    structure_points: list[StructurePoint]


class PullbackStructureDetector:
    """
    Detects the counter-trend structure sequence inside the SP2L
    second-leg pullback.

    The Second Leg pullback candle is the structural anchor.

    It is NOT counted as structure #1.

    Bullish setup:
        - Second Leg candle is the anchor high.
        - Subsequent lower swing highs form LH #1, LH #2, ...
        - At least 3 consecutive Lower Highs are required.
        - At most 7 consecutive Lower Highs are allowed.
        - An 8th consecutive Lower High invalidates the setup.

    Bearish setup:
        - Second Leg candle is the anchor low.
        - Subsequent higher swing lows form HL #1, HL #2, ...
        - At least 3 consecutive Higher Lows are required.
        - At most 7 consecutive Higher Lows are allowed.
        - An 8th consecutive Higher Low invalidates the setup.

    If the structural condition is broken, the sequence resets and a
    new sequence may begin from the next structural point.

    The detector does not determine:
        - Signal Bar
        - Key Bar
        - entry
        - stop loss
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        history: SwingHistory,
        minimum_length: int = 3,
        maximum_length: int = 7,
    ):
        if minimum_length <= 0:
            raise ValueError("minimum_length must be greater than zero")

        if maximum_length < minimum_length:
            raise ValueError(
                "maximum_length must be greater than or equal to "
                "minimum_length"
            )

        self.history = history
        self.minimum_length = minimum_length
        self.maximum_length = maximum_length

    def detect(
        self,
        second_leg: SecondLegEvent,
    ) -> PullbackStructureEvent | None:

        anchor_candle = second_leg.pullback_candle

        if second_leg.direction == "buy":
            sequence = self._lower_high_sequence(anchor_candle)

        elif second_leg.direction == "sell":
            sequence = self._higher_low_sequence(anchor_candle)

        else:
            return None

        if len(sequence) < self.minimum_length:
            return None

        if len(sequence) > self.maximum_length:
            return None

        return PullbackStructureEvent(
            direction=second_leg.direction,
            second_leg=second_leg,
            anchor_candle=anchor_candle,
            structure_points=sequence,
        )

    def _higher_low_sequence(self, anchor_candle):
        anchor_low = Decimal(str(anchor_candle.low))

        lows = [
            point
            for point in self.history.lows()
            if point.candle.timestamp > anchor_candle.timestamp
        ]

        sequence: list[StructurePoint] = []
        previous_price = anchor_low

        for point in lows:
            # Breaking the original Anchor permanently invalidates
            # the entire setup.
            if point.price < anchor_low:
                return []

            if point.price > previous_price:
                sequence.append(
                    StructurePoint(
                        type="higher_low",
                        candle=point.candle,
                        price=point.price,
                    )
                )
                previous_price = point.price

                # An 8th consecutive HL invalidates only the
                # current compression sequence. Start fresh
                # from this structural point.
                if len(sequence) > self.maximum_length:
                    sequence = []
                    previous_price = point.price

            else:
                # Structural violation, but the Anchor is still intact.
                # Start a fresh sequence from this structural point.
                sequence = []
                previous_price = point.price

        return sequence

    def _lower_high_sequence(self, anchor_candle):
        anchor_high = Decimal(str(anchor_candle.high))

        highs = [
            point
            for point in self.history.highs()
            if point.candle.timestamp > anchor_candle.timestamp
        ]

        sequence: list[StructurePoint] = []
        previous_price = anchor_high

        for point in highs:
            # Breaking the original Anchor permanently invalidates
            # the entire setup.
            if point.price > anchor_high:
                return []

            if point.price < previous_price:
                sequence.append(
                    StructurePoint(
                        type="lower_high",
                        candle=point.candle,
                        price=point.price,
                    )
                )
                previous_price = point.price

                # An 8th consecutive LH invalidates only the
                # current compression sequence. Start fresh
                # from this structural point.
                if len(sequence) > self.maximum_length:
                    sequence = []
                    previous_price = point.price

            else:
                # Structural violation, but the Anchor is still intact.
                # Start a fresh sequence from this structural point.
                sequence = []
                previous_price = point.price
        
        return sequence

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\strategies\p_gap.py
================================================================================

from dataclasses import dataclass

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.spike_event import SpikeEvent


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PGapEvent:
    direction: str
    pre_breakout_candle: Candle
    breakout_candle: Candle
    follow_through_candle: Candle


class PGapDetector:
    """
    Detects a strict non-overlapping pressure gap (P-Gap).

    Source-derived rule:
        After a breakout, subsequent follow-through price action must
        not pull back far enough for its shadow to overlap the shadow
        of the preceding pre-breakout candle.

    Current implementation assumption:
        The first candle immediately after the Spike is treated as the
        follow-through candidate.

    The Spike itself is supplied as a SpikeEvent, so this detector does
    not independently determine whether the breakout candle is a Spike.
    """

    def detect(
        self,
        spike_event: SpikeEvent,
        pre_breakout_candle: Candle,
        follow_through_candle: Candle,
    ) -> PGapEvent | None:

        if spike_event.direction == "buy":
            if follow_through_candle.low > pre_breakout_candle.high:
                return PGapEvent(
                    direction="buy",
                    pre_breakout_candle=pre_breakout_candle,
                    breakout_candle=spike_event.breakout_candle,
                    follow_through_candle=follow_through_candle,
                )

        if spike_event.direction == "sell":
            if follow_through_candle.high < pre_breakout_candle.low:
                return PGapEvent(
                    direction="sell",
                    pre_breakout_candle=pre_breakout_candle,
                    breakout_candle=spike_event.breakout_candle,
                    follow_through_candle=follow_through_candle,
                )

        return None

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\strategies\second_leg.py
================================================================================

from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.p_gap import PGapEvent


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SecondLegEvent:
    direction: str
    p_gap: PGapEvent
    pullback_candle: Candle
    reference_candle: Candle


class SecondLegDetector:
    """
    Detects the source-defined second-leg/pullback condition.

    Bullish setup:
        The corrective candle reaches the low of the previous candle.

    Bearish setup:
        The corrective candle reaches the high of the previous candle.

    This detector deliberately does not determine:
        - how many pullback candles exist
        - the 3-7 lower-high / higher-low sequence
        - Signal Bar
        - Key Bar
        - entry

    Those are separate parts of the strategy.
    """

    def detect(
        self,
        p_gap_event: PGapEvent,
        reference_candle: Candle,
        pullback_candle: Candle,
    ) -> SecondLegEvent | None:

        if p_gap_event.direction == "buy":
            if Decimal(str(pullback_candle.low)) <= Decimal(
                str(reference_candle.low)
            ):
                return SecondLegEvent(
                    direction="buy",
                    p_gap=p_gap_event,
                    pullback_candle=pullback_candle,
                    reference_candle=reference_candle,
                )

        if p_gap_event.direction == "sell":
            if Decimal(str(pullback_candle.high)) >= Decimal(
                str(reference_candle.high)
            ):
                return SecondLegEvent(
                    direction="sell",
                    p_gap=p_gap_event,
                    pullback_candle=pullback_candle,
                    reference_candle=reference_candle,
                )

        return None

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\strategies\signal_bar.py
================================================================================

from dataclasses import dataclass

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.pullback_structure import PullbackStructureEvent


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SignalBarEvent:
    direction: str
    pullback_structure: PullbackStructureEvent
    signal_candle: Candle


class SignalBarDetector:
    """
    Detects the SP2L Signal Bar after the corrective structure sequence.

    Bullish:
        The signal candle breaks the previous corrective candle's high.

    Bearish:
        The signal candle breaks the previous corrective candle's low.

    The Signal Bar itself does NOT trigger entry.
    The following candle must qualify as the Key Bar.
    """

    def detect(
        self,
        pullback_structure: PullbackStructureEvent,
        previous_candle: Candle,
        signal_candle: Candle,
    ) -> SignalBarEvent | None:

        if signal_candle.timestamp <= previous_candle.timestamp:
            return None

        if pullback_structure.direction == "buy":
            if self._bullish_break_of_previous_high(
                previous_candle,
                signal_candle,
            ):
                return SignalBarEvent(
                    direction="buy",
                    pullback_structure=pullback_structure,
                    signal_candle=signal_candle,
                )

        elif pullback_structure.direction == "sell":
            if self._bearish_break_of_previous_low(
                previous_candle,
                signal_candle,
            ):
                return SignalBarEvent(
                    direction="sell",
                    pullback_structure=pullback_structure,
                    signal_candle=signal_candle,
                )

        return None

    @staticmethod
    def _bullish_break_of_previous_high(
        previous_candle: Candle,
        signal_candle: Candle,
    ) -> bool:
        return signal_candle.high > previous_candle.high

    @staticmethod
    def _bearish_break_of_previous_low(
        previous_candle: Candle,
        signal_candle: Candle,
    ) -> bool:
        return signal_candle.low < previous_candle.low

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\strategies\sp2l.py
================================================================================

from collections import deque

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.key_bar import KeyBarDetector
from strategies.p_gap import PGapDetector
from strategies.pullback_structure import PullbackStructureDetector
from strategies.second_leg import SecondLegDetector
from strategies.signal_bar import SignalBarDetector
from strategies.sp2l_state import SP2LSetup, SP2LState
from strategies.spike import SpikeDetector
from strategies.spike_event import SpikeEvent
from strategies.swing_processor import SwingProcessor


class SP2LStateMachine:
    """
    Causal SP2L strategy state machine.

    Processes one completed candle at a time.

    State flow:

        IDLE
          ↓
        SPIKE_VALIDATED
          ↓
        PULLBACK
          ↓
        COMPRESSION_VALID
          ↓
        WAITING_FOR_KEY_BAR
          ↓
        TRIGGER

    The machine only recognizes setups.
    Trade execution is handled separately.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        spike_detector: SpikeDetector | None = None,
        p_gap_detector: PGapDetector | None = None,
        second_leg_detector: SecondLegDetector | None = None,
        pullback_structure_detector: PullbackStructureDetector | None = None,
        signal_bar_detector: SignalBarDetector | None = None,
        key_bar_detector: KeyBarDetector | None = None,
        swing_processor: SwingProcessor | None = None,
    ):
        self.spike_detector = spike_detector or SpikeDetector()
        self.p_gap_detector = p_gap_detector or PGapDetector()
        self.second_leg_detector = (
            second_leg_detector or SecondLegDetector()
        )
        self.signal_bar_detector = (
            signal_bar_detector or SignalBarDetector()
        )
        self.key_bar_detector = key_bar_detector or KeyBarDetector()

        self.swing_processor = swing_processor or SwingProcessor()

        if pullback_structure_detector is None:
            pullback_structure_detector = PullbackStructureDetector(
                history=self.swing_processor.history
            )

        self.pullback_structure_detector = pullback_structure_detector

        self._spike_lookback = self.spike_detector.config.lookback

        self._candles = deque(
            maxlen=self._spike_lookback + 2
        )

        self.state = SP2LState.IDLE

        self._previous_candle: Candle | None = None
        self._pre_breakout_candle: Candle | None = None

        self._pending_spike: SpikeEvent | None = None
        self._p_gap = None
        self._second_leg = None
        self._pullback_structure = None
        self._signal_bar = None

    @property
    def pending_spike(self) -> SpikeEvent | None:
        return self._pending_spike

    @property
    def p_gap(self):
        return self._p_gap

    @property
    def second_leg(self):
        return self._second_leg

    @property
    def pullback_structure(self):
        return self._pullback_structure

    @property
    def signal_bar(self):
        return self._signal_bar

    def process(self, candle: Candle) -> SP2LSetup | None:
        """
        Process one completed candle.

        Returns:
            SP2LSetup when a Key Bar confirms the setup.
            None otherwise.
        """
        self._candles.append(candle)

        self.swing_processor.process(candle)

        if self.state == SP2LState.IDLE:
            return self._process_idle(candle)

        if self.state == SP2LState.SPIKE_VALIDATED:
            return self._process_spike_validated(candle)

        if self.state == SP2LState.PULLBACK:
            return self._process_pullback(candle)

        if self.state == SP2LState.COMPRESSION_VALID:
            return self._process_compression(candle)

        if self.state == SP2LState.WAITING_FOR_KEY_BAR:
            return self._process_key_bar(candle)

        self._reset()
        self._previous_candle = candle

        return None

    def _process_idle(
        self,
        candle: Candle,
    ) -> SP2LSetup | None:
        required_candles = self._spike_lookback + 1

        if len(self._candles) < required_candles:
            self._previous_candle = candle
            return None

        spike = self.spike_detector.detect_event(
            list(self._candles)
        )

        if spike is not None:
            self._pending_spike = spike
            self._pre_breakout_candle = self._candles[-2]
            self.state = SP2LState.SPIKE_VALIDATED

        self._previous_candle = candle

        return None

    def _process_spike_validated(
        self,
        candle: Candle,
    ) -> SP2LSetup | None:
        spike = self._pending_spike
        pre_breakout_candle = self._pre_breakout_candle

        if spike is None or pre_breakout_candle is None:
            self._reset()
            self._previous_candle = candle
            return None

        if candle.timestamp <= spike.breakout_candle.timestamp:
            self._previous_candle = candle
            return None

        p_gap = self.p_gap_detector.detect(
            spike_event=spike,
            pre_breakout_candle=pre_breakout_candle,
            follow_through_candle=candle,
        )

        if p_gap is None:
            self._reset()
            self._previous_candle = candle
            return None

        self._p_gap = p_gap
        self._previous_candle = candle
        self.state = SP2LState.PULLBACK

        return None

    def _process_pullback(
        self,
        candle: Candle,
    ) -> SP2LSetup | None:
        if self._p_gap is None:
            self._reset()
            self._previous_candle = candle
            return None

        if self._second_leg is None:
            reference_candle = self._previous_candle

            if reference_candle is None:
                self._previous_candle = candle
                return None

            second_leg = self.second_leg_detector.detect(
                p_gap_event=self._p_gap,
                reference_candle=reference_candle,
                pullback_candle=candle,
            )

            if second_leg is None:
                self._previous_candle = candle
                return None

            self._second_leg = second_leg
            self._previous_candle = candle

            return None

        if self._anchor_invalidated(candle):
            self._reset()
            self._previous_candle = candle
            return None

        pullback_structure = (
            self.pullback_structure_detector.detect(
                self._second_leg
            )
        )

        if pullback_structure is not None:
            self._pullback_structure = pullback_structure
            self.state = SP2LState.COMPRESSION_VALID

        self._previous_candle = candle

        return None

    def _process_compression(
        self,
        candle: Candle,
    ) -> SP2LSetup | None:
        if self._second_leg is None:
            self._reset()
            self._previous_candle = candle
            return None

        if self._pullback_structure is None:
            self.state = SP2LState.PULLBACK
            self._previous_candle = candle
            return None

        if self._anchor_invalidated(candle):
            self._reset()
            self._previous_candle = candle
            return None

        # Re-check the corrective structure before looking
        # for a Signal Bar. A structural violation can invalidate
        # the current compression sequence without invalidating
        # the original Anchor.
        pullback_structure = (
            self.pullback_structure_detector.detect(
                self._second_leg
            )
        )

        if pullback_structure is None:
            self._pullback_structure = None
            self.state = SP2LState.PULLBACK
            self._previous_candle = candle
            return None

        self._pullback_structure = pullback_structure

        previous_candle = self._previous_candle

        if previous_candle is None:
            self._previous_candle = candle
            return None

        signal_bar = self.signal_bar_detector.detect(
            pullback_structure=self._pullback_structure,
            previous_candle=previous_candle,
            signal_candle=candle,
        )

        if signal_bar is not None:
            self._signal_bar = signal_bar
            self.state = SP2LState.WAITING_FOR_KEY_BAR

        self._previous_candle = candle

        return None

    def _process_key_bar(
        self,
        candle: Candle,
    ) -> SP2LSetup | None:
        if self._signal_bar is None:
            self._reset()
            self._previous_candle = candle
            return None

        key_bar = self.key_bar_detector.detect(
            signal_bar=self._signal_bar,
            key_candle=candle,
        )

        if key_bar is None:
            self._reset()
            self._previous_candle = candle
            return None

        if (
            self._pending_spike is None
            or self._p_gap is None
            or self._pullback_structure is None
        ):
            self._reset()
            self._previous_candle = candle
            return None

        setup = SP2LSetup(
            direction=key_bar.direction,
            spike=self._pending_spike,
            p_gap=self._p_gap,
            pullback_structure=self._pullback_structure,
            signal_bar=self._signal_bar,
            key_bar=key_bar,
        )

        self._reset()
        self._previous_candle = candle

        return setup

    def _anchor_invalidated(
        self,
        candle: Candle,
    ) -> bool:
        if self._second_leg is None:
            return False

        anchor = self._second_leg.pullback_candle

        if self._second_leg.direction == "buy":
            return candle.high > anchor.high

        if self._second_leg.direction == "sell":
            return candle.low < anchor.low

        return True

    def _reset(self) -> None:
        self.state = SP2LState.IDLE

        self._pre_breakout_candle = None
        self._pending_spike = None
        self._p_gap = None
        self._second_leg = None
        self._pullback_structure = None
        self._signal_bar = None

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\strategies\sp2l_state.py
================================================================================

from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum

from strategies.key_bar import KeyBarEvent
from strategies.p_gap import PGapEvent
from strategies.pullback_structure import PullbackStructureEvent
from strategies.signal_bar import SignalBarEvent
from strategies.spike_event import SpikeEvent

class SP2LState(Enum):
    IDLE = "idle"
    SPIKE_VALIDATED = "spike_validated"
    PULLBACK = "pullback"
    COMPRESSION_VALID = "compression_valid"
    WAITING_FOR_KEY_BAR = "waiting_for_key_bar"
    TRIGGER = "trigger"

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SP2LSetup:
    """
    Complete evidence chain for a confirmed SP2L setup.
    """
    direction: str
    spike: SpikeEvent
    p_gap: PGapEvent
    pullback_structure: PullbackStructureEvent
    signal_bar: SignalBarEvent
    key_bar: KeyBarEvent


================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\strategies\sp2l_strategy.py
================================================================================

from backtest.signal import Signal
from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.base import Strategy
from strategies.sp2l import SP2LStateMachine


class SP2LStrategy(Strategy):
    def __init__(self, state_machine: SP2LStateMachine):
        self.state_machine = state_machine

    def on_candle(self, candle: Candle) -> Signal:
        setup = self.state_machine.process(candle)

        if setup is None:
            return Signal.NONE

        if setup.direction == "buy":
            return Signal.BUY

        if setup.direction == "sell":
            return Signal.SELL

        return Signal.NONE

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\strategies\spike.py
================================================================================

from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal

from strategies.spike_event import SpikeEvent
from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.spike_measurements import SpikeMeasurements

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SpikeDetectorConfig:
    """
    Working assumptions for Spike detection.

    These values are NOT claimed to be Poursamadi's exact parameters.
    They are baseline values that can later be tested and optimized.
    """

    lookback: int = 5

    minimum_range_ratio: Decimal = Decimal("1.8")
    minimum_body_ratio: Decimal = Decimal("1.5")
    minimum_body_ratio_of_range: Decimal = Decimal("0.65")
    maximum_closing_wick_ratio: Decimal = Decimal("0.20")



class SpikeDetector:
    def __init__(
        self,
        config: SpikeDetectorConfig | None = None,
    ):
        self.config = config or SpikeDetectorConfig()

        if self.config.lookback <= 0:
            raise ValueError("lookback must be greater than zero")

    def detect(
        self,
        candles: list[Candle] | tuple[Candle, ...],
    ) -> SpikeMeasurements | None:
        """
        Evaluate the latest completed candle against its recent context.

        The detector is timeframe-agnostic. The caller decides whether
        the supplied candles are M5, M15, or another timeframe.
        """

        required_candles = self.config.lookback + 1

        if len(candles) < required_candles:
            return None

        candidate = candles[-1]
        previous = candles[-required_candles:-1]

        average_range = self._average_range(previous)
        average_body = self._average_body(previous)

        return self._measure_candidate(
            candidate=candidate,
            average_range=average_range,
            average_body=average_body,
            previous=previous,
        )

    def _measure_candidate(
        self,
        candidate: Candle,
        average_range: Decimal,
        average_body: Decimal,
        previous: list[Candle] | tuple[Candle, ...],
    ) -> SpikeMeasurements:
        open_price = Decimal(str(candidate.open))
        high = Decimal(str(candidate.high))
        low = Decimal(str(candidate.low))
        close = Decimal(str(candidate.close))

        total_range = high - low
        body = abs(close - open_price)

        upper_wick = high - max(open_price, close)
        lower_wick = min(open_price, close) - low

        if total_range == 0:
            body_ratio_of_range = Decimal("0")
            closing_wick_ratio = Decimal("0")
        else:
            body_ratio_of_range = body / total_range

            if close >= open_price:
                closing_wick = upper_wick
            else:
                closing_wick = lower_wick

            closing_wick_ratio = closing_wick / total_range

        if average_range == 0:
            range_ratio = Decimal("0")
        else:
            range_ratio = total_range / average_range

        if average_body == 0:
            body_ratio = Decimal("0")
        else:
            body_ratio = body / average_body

        previous_high = max(
            Decimal(str(candle.high))
            for candle in previous
        )

        previous_low = min(
            Decimal(str(candle.low))
            for candle in previous
        )

        high_breakout_distance = high - previous_high
        low_breakout_distance = previous_low - low

        if close > open_price:
            direction = "buy"
            close_breakout_distance = close - previous_high
        elif close < open_price:
            direction = "sell"
            close_breakout_distance = previous_low - close
        else:
            direction = "none"
            close_breakout_distance = Decimal("0")
        breakout_distance = close_breakout_distance
        is_spike = (
            direction != "none"
            and range_ratio >= self.config.minimum_range_ratio
            and body_ratio >= self.config.minimum_body_ratio
            and body_ratio_of_range
            >= self.config.minimum_body_ratio_of_range
            and closing_wick_ratio
            <= self.config.maximum_closing_wick_ratio
            and close_breakout_distance > 0
        )

        return SpikeMeasurements(
            direction=direction,
            total_range=total_range,
            body=body,
            upper_wick=upper_wick,
            lower_wick=lower_wick,
            body_ratio_of_range=body_ratio_of_range,
            closing_wick_ratio=closing_wick_ratio,
            average_previous_range=average_range,
            average_previous_body=average_body,
            range_ratio=range_ratio,
            body_ratio=body_ratio,
            previous_high=previous_high,
            previous_low=previous_low,
            high_breakout_distance=high_breakout_distance,
            low_breakout_distance=low_breakout_distance,
            close_breakout_distance=close_breakout_distance,
            is_spike=is_spike,
            breakout_distance=breakout_distance,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _average_range(
        candles: list[Candle] | tuple[Candle, ...],
    ) -> Decimal:
        ranges = [
            Decimal(str(candle.high)) - Decimal(str(candle.low))
            for candle in candles
        ]

        return sum(ranges, Decimal("0")) / Decimal(len(ranges))

    @staticmethod
    def _average_body(
        candles: list[Candle] | tuple[Candle, ...],
    ) -> Decimal:
        bodies = [
            abs(
                Decimal(str(candle.close))
                - Decimal(str(candle.open))
            )
            for candle in candles
        ]

        return sum(bodies, Decimal("0")) / Decimal(len(bodies))

    def detect_event(self, candles: list[Candle]) -> "SpikeEvent | None":

        measurements = self.detect(candles)

        if measurements is None or not measurements.is_spike:
            return None

        return SpikeEvent(
            breakout_candle=candles[-1],
            direction=measurements.direction,
            measurements=measurements,
        )

    lower_wick: Decimal

    body_ratio: Decimal

FILE: trade-bot-gpt\strategies\structure.py

from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.swing import SwingPoint


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StructurePoint:
    type: str
    candle: Candle
    price: Decimal


def detect_higher_low(
    previous: SwingPoint,
    current: SwingPoint,
) -> StructurePoint | None:
    if (
        previous.type != "low"
        or current.type != "low"
    ):
        return None

    if current.price > previous.price:
        return StructurePoint(
            type="higher_low",
            candle=current.candle,
            price=current.price,
        )

    return None


def detect_lower_high(
    previous: SwingPoint,
    current: SwingPoint,
) -> StructurePoint | None:
    if (
        previous.type != "high"
        or current.type != "high"
    ):
        return None

    if current.price < previous.price:
        return StructurePoint(
            type="lower_high",
            candle=current.candle,
            price=current.price,
        )

    return None


================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\strategies\structure_sequence.py
================================================================================

from strategies.structure import (
    StructurePoint,
    detect_higher_low,
    detect_lower_high,
)
from strategies.swing_history import SwingHistory


class StructureSequenceDetector:
    def __init__(self, history: SwingHistory):
        self.history = history

    def higher_low_sequence(
        self,
        minimum_length: int = 1,
    ) -> list[StructurePoint]:
        if minimum_length <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "minimum_length must be greater than zero"
            )

        lows = self.history.lows()

        if len(lows) < minimum_length + 1:
            return []

        sequence: list[StructurePoint] = []

        for previous, current in zip(lows[:-1], lows[1:]):
            structure = detect_higher_low(
                previous,
                current,
            )

            if structure is None:
                sequence = []
                continue

            sequence.append(structure)

        if len(sequence) < minimum_length:
            return []

        return sequence[-minimum_length:]

    def lower_high_sequence(
        self,
        minimum_length: int = 1,
    ) -> list[StructurePoint]:
        if minimum_length <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "minimum_length must be greater than zero"
            )

        highs = self.history.highs()

        if len(highs) < minimum_length + 1:
            return []

        sequence: list[StructurePoint] = []

        for previous, current in zip(highs[:-1], highs[1:]):
            structure = detect_lower_high(
                previous,
                current,
            )

            if structure is None:
                sequence = []
                continue

            sequence.append(structure)

        if len(sequence) < minimum_length:
            return []

        return sequence[-minimum_length:]

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\strategies\swing.py
================================================================================

from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal

from models.candle import Candle


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SwingPoint:
    type: str
    candle: Candle
    price: Decimal


class SwingDetector:
    def detect(self, candles: list[Candle]) -> SwingPoint | None:
        if len(candles) < 3:
            return None

        previous = candles[-3]
        candidate = candles[-2]
        current = candles[-1]
        if (
            candidate.high == previous.high
            or candidate.high == current.high
            or candidate.low == previous.low
            or candidate.low == current.low
            ):
                return None

        is_swing_high = (
            candidate.high > previous.high
            and candidate.high > current.high
        )

        is_swing_low = (
            candidate.low < previous.low
            and candidate.low < current.low
        )

        if is_swing_high:
            return SwingPoint(
                type="high",
                candle=candidate,
                price=Decimal(str(candidate.high)),
            )

        if is_swing_low:
            return SwingPoint(
                type="low",
                candle=candidate,
                price=Decimal(str(candidate.low)),
            )

        return None

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\strategies\swing_history.py
================================================================================

from strategies.swing import SwingPoint


class SwingHistory:
    def __init__(self):
        self._points: list[SwingPoint] = []

    def add(self, point: SwingPoint) -> None:
        self._points.append(point)

    @property
    def points(self) -> list[SwingPoint]:
        return list(self._points)

    def highs(self) -> list[SwingPoint]:
        return [
            point
            for point in self._points
            if point.type == "high"
        ]

    def lows(self) -> list[SwingPoint]:
        return [
            point
            for point in self._points
            if point.type == "low"
        ]

    def latest_high(self) -> SwingPoint | None:
        highs = self.highs()

        if not highs:
            return None

        return highs[-1]

    def latest_low(self) -> SwingPoint | None:
        lows = self.lows()

        if not lows:
            return None

        return lows[-1]

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\strategies\swing_processor.py
================================================================================

from collections import deque

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.swing import SwingDetector, SwingPoint
from strategies.swing_history import SwingHistory


class SwingProcessor:
    def __init__(
        self,
        detector: SwingDetector | None = None,
        history: SwingHistory | None = None,
    ):
        self.detector = detector or SwingDetector()
        self.history = history or SwingHistory()

        self._candles: deque[Candle] = deque(maxlen=3)

    def process(self, candle: Candle) -> SwingPoint | None:
        self._candles.append(candle)

        if len(self._candles) < 3:
            return None

        point = self.detector.detect(list(self._candles))

        if point is None:
            return None

        self.history.add(point)
        return point

from backtest.signal import Signal
from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.base import Strategy


class TestStrategy(Strategy):
    def __init__(self):
        self.candle_count = 0

    def on_candle(self, candle: Candle) -> Signal:
        self.candle_count += 1

        if self.candle_count % 100 == 0:
            return Signal.BUY

        return Signal.NONE

import unittest
from decimal import Decimal

from backtest.account import Account


class AccountTest(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_starting_balance(self):
        account = Account("10000.00")

        self.assertEqual(
            account.balance,
            Decimal("10000.00")
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            account.equity,
            Decimal("10000.00")
        )

    def test_realized_pnl_changes_balance(self):
        account = Account("10000.00")

        account.apply_pnl(Decimal("-25.50"))

        self.assertEqual(
            account.balance,
            Decimal("9974.50")
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            account.equity,
            Decimal("9974.50")
        )

    def test_floating_pnl_changes_equity_not_balance(self):
        account = Account("10000.00")

        account.update_floating_pnl(
            Decimal("-80.00")
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            account.balance,
            Decimal("10000.00")
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            account.floating_pnl,
            Decimal("-80.00")
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            account.equity,
            Decimal("9920.00")
        )

    def test_realized_and_floating_pnl_together(self):
        account = Account("10000.00")

        account.apply_pnl(Decimal("-30.00"))
        account.update_floating_pnl(
            Decimal("-80.00")
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            account.balance,
            Decimal("9970.00")
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            account.equity,
            Decimal("9890.00")
        )

import unittest
from datetime import datetime

from backtest.engine import BacktestEngine
from backtest.equity import EquityPoint
from backtest.signal import Signal
from config.instrument import XAUUSD
from config.trading import TradingConfig
from models.candle import Candle


class OneShotBuyStrategy:
    def __init__(self):
        self.called = 0

    def on_candle(self, candle):
        self.called += 1

        if self.called == 1:
            return Signal.BUY

        return Signal.NONE


class TestEquityCurve(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_equity_curve_records_points(self):
        candles = [
            Candle(
                timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
                open=2000.0,
                high=2000.0,
                low=2000.0,
                close=2000.0,
            ),
            Candle(
                timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
                open=2001.0,
                high=2001.0,
                low=2001.0,
                close=2001.0,
            ),
        ]

        config = TradingConfig()

        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=candles,
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=config,
            stop_loss_distance=1000,
            take_profit_distance=1000,
        )

        result = engine.run(OneShotBuyStrategy())

        self.assertEqual(
            len(result.equity_curve),
            len(candles),
        )

        self.assertIsInstance(
            result.equity_curve[0],
            EquityPoint,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            result.equity_curve[0].timestamp,
            candles[0].timestamp,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            result.equity_curve[1].timestamp,
            candles[1].timestamp,
        )


================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_execution_timing.py
================================================================================

import unittest
from datetime import datetime
from decimal import Decimal

from backtest.account import Account
from backtest.engine import BacktestEngine
from backtest.result import BacktestResult
from backtest.risk import RiskManager
from backtest.signal import Signal
from backtest.trade import Trade
from config.instrument import XAUUSD
from config.trading import TRADING_CONFIG
from models.candle import Candle


class OneShotBuyStrategy:
    """
    Generates exactly one BUY signal after the first candle closes.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.called = 0

    def on_candle(self, candle):
        self.called += 1

        if self.called == 1:
            return Signal.BUY

        return Signal.NONE

class OneShotSellStrategy:
    """
    Generates exactly one SELL signal after the first candle closes.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.called = 0

    def on_candle(self, candle):
        self.called += 1

        if self.called == 1:
            return Signal.SELL

        return Signal.NONE

class MultiSignalStrategy:
    def __init__(self, signals):
        self.signals = signals
        self.index = 0

    def on_candle(self, candle):
        signal = self.signals[self.index]
        self.index += 1
        return signal

class ExecutionTimingTest(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_signal_executes_on_next_candle_open(self):
        """
        Verify:

        1. Signal is generated from candle N.
        2. Entry happens on candle N+1.
        3. BUY entry uses ASK.
        4. BUY exit uses BID.
        5. SL is evaluated using BID.
        6. Commission is deducted.
        7. P&L is calculated using the XAUUSD contract size.
        """

        candles = [
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
                100.0,
                101.0,
                99.0,
                100.5,
            ),
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
                105.0,
                105.5,
                104.5,
                105.2,
            ),
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 10),
                105.0,
                105.5,
                103.5,
                104.0,
            ),
        ]

        strategy = OneShotBuyStrategy()

        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=candles,
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=TRADING_CONFIG,
            stop_loss_distance=1000,
            take_profit_distance=1000,
        )

        result = engine.run(strategy)

        self.assertEqual(len(result.completed_trades), 1)

        trade = result.completed_trades[0]

        # Signal timing
        self.assertEqual(
            trade.signal_time,
            datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
        )

        # Entry happens on the next candle.
        self.assertEqual(
            trade.entry_time,
            datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
        )

        # Candle open = 105.000
        # Spread = 0.008
        # Half spread = 0.004
        # BUY executes at ASK = 105.004
        self.assertEqual(
            trade.entry_ticks,
            105004,
        )

        # SL = 105.004 - 1.000 = 104.004
        self.assertEqual(
            trade.stop_loss_ticks,
            104004,
        )

        # TP = 105.004 + 1.000 = 106.004
        self.assertEqual(
            trade.take_profit_ticks,
            106004,
        )

        # Candle 3 reference low = 103.500
        #
        # BID low = 103.500 - 0.004
        #         = 103.496
        #
        # Therefore the BUY SL is hit.
        self.assertEqual(
            trade.exit_time,
            datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 10),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            trade.exit_ticks,
            104004,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            trade.exit_reason,
            "stop_loss",
        )

        # Entry: 105.004
        # Exit:  104.004
        #
        # Price movement = -1.000
        # = -1000 ticks
        self.assertEqual(
            trade.profit_ticks,
            -1000,
        )

        # 0.01 lot is used by the current configuration.
        self.assertEqual(
            trade.lot_size,
            Decimal("0.01"),
        )

        # 0.01 lot = 1 oz.
        #
        # $1.00 price movement × 1 oz = $1 loss.
        self.assertEqual(
            trade.gross_profit,
            Decimal("-1.000"),
        )

        # Commission:
        # $8 × 0.01 = $0.08
        self.assertEqual(
            trade.commission,
            Decimal("0.08"),
        )

        # Net:
        # -$1.00 - $0.08 = -$1.08
        self.assertEqual(
            trade.net_profit,
            Decimal("-1.080"),
        )

        # Starting balance = $10,000
        self.assertEqual(
            engine.account.balance,
            Decimal("9998.920"),
        )

        self.assertGreater(
            trade.entry_time,
            trade.signal_time,
        )

    def test_buy_floating_pnl_uses_bid(self):
        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=[],
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=TRADING_CONFIG,
            stop_loss_distance=1000,
            take_profit_distance=1000,
        )

        engine.current_trade = Trade(
            direction="BUY",
            lot_size=Decimal("0.01"),
            signal_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
            entry_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
            entry_ticks=2000004,
            stop_loss_ticks=1999004,
            take_profit_ticks=2001004,
        )

        candle = Candle(
            timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 10),
            open=2000.0,
            high=2000.0,
            low=2000.0,
            close=1999.0,
        )

        pnl = engine.calculate_floating_pnl(candle)

        self.assertEqual(
            pnl,
            Decimal("-1.008")
        )

    def test_sell_floating_pnl_uses_ask(self):
        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=[],
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=TRADING_CONFIG,
            stop_loss_distance=1000,
            take_profit_distance=1000,
        )

        engine.current_trade = Trade(

            lot_size=Decimal("0.01"),
            signal_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
            entry_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
            entry_ticks=2000004,
            stop_loss_ticks=2001004,
            take_profit_ticks=1999004,
        )

        candle = Candle(
            timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 10),
            open=2000.0,
            high=2000.0,
            low=2000.0,
            close=2001.0,
        )

        pnl = engine.calculate_floating_pnl(candle)

        self.assertEqual(
            pnl,
            Decimal("-1.000")
        )

    def test_engine_updates_floating_pnl(self):
        candles = [
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
                2000.0,
                2000.5,
                1999.5,
                2000.0,
            ),
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
                2000.0,
                2000.5,
                1999.5,
                2000.0,
            ),
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 10),
                1999.0,
                1999.5,
                1998.5,
                1999.0,
            ),
        ]

        strategy = OneShotBuyStrategy()

        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=candles,
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=TRADING_CONFIG,
            stop_loss_distance=10000,
            take_profit_distance=10000,
        )

        engine.run(strategy)

        # The trade should still be open because the
        # SL/TP are intentionally very far away.
        self.assertIsNotNone(
            engine.current_trade
        )

        # Entry:
        # 2000.004
        #
        # Current close:
        # 1999.000
        #
        # BUY is valued at BID:
        # 1998.996
        #
        # Difference:
        # -1.008
        #
        # 0.01 lot = 1 oz
        # Floating P&L = -$1.008

        self.assertEqual(
            engine.account.floating_pnl,
            Decimal("-1.008"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            engine.account.equity,
            Decimal("9998.992"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            engine.risk_manager.floating_pnl,
            Decimal("-1.008"),
        )

    def test_open_trade_is_preserved_at_end_of_data(self):
        candles = [
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
                2000.0,
                2000.5,
                1999.5,
                2000.0,
            ),
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
                2000.0,
                2000.5,
                1999.5,
                2000.0,
            ),
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 10),
                1999.0,
                1999.5,
                1998.5,
                1999.0,
            ),
        ]

        strategy = OneShotBuyStrategy()

        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=candles,
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=TRADING_CONFIG,
            stop_loss_distance=10000,
            take_profit_distance=10000,
        )

        result = engine.run(strategy)

        # The trade never hit SL or TP.
        self.assertEqual(
            len(result.completed_trades),
            0,
        )

        # It must therefore be preserved as an
        # open trade at the end of the dataset.
        self.assertIsNotNone(
            engine.open_trade_at_end,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            engine.open_trade_at_end.direction,
            "BUY",
        )

        # Final candle close = 1999.000
        # BUY is valued at BID = 1998.996
        # Entry = 2000.004
        # Difference = -1.008
        self.assertEqual(
            engine.final_floating_pnl,
            Decimal("-1.008"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            engine.account.floating_pnl,
            Decimal("-1.008"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            engine.account.equity,
            Decimal("9998.992"),
        )

    def test_open_sell_trade_is_preserved_at_end_of_data(self):
        candles = [

                2000.0,

        # The trade never hit SL or TP.
        self.assertEqual(
            len(result.completed_trades),
            0,
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(
            engine.open_trade_at_end,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            engine.open_trade_at_end.direction,
            "SELL",

        # SELL entry:
        # 2000.000 - 0.004 = 1999.996
        #
        # Final close:
        # 2001.000
        #
        # SELL is valued at ASK:
        # 2001.004
        #
        # P&L:

        # = -1.008

        # 0.01 lot = 1 oz
        self.assertEqual(

            Decimal("-1.008"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            engine.account.floating_pnl,
            Decimal("-1.008"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            engine.account.equity,
            Decimal("9998.992"),
        )

    def test_daily_loss_limit_blocks_new_trade(self):
        candles = [
            # 1. Signal BUY
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
                2000.0, 2000.5, 1999.5, 2000.0
            ),

            # 2. BUY executes here and immediately hits SL.
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
                2000.0, 2000.5, 1899.0, 1999.0
            ),

            # 3. Signal BUY for a second trade.
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 10),
                2000.0, 2000.5, 1999.5, 2000.0
            ),

            # 4. Second trade would execute here.
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 15),
                2000.0, 2000.5, 1999.5, 2000.0
            ),
        ]

        strategy = MultiSignalStrategy([
            Signal.BUY,
            Signal.NONE,
            Signal.BUY,
            Signal.NONE,
        ])

        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=candles,
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=TRADING_CONFIG,
            stop_loss_distance=100000,
            take_profit_distance=10000,
        )

        # Make the first loss large enough to hit the $100 daily limit.
        engine.current_trade = None

        result = engine.run(strategy)

        self.assertEqual(len(result.completed_trades), 1)

        first_trade = result.completed_trades[0]

        self.assertEqual(first_trade.direction, "BUY")
        self.assertEqual(first_trade.exit_reason, "stop_loss")

        self.assertLessEqual(
            first_trade.net_profit,
            Decimal("-100.00"),
        )

        # The second trade must NOT have been opened.
        self.assertIsNone(engine.open_trade_at_end)

        # The first trade's loss should remain realized.
        self.assertEqual(
            engine.account.floating_pnl,
            Decimal("0"),
        )
    def test_daily_loss_below_limit_allows_new_trade(self):
        candles = [
            # Signal trade #1.
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
                2000.0, 2000.5, 1999.5, 2000.0
            ),

            # Trade #1 executes and hits its SL.
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
                2000.0, 2000.5, 1949.0, 1999.0
            ),

            # Signal trade #2.
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 10),
                2000.0, 2000.5, 1999.5, 2000.0
            ),

            # Trade #2 executes here and remains open.
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 15),
                2000.0, 2000.5, 1999.5, 2000.0
            ),
        ]

        strategy = MultiSignalStrategy([
            Signal.BUY,
            Signal.NONE,
            Signal.BUY,
            Signal.NONE,
        ])

        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=candles,
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=TRADING_CONFIG,
            stop_loss_distance=50000,
            take_profit_distance=100000,
        )

        result = engine.run(strategy)

        # Trade #1 hit its SL.
        self.assertEqual(len(result.completed_trades), 1)

        first_trade = result.completed_trades[0]

        self.assertEqual(first_trade.direction, "BUY")
        self.assertEqual(first_trade.exit_reason, "stop_loss")

        # Gross loss is approximately $50, plus $0.08 commission.
        self.assertEqual(
            first_trade.net_profit,
            Decimal("-50.080"),
        )

        # Trade #2 was allowed and is still open at the end.
        self.assertIsNotNone(engine.open_trade_at_end)
        self.assertEqual(
            engine.open_trade_at_end.direction,
            "BUY",
        )

        # Its entry should be on the fourth candle.
        self.assertEqual(
            engine.open_trade_at_end.entry_time,
            datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 15),
        )

    def test_daily_loss_limit_includes_realized_and_floating_pnl(self):
        candles = [
            # Signal trade #1.
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
                2000.0, 2000.5, 1999.5, 2000.0
            ),

            # Trade #1 executes and hits SL for roughly $60 loss.
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
                2000.0, 2000.5, 1939.0, 1999.0
            ),

            # Signal trade #2.
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 10),
                2000.0, 2000.5, 1999.5, 2000.0
            ),

            # Trade #2 executes.
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 15),
                2000.0, 2000.5, 1999.5, 2000.0
            ),

            # Trade #2 moves against us by about $41.
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 20),
                1959.0, 1960.0, 1958.0, 1959.0
            ),

            # Signal trade #3.
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 25),
                2000.0, 2000.5, 1999.5, 2000.0
            ),

            # Trade #3 would execute here if risk allowed it.
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 30),
                2000.0, 2000.5, 1999.5, 2000.0
            ),
        ]

        strategy = MultiSignalStrategy([
            Signal.BUY,
            Signal.NONE,
            Signal.BUY,
            Signal.NONE,
            Signal.NONE,
            Signal.BUY,
            Signal.NONE,
        ])

        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=candles,
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=TRADING_CONFIG,
            stop_loss_distance=60000,
            take_profit_distance=100000,
        )

        result = engine.run(strategy)

        # Trade #1 should have closed at its SL.
        self.assertEqual(len(result.completed_trades), 1)

        first_trade = result.completed_trades[0]

        self.assertEqual(first_trade.exit_reason, "stop_loss")

        # Trade #1 loses $60 + $0.08 commission.
        self.assertEqual(
            first_trade.net_profit,
            Decimal("-60.080"),
        )

        # Trade #2 was allowed because realized loss was still
        # below the $100 daily limit.
        self.assertIsNotNone(engine.open_trade_at_end)

        # Trade #3 must NOT have been opened because:
        #
        # realized ≈ -$60.08
        # floating ≈ -$41
        # total < -$100
        #
        # The existing trade (#2) should remain open.
        self.assertEqual(
            engine.open_trade_at_end.entry_time,
            datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 15),
        )

    def test_weekly_loss_limit_resets_on_new_week(self):
        risk_manager = RiskManager(TRADING_CONFIG)

        # Monday, January 5, 2026.
        monday = datetime(2026, 1, 5, 10, 0)

        # Create a completed trade with a $400 loss.
        trade = Trade(
            direction="BUY",
            lot_size=Decimal("0.01"),
            signal_time=monday,
            entry_time=monday,
            entry_ticks=2000000,
            stop_loss_ticks=1900000,
            take_profit_ticks=2100000,
            exit_time=monday,
            exit_ticks=1900000,
            gross_profit=Decimal("-400.00"),
            commission=Decimal("0.00"),
            net_profit=Decimal("-400.00"),
            profit_ticks=-100000,
            exit_reason="stop_loss",
        )

        risk_manager.update(trade)

        # The weekly limit has been reached.
        self.assertTrue(
            risk_manager.weekly_loss_reached()
        )

        # Move to the following Monday.
        next_monday = datetime(2026, 1, 12, 10, 0)

        # A new week should reset the weekly realized P&L.
        self.assertTrue(
            risk_manager.can_open_trade(next_monday)
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            risk_manager.weekly_realized_pnl,
            Decimal("0"),
        )


    def test_sell_signal_executes_on_next_candle_open(self):
        candles = [
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
                2000.0,
                2000.5,
                1999.5,
                2000.0,
            ),
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
                2000.0,
                2000.5,
                1989.0,
                1999.0,
            ),
        ]

        strategy = OneShotSellStrategy()

        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=candles,
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=TRADING_CONFIG,
            stop_loss_distance=1000,
            take_profit_distance=1000,
        )

        result = engine.run(strategy)

        self.assertEqual(len(result.completed_trades), 1)

        trade = result.completed_trades[0]

        # SELL executes at BID:
        # 2000.000 - 0.004 = 1999.996
        self.assertEqual(
            trade.entry_ticks,
            1999996,
        )

        # SELL TP is 1000 ticks lower.
        self.assertEqual(
            trade.take_profit_ticks,
            1998996,
        )

        # The next candle's ASK reaches the TP.
        self.assertEqual(
            trade.exit_reason,
            "take_profit",
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            trade.exit_time,
            datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            trade.exit_ticks,
            1998996,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            trade.profit_ticks,
            1000,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            trade.gross_profit,
            Decimal("1.000"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            trade.commission,
            Decimal("0.080"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            trade.net_profit,
            Decimal("0.920"),
        )

    def test_buy_same_candle_sl_and_tp_hits_sl_first(self):
        candles = [
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
                2000.0,
                2000.5,
                1999.5,
                2000.0,
            ),
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
                2000.0,
                2001.5,
                1998.5,
                2000.5,
            ),
        ]

        strategy = OneShotBuyStrategy()

        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=candles,
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=TRADING_CONFIG,
            stop_loss_distance=1000,
            take_profit_distance=1000,
        )

        result = engine.run(strategy)

        self.assertEqual(len(result.completed_trades), 1)

        trade = result.completed_trades[0]

        # BUY entry is at ASK:
        # 2000.000 + 0.004 = 2000.004
        self.assertEqual(
            trade.entry_ticks,
            2000004,
        )

        # Both SL and TP are reached during the same candle.
        # The backtester conservatively assumes SL happened first.
        self.assertEqual(
            trade.exit_reason,
            "stop_loss",
        )

        # SL:
        # 2000.004 - 1.000 = 1999.004
        self.assertEqual(
            trade.stop_loss_ticks,
            1999004,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            trade.exit_ticks,
            1999004,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            trade.profit_ticks,
            -1000,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            trade.gross_profit,
            Decimal("-1.000"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            trade.commission,
            Decimal("0.080"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            trade.net_profit,
            Decimal("-1.080"),
        )

    def test_engine_returns_backtest_result(self):
        candles = [
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
                2000.0,
                2000.5,
                1999.5,
                2000.0,
            ),
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
                2000.0,
                2000.5,
                1989.0,
                1999.0,
            ),
        ]

        strategy = OneShotBuyStrategy()

        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=candles,
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=TRADING_CONFIG,
            stop_loss_distance=1000,
            take_profit_distance=1000,
        )

        result = engine.run(strategy)

        self.assertIsInstance(
            result,
            BacktestResult,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            result.starting_balance,
            Decimal("10000.00"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            len(result.completed_trades),
            1,
        )

        self.assertIsNone(
            result.open_trade,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            result.final_floating_pnl,
            Decimal("0"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            result.ending_balance,
            Decimal("9998.920"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            result.net_profit,
            Decimal("-1.080"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            result.equity,
            Decimal("9998.920"),
        )

    def test_strategy_cannot_see_future_candles(self):
        """
        Verify that the strategy only receives the current candle
        and cannot access future candle data through the engine.
        """

        class InspectingStrategy:
            def __init__(self):
                self.received_candles = []

            def on_candle(self, candle):
                self.received_candles.append(candle)
                return Signal.NONE

        candles = [
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
                100.0,
                101.0,
                99.0,
                100.5,
            ),
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
                200.0,
                201.0,
                199.0,
                200.5,
            ),
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 10),
                300.0,
                301.0,
                299.0,
                300.5,
            ),
        ]

        strategy = InspectingStrategy()

        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=candles,
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=TRADING_CONFIG,
            stop_loss_distance=1000,
            take_profit_distance=1000,
        )

        engine.run(strategy)

        self.assertEqual(
            len(strategy.received_candles),
            3,
        )

        # First strategy call must receive only candle 1.
        self.assertEqual(
            strategy.received_candles[0],
            candles[0],
        )

        # Second call must receive candle 2.
        self.assertEqual(
            strategy.received_candles[1],
            candles[1],
        )

        # Third call must receive candle 3.
        self.assertEqual(
            strategy.received_candles[2],
            candles[2],
        )

    def test_loss_limit_does_not_force_close_existing_position(self):
        """
        Verify that reaching the daily loss limit prevents new trades
        but does not forcibly close an already-open position.
        """

        class BuyAndHoldStrategy:
            def __init__(self):
                self.called = 0

            def on_candle(self, candle):
                self.called += 1

                if self.called == 1:
                    return Signal.BUY

                return Signal.NONE

        candles = [
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 5, 10, 0),
                2000.0,
                2000.5,
                1999.5,
                2000.0,
            ),
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 5, 10, 5),
                2000.0,
                2000.5,
                1899.0,
                1900.0,
            ),
            Candle(
                datetime(2026, 1, 5, 10, 10),
                1900.0,
                1900.5,
                1899.5,
                1900.0,
            ),
        ]

        strategy = BuyAndHoldStrategy()

        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=candles,
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=TRADING_CONFIG,
            stop_loss_distance=200000,
            take_profit_distance=200000,
        )

        result = engine.run(strategy)

        # The position must remain open.
        self.assertIsNotNone(result.open_trade)

        # It must NOT have been force-closed.
        self.assertEqual(
            len(result.completed_trades),
            0,
        )

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_key_bar.py
