import unittest
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.key_bar import KeyBarDetector
from strategies.p_gap import PGapEvent
from strategies.pullback_structure import PullbackStructureEvent
from strategies.second_leg import SecondLegEvent
from strategies.signal_bar import SignalBarEvent
from strategies.structure import StructurePoint


class TestKeyBarDetector(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.start = datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0)

    def make_candle(
        self,
        offset: int,
        open_price: str,
        high: str,
        low: str,
        close: str,
    ) -> Candle:
        return Candle(
            timestamp=self.start + timedelta(minutes=5 * offset),
            open=float(open_price),
            high=float(high),
            low=float(low),
            close=float(close),
        )

    def make_signal_bar(
        self,
        direction: str,
        signal_offset: int = 10,
        signal_open: str = "104",
        signal_high: str = "108",
        signal_low: str = "101",
        signal_close: str = "106",
    ) -> SignalBarEvent:

        pullback = self.make_candle(
            3,
            "105",
            "110",
            "100",
            "102",
        )

        breakout = self.make_candle(
            1,
            "100",
            "108",
            "100",
            "107",
        )

        pre_breakout = self.make_candle(
            0,
            "99",
            "100",
            "97",
            "99",
        )

        follow_through = self.make_candle(
            2,
            "107",
            "110",
            "105",
            "109",
        )

        p_gap = PGapEvent(
            direction=direction,
            pre_breakout_candle=pre_breakout,
            breakout_candle=breakout,
            follow_through_candle=follow_through,
        )

        second_leg = SecondLegEvent(
            direction=direction,
            p_gap=p_gap,
            pullback_candle=pullback,
            reference_candle=pre_breakout,
        )

        structure_candle = self.make_candle(
            9,
            "105",
            "105",
            "105",
            "105",
        )

        structure_point = StructurePoint(
            type=(
                "lower_high"
                if direction == "buy"
                else "higher_low"
            ),
            candle=structure_candle,
            price=105,
        )

        pullback_structure = PullbackStructureEvent(
            direction=direction,
            second_leg=second_leg,
            anchor_candle=pullback,
            structure_points=[structure_point],
        )

        signal_candle = self.make_candle(
            signal_offset,
            signal_open,
            signal_high,
            signal_low,
            signal_close,
        )

        return SignalBarEvent(
            direction=direction,
            pullback_structure=pullback_structure,
            signal_candle=signal_candle,
        )

    def test_detects_bullish_key_bar(self):
        signal = self.make_signal_bar(
            direction="buy",
            signal_offset=10,
            signal_high="108",
            signal_low="101",
            signal_close="106",
        )

        key = self.make_candle(
            11,
            "106",
            "112",
            "105",
            "110",
        )

        detector = KeyBarDetector()

        result = detector.detect(signal, key)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(result.direction, "buy")
        self.assertEqual(result.key_candle, key)
        self.assertIs(result.signal_bar, signal)

    def test_detects_bearish_key_bar(self):
        signal = self.make_signal_bar(
            direction="sell",
            signal_offset=10,
            signal_high="108",
            signal_low="101",
            signal_close="103",
        )

        key = self.make_candle(
            11,
            "103",
            "104",
            "96",
            "98",
        )

        detector = KeyBarDetector()

        result = detector.detect(signal, key)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(result.direction, "sell")
        self.assertEqual(result.key_candle, key)
        self.assertIs(result.signal_bar, signal)

    def test_bullish_key_bar_requires_close_above_signal_high(self):
        signal = self.make_signal_bar(
            direction="buy",
            signal_offset=10,
            signal_high="108",
            signal_low="101",
            signal_close="106",
        )

        # Breaks the high but closes back below it.
        key = self.make_candle(
            11,
            "106",
            "112",
            "105",
            "107",
        )

        detector = KeyBarDetector()

        result = detector.detect(signal, key)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_bearish_key_bar_requires_close_below_signal_low(self):
        signal = self.make_signal_bar(
            direction="sell",
            signal_offset=10,
            signal_high="108",
            signal_low="101",
            signal_close="103",
        )

        # Breaks the low but closes back above it.
        key = self.make_candle(
            11,
            "103",
            "104",
            "96",
            "102",
        )

        detector = KeyBarDetector()

        result = detector.detect(signal, key)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_candle_inside_signal_range_is_rejected(self):
        signal = self.make_signal_bar(
            direction="buy",
            signal_offset=10,
            signal_high="108",
            signal_low="101",
        )

        key = self.make_candle(
            11,
            "105",
            "107",
            "102",
            "106",
        )

        detector = KeyBarDetector()

        result = detector.detect(signal, key)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_candle_two_bars_after_signal_is_rejected(self):
        signal = self.make_signal_bar(
            direction="buy",
            signal_offset=10,
            signal_high="108",
            signal_low="101",
        )

        key = self.make_candle(
            12,
            "106",
            "112",
            "105",
            "110",
        )

        detector = KeyBarDetector()

        result = detector.detect(signal, key)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_candle_before_signal_is_rejected(self):
        signal = self.make_signal_bar(
            direction="buy",
            signal_offset=10,
            signal_high="108",
            signal_low="101",
        )

        key = self.make_candle(
            9,
            "106",
            "112",
            "105",
            "110",
        )

        detector = KeyBarDetector()

        result = detector.detect(signal, key)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_gap_after_signal_rejects_key_bar(self):
        signal = self.make_signal_bar(
            direction="buy",
            signal_offset=10,
            signal_high="108",
            signal_low="101",
        )

        key = self.make_candle(
            12,
            "106",
            "112",
            "105",
            "110",
        )

        detector = KeyBarDetector()

        result = detector.detect(signal, key)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_key_bar_event_preserves_signal_bar(self):
        signal = self.make_signal_bar(
            direction="buy",
            signal_offset=10,
            signal_high="108",
            signal_low="101",
        )

        key = self.make_candle(
            11,
            "106",
            "112",
            "105",
            "110",
        )

        detector = KeyBarDetector()

        result = detector.detect(signal, key)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertIs(result.signal_bar, signal)

FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_metrics.py

import unittest
from decimal import Decimal
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from backtest.metrics import MetricsCalculator
from backtest.result import BacktestResult
from backtest.trade import Trade
from backtest.metrics import BacktestMetrics
from backtest.equity import EquityPoint

class BacktestMetricsTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def make_trade(self, net_profit):
        return Trade(
            direction="buy",
            lot_size=Decimal("0.01"),
            signal_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
            entry_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
            entry_ticks=2_000_000,
            stop_loss_ticks=1_999_000,
            take_profit_ticks=2_001_000,
            exit_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 10),
            exit_ticks=2_000_500,
            gross_profit=net_profit,
            commission=Decimal("0"),
            net_profit=net_profit,
            profit_ticks=500,
            exit_reason="take_profit",
        )
    
    def test_metrics_stores_values(self):
        metrics = BacktestMetrics(
            starting_balance=Decimal("10000.00"),
            ending_balance=Decimal("10500.00"),
            net_profit=Decimal("500.00"),
            return_percent=Decimal("5.00"),
            total_trades=10,
            winning_trades=6,
            losing_trades=4,
            win_rate_percent=Decimal("60.00"),
            average_trade=Decimal("50.00"),
            largest_win=Decimal("120.00"),
            largest_loss=Decimal("-80.00"),
            profit_factor=Decimal("1.50"),
            max_drawdown=Decimal("300.00"),
            max_drawdown_percent=Decimal("2.91"),
            max_consecutive_losses=3,
            total_time_in_market=timedelta(0),
            exposure_percent=Decimal("0.00"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.starting_balance,
            Decimal("10000.00"),
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.ending_balance,
            Decimal("10500.00"),
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.net_profit,
            Decimal("500.00"),
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.return_percent,
            Decimal("5.00"),
        )
        self.assertEqual(metrics.total_trades, 10)
        self.assertEqual(metrics.winning_trades, 6)
        self.assertEqual(metrics.losing_trades, 4)
        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.win_rate_percent,
            Decimal("60.00"),
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.average_trade,
            Decimal("50.00"),
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.largest_win,
            Decimal("120.00"),
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.largest_loss,
            Decimal("-80.00"),
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.profit_factor,
            Decimal("1.50"),
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.max_drawdown,
            Decimal("300.00"),
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.max_drawdown_percent,
            Decimal("2.91"),
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.max_consecutive_losses,
            3,
        )

    def test_calculates_trade_metrics(self):
        trades = [
            Trade(
                direction="BUY",
                lot_size=Decimal("0.01"),
                signal_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
                entry_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
                entry_ticks=2000000,
                stop_loss_ticks=1999000,
                take_profit_ticks=2001000,
                exit_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 10),
                exit_ticks=2001000,
                gross_profit=Decimal("1.000"),
                commission=Decimal("0.080"),
                net_profit=Decimal("0.920"),
                profit_ticks=1000,
                exit_reason="take_profit",
            ),
            Trade(
                direction="SELL",
                lot_size=Decimal("0.01"),
                signal_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 11, 0),
                entry_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 11, 5),
                entry_ticks=2000000,
                stop_loss_ticks=2001000,
                take_profit_ticks=1999000,
                exit_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 11, 10),
                exit_ticks=2000500,
                gross_profit=Decimal("-0.500"),
                commission=Decimal("0.080"),
                net_profit=Decimal("-0.580"),
                profit_ticks=-500,
                exit_reason="stop_loss",
            ),
            Trade(
                direction="BUY",
                lot_size=Decimal("0.01"),
                signal_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0),
                entry_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 5),
                entry_ticks=2000000,
                stop_loss_ticks=1999000,
                take_profit_ticks=2001000,
                exit_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 10),
                exit_ticks=2000200,
                gross_profit=Decimal("0.200"),
                commission=Decimal("0.080"),
                net_profit=Decimal("0.120"),
                profit_ticks=200,
                exit_reason="take_profit",
            ),
        ]

        result = BacktestResult(
            starting_balance=Decimal("10000.00"),
            ending_balance=Decimal("10000.46"),
            completed_trades=trades,
            open_trade=None,
            final_floating_pnl=Decimal("0"),
            equity_curve=[],
        )

        metrics = MetricsCalculator().calculate(result)

        self.assertEqual(metrics.total_trades, 3)
        self.assertEqual(metrics.winning_trades, 2)
        self.assertEqual(metrics.losing_trades, 1)

        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.win_rate_percent,
            Decimal("66.67"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.net_profit,
            Decimal("0.46"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.return_percent,
            Decimal("0.00"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.average_trade,
            Decimal("0.153"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.largest_win,
            Decimal("0.920"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.largest_loss,
            Decimal("-0.580"),
        )
    def test_calculates_metrics_with_no_trades(self):
        result = BacktestResult(
            starting_balance=Decimal("10000.00"),
            ending_balance=Decimal("10000.00"),
            completed_trades=[],
            open_trade=None,
            final_floating_pnl=Decimal("0"),
            equity_curve=[],
        )

        metrics = MetricsCalculator().calculate(result)

        self.assertEqual(metrics.total_trades, 0)
        self.assertEqual(metrics.winning_trades, 0)
        self.assertEqual(metrics.losing_trades, 0)
        self.assertEqual(metrics.win_rate_percent, Decimal("0.00"))
        self.assertEqual(metrics.average_trade, Decimal("0.000"))
        self.assertEqual(metrics.largest_win, Decimal("0"))
        self.assertEqual(metrics.largest_loss, Decimal("0"))
        self.assertEqual(metrics.profit_factor,Decimal("0.00"))

    def test_calculates_profit_factor(self):
        trades = [
            Trade(
                direction="BUY",
                lot_size=Decimal("0.01"),
                signal_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
                entry_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
                entry_ticks=2000000,
                stop_loss_ticks=1999000,
                take_profit_ticks=2001000,
                exit_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 10),
                exit_ticks=2001000,
                gross_profit=Decimal("2.000"),
                commission=Decimal("0.080"),
                net_profit=Decimal("1.920"),
                profit_ticks=1000,
                exit_reason="take_profit",
            ),
            Trade(
                direction="BUY",
                lot_size=Decimal("0.01"),
                signal_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 11, 0),
                entry_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 11, 5),
                entry_ticks=2000000,
                stop_loss_ticks=1999000,
                take_profit_ticks=2001000,
                exit_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 11, 10),
                exit_ticks=1999500,
                gross_profit=Decimal("-1.000"),
                commission=Decimal("0.080"),
                net_profit=Decimal("-1.080"),
                profit_ticks=-500,
                exit_reason="stop_loss",
            ),
        ]

        result = BacktestResult(
            starting_balance=Decimal("10000.00"),
            ending_balance=Decimal("10000.84"),
            completed_trades=trades,
            open_trade=None,
            final_floating_pnl=Decimal("0"),
            equity_curve=[],
        )

        metrics = MetricsCalculator().calculate(result)

        self.assertEqual(
            metrics.profit_factor,
            Decimal("1.78"),
        )

    def test_calculates_max_drawdown(self):
        equity_curve = [
            EquityPoint(

                equity=Decimal("9800"),

    def test_calculates_max_consecutive_losses(self):

        )

    def test_calculates_exposure(self):

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_pending_order.py
================================================================================

from datetime import datetime
from decimal import Decimal
import unittest

from backtest.engine import BacktestEngine
from backtest.signal import Signal
from config.instrument import XAUUSD
from config.trading import TRADING_CONFIG
from models.candle import Candle


class LastCandleBuyStrategy:
    def on_candle(self, candle):
        return Signal.BUY


class TestPendingOrder(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_pending_order_at_end_of_data_is_not_executed(self):
        candles = [
            Candle(
                timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0),
                open=2000.0,
                high=2001.0,
                low=1999.0,
                close=2000.5,
            ),
        ]

        strategy = LastCandleBuyStrategy()

        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=candles,
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=TRADING_CONFIG,
            stop_loss_distance=1000,
            take_profit_distance=1000,
        )

        result = engine.run(strategy)

        self.assertEqual(result.completed_trades, [])
        self.assertIsNone(result.open_trade)
        self.assertEqual(result.final_floating_pnl, Decimal("0"))
        self.assertEqual(result.ending_balance, Decimal("10000.00"))


================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_position_sizing.py
================================================================================

import unittest
from decimal import Decimal
from datetime import datetime
from backtest.position_sizing import PositionSizer
from config.trading import TRADING_CONFIG


class PositionSizerTest(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.sizer = PositionSizer(TRADING_CONFIG)

    def test_valid_minimum_lot(self):
        self.sizer.validate_lot_size(Decimal("0.01"))

    def test_valid_lot_sizes(self):
        valid_sizes = [
            Decimal("0.01"),
            Decimal("0.02"),
            Decimal("0.10"),
            Decimal("0.50"),
            Decimal("1.00"),
        ]

        for lot_size in valid_sizes:
            with self.subTest(lot_size=lot_size):
                self.sizer.validate_lot_size(lot_size)

    def test_below_minimum_is_rejected(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            self.sizer.validate_lot_size(
                Decimal("0.005")
            )

    def test_invalid_step_is_rejected(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            self.sizer.validate_lot_size(
                Decimal("0.015")
            )

    def test_normalize_valid_size(self):
        result = self.sizer.normalize_lot_size(
            Decimal("0.10")
        )

        self.assertEqual(result, Decimal("0.10"))

    def test_normalize_below_minimum(self):
        result = self.sizer.normalize_lot_size(
            Decimal("0.005")
        )

        self.assertEqual(result, Decimal("0.01"))

    def test_engine_rejects_invalid_default_lot(self):
        from dataclasses import replace

        from backtest.engine import BacktestEngine
        from backtest.signal import Signal
        from models.candle import Candle
        from config.instrument import XAUUSD

        config = replace(
            TRADING_CONFIG,
            default_lot=0.015,
        )

        candles = [
            Candle(
                timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
                open=2000.0,
                high=2001.0,
                low=1999.0,
                close=2000.5,
            ),
            Candle(
                timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
                open=2000.5,
                high=2001.5,
                low=2000.0,
                close=2001.0,
            ),
        ]

        class BuyStrategy:
            def on_candle(self, candle):
                if candle.timestamp == candles[0].timestamp:
                    return Signal.BUY
                return Signal.NONE

        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=candles,
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=config,
            stop_loss_distance=100,
            take_profit_distance=100,
        )

        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            engine.run(BuyStrategy())



================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_pullback_structure.py
================================================================================

import unittest
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.p_gap import PGapEvent
from strategies.pullback_structure import (
    PullbackStructureDetector,
    PullbackStructureEvent,
)
from strategies.second_leg import SecondLegEvent
from strategies.swing import SwingPoint
from strategies.swing_history import SwingHistory


class TestPullbackStructureDetector(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.start = datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0)

    def make_candle(
        self,
        offset: int,
        high: str,
        low: str,
    ) -> Candle:
        return Candle(
            timestamp=self.start + timedelta(minutes=5 * offset),
            open=float(low),
            high=float(high),
            low=float(low),
            close=float(high),
        )

    def make_second_leg(
        self,
        direction: str,
        pullback_offset: int = 3,
        pullback_high: str = "110",
        pullback_low: str = "100",
    ) -> SecondLegEvent:

        pre_breakout = self.make_candle(0, "100", "98")
        breakout = self.make_candle(1, "108", "100")
        follow_through = self.make_candle(2, "110", "105")

        pullback = self.make_candle(
            pullback_offset,
            pullback_high,
            pullback_low,
        )

        reference = self.make_candle(4, "105", "100")

        p_gap = PGapEvent(
            direction=direction,
            pre_breakout_candle=pre_breakout,
            breakout_candle=breakout,
            follow_through_candle=follow_through,
        )

        return SecondLegEvent(
            direction=direction,
            p_gap=p_gap,
            pullback_candle=pullback,
            reference_candle=reference,
        )

    def add_high(
        self,
        history: SwingHistory,
        offset: int,
        price: str,
    ) -> None:
        candle = self.make_candle(offset, price, price)

        history.add(
            SwingPoint(
                type="high",
                candle=candle,
                price=Decimal(price),
            )
        )

    def add_low(
        self,
        history: SwingHistory,
        offset: int,
        price: str,
    ) -> None:
        candle = self.make_candle(offset, price, price)

        history.add(
            SwingPoint(
                type="low",
                candle=candle,
                price=Decimal(price),
            )
        )

    def test_anchor_is_not_counted_as_structure(self):
        history = SwingHistory()

        second_leg = self.make_second_leg(
            "buy",
            pullback_offset=3,
            pullback_high="110",
            pullback_low="100",
        )

        # This is the anchor itself.
        self.add_high(history, 3, "110")

        detector = PullbackStructureDetector(history)

        result = detector.detect(second_leg)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_first_lower_high_after_anchor_is_lh_one(self):
        history = SwingHistory()

        second_leg = self.make_second_leg(
            "buy",
            pullback_offset=3,
            pullback_high="110",
            pullback_low="100",
        )

        self.add_high(history, 3, "110")
        self.add_high(history, 4, "108")

        detector = PullbackStructureDetector(
            history,
            minimum_length=1,
        )

        result = detector.detect(second_leg)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(len(result.structure_points), 1)
        self.assertEqual(
            result.structure_points[0].type,
            "lower_high",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            result.structure_points[0].price,
            Decimal("108"),
        )

    def test_detects_three_lower_highs_after_anchor(self):
        history = SwingHistory()

        second_leg = self.make_second_leg(
            "buy",
            pullback_offset=3,
            pullback_high="110",
            pullback_low="100",
        )

        self.add_high(history, 3, "110")
        self.add_high(history, 4, "108")
        self.add_high(history, 5, "106")
        self.add_high(history, 6, "104")

        detector = PullbackStructureDetector(history)

        result = detector.detect(second_leg)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertIsInstance(result, PullbackStructureEvent)
        self.assertEqual(result.direction, "buy")
        self.assertEqual(len(result.structure_points), 3)

        self.assertEqual(
            [point.price for point in result.structure_points],
            [
                Decimal("108"),
                Decimal("106"),
                Decimal("104"),
            ],
        )

    def test_detects_three_higher_lows_after_anchor(self):
        history = SwingHistory()

        second_leg = self.make_second_leg(
            "sell",
            pullback_offset=3,
            pullback_high="100",
            pullback_low="90",
        )

        self.add_low(history, 3, "90")
        self.add_low(history, 4, "92")
        self.add_low(history, 5, "94")
        self.add_low(history, 6, "96")

        detector = PullbackStructureDetector(history)

        result = detector.detect(second_leg)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(result.direction, "sell")
        self.assertEqual(len(result.structure_points), 3)

        self.assertEqual(
            [point.price for point in result.structure_points],
            [
                Decimal("92"),
                Decimal("94"),
                Decimal("96"),
            ],
        )

    def test_ignores_structure_before_anchor(self):
        history = SwingHistory()

        second_leg = self.make_second_leg(
            "buy",
            pullback_offset=5,
            pullback_high="110",
            pullback_low="100",
        )

        # These are valid lower highs, but they happened before
        # the Second Leg anchor and must not count.
        self.add_high(history, 0, "120")
        self.add_high(history, 1, "118")
        self.add_high(history, 2, "116")

        # Anchor.
        self.add_high(history, 5, "110")

        # Only two valid structures after the anchor.
        self.add_high(history, 6, "108")
        self.add_high(history, 7, "106")

        detector = PullbackStructureDetector(history)

        result = detector.detect(second_leg)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_sequence_resets_when_lower_high_condition_breaks(self):
        history = SwingHistory()

        second_leg = self.make_second_leg(
            "buy",
            pullback_offset=3,
            pullback_high="110",
            pullback_low="100",
        )

        self.add_high(history, 3, "110")
        self.add_high(history, 4, "108")
        self.add_high(history, 5, "106")

        # 108 is higher than the previous 106, breaking the sequence.
        self.add_high(history, 6, "108")

        # New sequence starts here.
        self.add_high(history, 7, "107")
        self.add_high(history, 8, "105")
        self.add_high(history, 9, "103")

        detector = PullbackStructureDetector(history)

        result = detector.detect(second_leg)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(len(result.structure_points), 3)

        self.assertEqual(
            [point.price for point in result.structure_points],
            [
                Decimal("107"),
                Decimal("105"),
                Decimal("103"),
            ],
        )

    def test_rejects_eighth_consecutive_lower_high(self):
        history = SwingHistory()

        second_leg = self.make_second_leg(
            "buy",
            pullback_offset=3,
            pullback_high="120",
            pullback_low="100",
        )

        # Anchor.
        self.add_high(history, 3, "120")

        # Eight consecutive LHs.
        prices = [
            "119",
            "118",
            "117",
            "116",
            "115",
            "114",
            "113",
            "112",
        ]

        for offset, price in enumerate(prices, start=4):
            self.add_high(history, offset, price)

        detector = PullbackStructureDetector(history)

        result = detector.detect(second_leg)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_rejects_eighth_consecutive_higher_low(self):
        history = SwingHistory()

        second_leg = self.make_second_leg(
            "sell",
            pullback_offset=3,
            pullback_high="100",
            pullback_low="80",
        )

        # Anchor.
        self.add_low(history, 3, "80")

        # Eight consecutive HLs.
        prices = [
            "81",
            "82",
            "83",
            "84",
            "85",
            "86",
            "87",
            "88",
        ]

        for offset, price in enumerate(prices, start=4):
            self.add_low(history, offset, price)

        detector = PullbackStructureDetector(history)

        result = detector.detect(second_leg)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_rejects_bullish_setup_without_three_lower_highs(self):
        history = SwingHistory()

        second_leg = self.make_second_leg(
            "buy",
            pullback_offset=3,
            pullback_high="110",
            pullback_low="100",
        )

        self.add_high(history, 3, "110")
        self.add_high(history, 4, "108")
        self.add_high(history, 5, "106")

        detector = PullbackStructureDetector(history)

        result = detector.detect(second_leg)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_rejects_bearish_setup_without_three_higher_lows(self):
        history = SwingHistory()

        second_leg = self.make_second_leg(
            "sell",
            pullback_offset=3,
            pullback_high="100",
            pullback_low="90",
        )

        self.add_low(history, 3, "90")
        self.add_low(history, 4, "92")
        self.add_low(history, 5, "94")

        detector = PullbackStructureDetector(history)

        result = detector.detect(second_leg)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_rejects_invalid_direction(self):
        history = SwingHistory()

        second_leg = self.make_second_leg(
            "invalid",
        )

        detector = PullbackStructureDetector(history)

        result = detector.detect(second_leg)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_invalid_minimum_length(self):
        history = SwingHistory()

        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            PullbackStructureDetector(
                history,
                minimum_length=0,
            )

    def test_invalid_sequence_range(self):
        history = SwingHistory()

        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            PullbackStructureDetector(
                history,
                minimum_length=7,
                maximum_length=3,
            )

    def test_starts_new_sequence_after_violation_without_breaking_anchor(self):
        history = SwingHistory()

        second_leg = self.make_second_leg(
            "buy",
            pullback_offset=3,
            pullback_high="100",
            pullback_low="90",
        )

        # Original Anchor.
        self.add_high(history, 3, "100")

        # First compression wave: LH1 -> LH7.
        prices = [
            "99",
            "98",
            "97",
            "96",
            "95",
            "94",
            "93",
        ]

        for offset, price in enumerate(prices, start=4):
            self.add_high(history, offset, price)

        # Violates the LH sequence, but does NOT break the Anchor.
        self.add_high(history, 11, "98")

        # New compression wave starts under the same Anchor.
        self.add_high(history, 12, "95")
        self.add_high(history, 13, "93")
        self.add_high(history, 14, "91")

        detector = PullbackStructureDetector(history)

        result = detector.detect(second_leg)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(len(result.structure_points), 3)

        self.assertEqual(
            [point.price for point in result.structure_points],
            [
                Decimal("95"),
                Decimal("93"),
                Decimal("91"),
            ],
        )
    def test_rejects_setup_when_violation_breaks_anchor(self):
        history = SwingHistory()

        second_leg = self.make_second_leg(
            "buy",
            pullback_offset=3,
            pullback_high="100",
            pullback_low="90",
        )

        # Original Anchor.
        self.add_high(history, 3, "100")

        # First compression wave.
        self.add_high(history, 4, "99")
        self.add_high(history, 5, "98")
        self.add_high(history, 6, "97")

        # Violates the sequence AND breaks the original Anchor.
        self.add_high(history, 7, "101")

        # These would otherwise form a new valid sequence.
        self.add_high(history, 8, "95")
        self.add_high(history, 9, "93")
        self.add_high(history, 10, "91")

        detector = PullbackStructureDetector(history)

        result = detector.detect(second_leg)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_starts_new_sequence_after_eighth_lower_high(self):
        history = SwingHistory()

        second_leg = self.make_second_leg(
            "buy",
            pullback_offset=3,
            pullback_high="120",
            pullback_low="100",
        )

        # Anchor.
        self.add_high(history, 3, "120")

        # First sequence: 8 consecutive LHs.
        first_sequence = [
            "119",
            "118",
            "117",
            "116",
            "115",
            "114",
            "113",
            "112",
        ]

        for offset, price in enumerate(first_sequence, start=4):
            self.add_high(history, offset, price)

        # This is a structural violation, but it does not
        # break the original Anchor.
        self.add_high(history, 12, "115")

        # Fresh sequence under the same Anchor.
        self.add_high(history, 13, "110")
        self.add_high(history, 14, "108")
        self.add_high(history, 15, "106")

        detector = PullbackStructureDetector(history)

        result = detector.detect(second_leg)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(len(result.structure_points), 3)

        self.assertEqual(
        [point.price for point in result.structure_points],
        [
            Decimal("110"),
            Decimal("108"),
            Decimal("106"),
        ],
    )

    def test_starts_new_sequence_after_eighth_higher_low(self):
        history = SwingHistory()

        second_leg = self.make_second_leg(
            "sell",
            pullback_offset=3,
            pullback_high="100",
            pullback_low="80",
        )

        # Anchor.
        self.add_low(history, 3, "80")

        # First sequence: 8 consecutive HLs.
        first_sequence = [
            "81",
            "82",
            "83",
            "84",
            "85",
            "86",
            "87",
            "88",
        ]

        for offset, price in enumerate(first_sequence, start=4):
            self.add_low(history, offset, price)

        # Structural violation, but the original Anchor remains intact.
        self.add_low(history, 12, "85")

        # Fresh sequence under the same Anchor.
        self.add_low(history, 13, "90")
        self.add_low(history, 14, "92")
        self.add_low(history, 15, "94")

        detector = PullbackStructureDetector(history)

        result = detector.detect(second_leg)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(len(result.structure_points), 3)

        self.assertEqual(
            [point.price for point in result.structure_points],
            [
                Decimal("90"),
                Decimal("92"),
                Decimal("94"),
            ],
        )

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_p_gap.py
================================================================================

import unittest
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.p_gap import PGapDetector, PGapEvent
from strategies.spike_event import SpikeEvent
from strategies.spike_measurements import SpikeMeasurements


class TestPGapDetector(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.detector = PGapDetector()
        self.start = datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0)

    def make_candle(
        self,
        offset: int,
        open_price: str,
        high: str,
        low: str,
        close: str,
    ) -> Candle:
        return Candle(
            timestamp=self.start + timedelta(minutes=5 * offset),
            open=float(open_price),
            high=float(high),
            low=float(low),
            close=float(close),
        )

    def make_spike_event(
        self,
        breakout_candle: Candle,
        direction: str,
    ) -> SpikeEvent:
        zero = Decimal("0")

        measurements = SpikeMeasurements(
            direction=direction,
            total_range=zero,
            body=zero,
            upper_wick=zero,
            lower_wick=zero,
            body_ratio_of_range=zero,
            closing_wick_ratio=zero,
            average_previous_range=zero,
            average_previous_body=zero,
            range_ratio=zero,
            body_ratio=zero,
            previous_high=zero,
            previous_low=zero,
            high_breakout_distance=zero,
            low_breakout_distance=zero,
            close_breakout_distance=zero,
            breakout_distance=zero,
            is_spike=True,
        )

        return SpikeEvent(
            breakout_candle=breakout_candle,
            direction=direction,
            measurements=measurements,
        )

    def test_detects_valid_bullish_p_gap(self):
        # Pre-breakout candle high = 100.0
        # Spike breaks upward.
        # Follow-through low = 101.0
        # 101.0 > 100.0 -> valid bullish P-Gap.

        pre_breakout = self.make_candle(
            0, "99.0", "100.0", "98.0", "99.5"
        )

        breakout = self.make_candle(
            1, "99.5", "105.0", "99.5", "104.0"
        )

        follow_through = self.make_candle(
            2, "104.0", "108.0", "101.0", "107.0"
        )

        spike_event = self.make_spike_event(
            breakout,
            "buy",
        )

        result = self.detector.detect(
            spike_event=spike_event,
            pre_breakout_candle=pre_breakout,
            follow_through_candle=follow_through,
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertIsInstance(result, PGapEvent)
        self.assertEqual(result.direction, "buy")
        self.assertEqual(result.pre_breakout_candle, pre_breakout)
        self.assertEqual(result.breakout_candle, breakout)
        self.assertEqual(result.follow_through_candle, follow_through)

    def test_rejects_bullish_shadow_overlap(self):
        # Pre-breakout high = 100.0
        # Follow-through low = 100.0
        # Exact contact means the shadows overlap -> invalid P-Gap.

        pre_breakout = self.make_candle(
            0, "99.0", "100.0", "98.0", "99.5"
        )

        breakout = self.make_candle(
            1, "99.5", "105.0", "99.5", "104.0"
        )

        follow_through = self.make_candle(
            2, "104.0", "108.0", "100.0", "107.0"
        )

        spike_event = self.make_spike_event(
            breakout,
            "buy",
        )

        result = self.detector.detect(
            spike_event=spike_event,
            pre_breakout_candle=pre_breakout,
            follow_through_candle=follow_through,
        )

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_detects_valid_bearish_p_gap(self):
        # Pre-breakout candle low = 98.0
        # Spike breaks downward.
        # Follow-through high = 97.0
        # 97.0 < 98.0 -> valid bearish P-Gap.

        pre_breakout = self.make_candle(
            0, "99.0", "100.0", "98.0", "99.0"
        )

        breakout = self.make_candle(
            1, "98.5", "98.5", "92.0", "93.0"
        )

        follow_through = self.make_candle(
            2, "93.0", "97.0", "90.0", "91.0"
        )

        spike_event = self.make_spike_event(
            breakout,
            "sell",
        )

        result = self.detector.detect(
            spike_event=spike_event,
            pre_breakout_candle=pre_breakout,
            follow_through_candle=follow_through,
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertIsInstance(result, PGapEvent)
        self.assertEqual(result.direction, "sell")
        self.assertEqual(result.pre_breakout_candle, pre_breakout)
        self.assertEqual(result.breakout_candle, breakout)
        self.assertEqual(result.follow_through_candle, follow_through)

    def test_rejects_bearish_shadow_overlap(self):
        # Pre-breakout low = 98.0
        # Follow-through high = 98.0
        # Exact contact means the shadows overlap -> invalid P-Gap.

        pre_breakout = self.make_candle(
            0, "99.0", "100.0", "98.0", "99.0"
        )

        breakout = self.make_candle(
            1, "98.5", "98.5", "92.0", "93.0"
        )

        follow_through = self.make_candle(
            2, "93.0", "98.0", "90.0", "91.0"
        )

        spike_event = self.make_spike_event(
            breakout,
            "sell",
        )

        result = self.detector.detect(
            spike_event=spike_event,
            pre_breakout_candle=pre_breakout,
            follow_through_candle=follow_through,
        )

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_rejects_direction_mismatch(self):
        # Bullish geometry with a bearish SpikeEvent should not
        # produce a bullish P-Gap.

        pre_breakout = self.make_candle(
            0, "99.0", "100.0", "98.0", "99.5"
        )

        breakout = self.make_candle(
            1, "99.5", "105.0", "99.5", "104.0"
        )

        follow_through = self.make_candle(
            2, "104.0", "108.0", "101.0", "107.0"
        )

        spike_event = self.make_spike_event(
            breakout,
            "sell",
        )

        result = self.detector.detect(
            spike_event=spike_event,
            pre_breakout_candle=pre_breakout,
            follow_through_candle=follow_through,
        )

        self.assertIsNone(result)

import unittest
from decimal import Decimal
from datetime import datetime
from backtest.result import BacktestResult
from backtest.trade import Trade


class BacktestResultTest(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_net_profit_uses_realized_balance(self):
        result = BacktestResult(
            starting_balance=Decimal("10000.00"),
            ending_balance=Decimal("10250.00"),
            completed_trades=[],
            open_trade=None,
            final_floating_pnl=Decimal("0"),
            equity_curve=[],
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            result.net_profit,
            Decimal("250.00"),
        )

    def test_equity_includes_floating_pnl(self):
        result = BacktestResult(
            starting_balance=Decimal("10000.00"),
            ending_balance=Decimal("9900.00"),
            completed_trades=[],
            open_trade=None,
            final_floating_pnl=Decimal("-50.00"),
            equity_curve=[],
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            result.net_profit,
            Decimal("-100.00"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            result.equity,
            Decimal("9850.00"),
        )

    def test_open_trade_is_preserved(self):
        trade = Trade(
            direction="BUY",
            lot_size=Decimal("0.01"),
            signal_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
            entry_time=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
            entry_ticks=2000000,
            stop_loss_ticks=1990000,
            take_profit_ticks=2010000,
        )

        result = BacktestResult(
            starting_balance=Decimal("10000.00"),
            ending_balance=Decimal("10000.00"),
            completed_trades=[],
            open_trade=trade,
            final_floating_pnl=Decimal("-10.00"),
            equity_curve=[],
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result.open_trade)
        self.assertEqual(
            result.open_trade.direction,
            "BUY",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            result.final_floating_pnl,
            Decimal("-10.00"),
        )



================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_risk.py
================================================================================

import unittest
from datetime import datetime
from decimal import Decimal

from backtest.risk import RiskManager
from backtest.trade import Trade
from config.trading import TRADING_CONFIG


def make_trade(
    exit_time: datetime,
    net_profit: str,
) -> Trade:
    return Trade(
        direction="BUY",
        lot_size=Decimal("0.01"),
        signal_time=exit_time,
        entry_time=exit_time,
        entry_ticks=100000,
        stop_loss_ticks=99000,
        take_profit_ticks=101000,
        exit_time=exit_time,
        exit_ticks=99000,
        gross_profit=Decimal(net_profit),
        commission=Decimal("0.00"),
        net_profit=Decimal(net_profit),
        profit_ticks=-1000,
        exit_reason="stop_loss",
    )


class RiskManagerTest(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_daily_loss_limit(self):
        risk = RiskManager(TRADING_CONFIG)

        timestamp = datetime(2026, 1, 5, 10, 0)

        trade = make_trade(
            timestamp,
            "-100.00",
        )

        risk.update(trade)

        self.assertTrue(
            risk.daily_loss_reached()
        )

        self.assertFalse(
            risk.can_open_trade(timestamp)
        )

    def test_daily_limit_resets_on_new_day(self):
        risk = RiskManager(TRADING_CONFIG)

        day_one = datetime(2026, 1, 5, 10, 0)
        day_two = datetime(2026, 1, 6, 10, 0)

        trade = make_trade(
            day_one,
            "-100.00",
        )

        risk.update(trade)

        self.assertFalse(
            risk.can_open_trade(day_one)
        )

        self.assertTrue(
            risk.can_open_trade(day_two)
        )

    def test_weekly_loss_limit(self):
        risk = RiskManager(TRADING_CONFIG)

        monday = datetime(2026, 1, 5, 10, 0)
        tuesday = datetime(2026, 1, 6, 10, 0)
        wednesday = datetime(2026, 1, 7, 10, 0)
        thursday = datetime(2026, 1, 8, 10, 0)

        risk.update(
            make_trade(
                monday,
                "-100.00",
            )
        )

        risk.update(
            make_trade(
                tuesday,
                "-100.00",
            )
        )

        risk.update(
            make_trade(
                wednesday,
                "-100.00",
            )
        )

        risk.update(
            make_trade(
                thursday,
                "-100.00",
            )
        )

        self.assertTrue(
            risk.weekly_loss_reached()
        )

        self.assertFalse(
            risk.can_open_trade(thursday)
        )

    def test_weekly_limit_resets_on_new_week(self):
        risk = RiskManager(TRADING_CONFIG)

        week_one = datetime(2026, 1, 9, 10, 0)
        week_two = datetime(2026, 1, 12, 10, 0)

        risk.update(
            make_trade(
                week_one,
                "-400.00",
            )
        )

        self.assertFalse(
            risk.can_open_trade(week_one)
        )

        self.assertTrue(
            risk.can_open_trade(week_two)
        )

    def test_daily_loss_includes_floating_pnl(self):
        risk = RiskManager(TRADING_CONFIG)

        timestamp = datetime(2026, 1, 5, 10, 0)

        risk.update(
            make_trade(timestamp, "-30.00")
        )

        risk.update_floating_pnl(
            Decimal("-70.00")
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            risk.current_daily_pnl(),
            Decimal("-100.00")
        )

        self.assertTrue(
            risk.daily_loss_reached()
        )

        self.assertFalse(
            risk.can_open_trade(timestamp)
        )

    def test_weekly_loss_includes_floating_pnl(self):
        risk = RiskManager(TRADING_CONFIG)

        timestamp = datetime(2026, 1, 5, 10, 0)

        risk.update(
            make_trade(timestamp, "-300.00")
        )

        risk.update_floating_pnl(
            Decimal("-100.00")
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            risk.current_weekly_pnl(),
            Decimal("-400.00")
        )

        self.assertTrue(
            risk.weekly_loss_reached()
        )

        self.assertFalse(
            risk.can_open_trade(timestamp)
        )

import unittest
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.p_gap import PGapEvent
from strategies.second_leg import SecondLegDetector, SecondLegEvent


class TestSecondLegDetector(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.detector = SecondLegDetector()
        self.start = datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0)

    def make_candle(
        self,
        offset: int,
        open_price: str,
        high: str,
        low: str,
        close: str,
    ) -> Candle:
        return Candle(
            timestamp=self.start + timedelta(minutes=5 * offset),
            open=float(open_price),
            high=float(high),
            low=float(low),
            close=float(close),
        )

    def make_p_gap_event(
        self,
        direction: str,
        pre_breakout: Candle,
        breakout: Candle,
        follow_through: Candle,
    ) -> PGapEvent:
        return PGapEvent(
            direction=direction,
            pre_breakout_candle=pre_breakout,
            breakout_candle=breakout,
            follow_through_candle=follow_through,
        )

    def test_detects_bullish_second_leg_when_low_reaches_reference_low(self):
        reference = self.make_candle(
            0, "104.0", "106.0", "100.0", "105.0"
        )

        pullback = self.make_candle(
            1, "105.0", "106.0", "100.0", "102.0"
        )

        pre_breakout = self.make_candle(
            2, "99.0", "100.0", "97.0", "99.5"
        )

        breakout = self.make_candle(
            3, "99.5", "108.0", "99.0", "107.0"
        )

        follow_through = self.make_candle(
            4, "107.0", "110.0", "105.0", "109.0"
        )

        p_gap = self.make_p_gap_event(
            "buy",
            pre_breakout,
            breakout,
            follow_through,
        )

        result = self.detector.detect(
            p_gap_event=p_gap,
            reference_candle=reference,
            pullback_candle=pullback,
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertIsInstance(result, SecondLegEvent)
        self.assertEqual(result.direction, "buy")
        self.assertEqual(result.reference_candle, reference)
        self.assertEqual(result.pullback_candle, pullback)
        self.assertEqual(result.p_gap, p_gap)

    def test_detects_bullish_second_leg_when_low_breaks_reference_low(self):
        reference = self.make_candle(
            0, "104.0", "106.0", "100.0", "105.0"
        )

        pullback = self.make_candle(
            1, "105.0", "106.0", "99.5", "102.0"
        )

        pre_breakout = self.make_candle(
            2, "99.0", "100.0", "97.0", "99.5"
        )

        breakout = self.make_candle(
            3, "99.5", "108.0", "99.0", "107.0"
        )

        follow_through = self.make_candle(
            4, "107.0", "110.0", "105.0", "109.0"
        )

        p_gap = self.make_p_gap_event(
            "buy",
            pre_breakout,
            breakout,
            follow_through,
        )

        result = self.detector.detect(
            p_gap_event=p_gap,
            reference_candle=reference,
            pullback_candle=pullback,
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(result.direction, "buy")

    def test_rejects_bullish_pullback_above_reference_low(self):
        reference = self.make_candle(
            0, "104.0", "106.0", "100.0", "105.0"
        )

        pullback = self.make_candle(
            1, "105.0", "106.0", "100.1", "102.0"
        )

        pre_breakout = self.make_candle(
            2, "99.0", "100.0", "97.0", "99.5"
        )

        breakout = self.make_candle(
            3, "99.5", "108.0", "99.0", "107.0"
        )

        follow_through = self.make_candle(
            4, "107.0", "110.0", "105.0", "109.0"
        )

        p_gap = self.make_p_gap_event(
            "buy",
            pre_breakout,
            breakout,
            follow_through,
        )

        result = self.detector.detect(
            p_gap_event=p_gap,
            reference_candle=reference,
            pullback_candle=pullback,
        )

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_detects_bearish_second_leg_when_high_reaches_reference_high(self):
        reference = self.make_candle(
            0, "96.0", "100.0", "94.0", "95.0"
        )

        pullback = self.make_candle(
            1, "95.0", "100.0", "94.0", "97.0"
        )

        pre_breakout = self.make_candle(
            2, "101.0", "103.0", "100.0", "102.0"
        )

        breakout = self.make_candle(
            3, "102.0", "102.5", "94.0", "95.0"
        )

        follow_through = self.make_candle(
            4, "95.0", "97.0", "91.0", "92.0"
        )

        p_gap = self.make_p_gap_event(
            "sell",
            pre_breakout,
            breakout,
            follow_through,
        )

        result = self.detector.detect(
            p_gap_event=p_gap,
            reference_candle=reference,
            pullback_candle=pullback,
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertIsInstance(result, SecondLegEvent)
        self.assertEqual(result.direction, "sell")
        self.assertEqual(result.reference_candle, reference)
        self.assertEqual(result.pullback_candle, pullback)
        self.assertEqual(result.p_gap, p_gap)

    def test_detects_bearish_second_leg_when_high_breaks_reference_high(self):
        reference = self.make_candle(
            0, "96.0", "100.0", "94.0", "95.0"
        )

        pullback = self.make_candle(
            1, "95.0", "100.5", "94.0", "97.0"
        )

        pre_breakout = self.make_candle(
            2, "101.0", "103.0", "100.0", "102.0"
        )

        breakout = self.make_candle(
            3, "102.0", "102.5", "94.0", "95.0"
        )

        follow_through = self.make_candle(
            4, "95.0", "97.0", "91.0", "92.0"
        )

        p_gap = self.make_p_gap_event(
            "sell",
            pre_breakout,
            breakout,
            follow_through,
        )

        result = self.detector.detect(
            p_gap_event=p_gap,
            reference_candle=reference,
            pullback_candle=pullback,
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(result.direction, "sell")

    def test_rejects_bearish_pullback_below_reference_high(self):
        reference = self.make_candle(
            0, "96.0", "100.0", "94.0", "95.0"
        )

        pullback = self.make_candle(
            1, "95.0", "99.9", "94.0", "97.0"
        )

        pre_breakout = self.make_candle(
            2, "101.0", "103.0", "100.0", "102.0"
        )

        breakout = self.make_candle(
            3, "102.0", "102.5", "94.0", "95.0"
        )

        follow_through = self.make_candle(
            4, "95.0", "97.0", "91.0", "92.0"
        )

        p_gap = self.make_p_gap_event(
            "sell",
            pre_breakout,
            breakout,
            follow_through,
        )

        result = self.detector.detect(
            p_gap_event=p_gap,
            reference_candle=reference,
            pullback_candle=pullback,
        )

        self.assertIsNone(result)

FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_signal_bar.py

import unittest
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.p_gap import PGapEvent
from strategies.pullback_structure import (
    PullbackStructureEvent,
)
from strategies.second_leg import SecondLegEvent
from strategies.signal_bar import SignalBarDetector

from strategies.structure import StructurePoint


class TestSignalBarDetector(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.start = datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0)

    def make_candle(
        self,
        offset: int,
        open_price: str,
        high: str,
        low: str,
        close: str,
    ) -> Candle:
        return Candle(
            timestamp=self.start + timedelta(minutes=5 * offset),
            open=float(open_price),
            high=float(high),
            low=float(low),
            close=float(close),
        )

    def make_pullback_structure(
        self,
        direction: str,
        latest_structure_offset: int = 10,
        latest_structure_price: str = "105",
    ) -> PullbackStructureEvent:

        pullback = self.make_candle(
            3,
            "105",
            "110",
            "100",
            "102",
        )

        breakout = self.make_candle(
            1,
            "100",
            "108",
            "100",
            "107",
        )

        pre_breakout = self.make_candle(
            0,
            "99",
            "100",
            "97",
            "99",
        )

        follow_through = self.make_candle(
            2,
            "107",
            "110",
            "105",
            "109",
        )

        p_gap = PGapEvent(
            direction=direction,
            pre_breakout_candle=pre_breakout,
            breakout_candle=breakout,
            follow_through_candle=follow_through,
        )

        second_leg = SecondLegEvent(
            direction=direction,
            p_gap=p_gap,
            pullback_candle=pullback,
            reference_candle=pre_breakout,
        )

        structure_type = (
            "lower_high"
            if direction == "buy"
            else "higher_low"
        )

        structure_candle = self.make_candle(
            latest_structure_offset,
            latest_structure_price,
            latest_structure_price,
            latest_structure_price,
            latest_structure_price,
        )

        structure_point = StructurePoint(
            type=structure_type,
            candle=structure_candle,
            price=Decimal(latest_structure_price),
        )

        return PullbackStructureEvent(
            direction=direction,
            second_leg=second_leg,
            anchor_candle=pullback,
            structure_points=[structure_point],
        )

    def test_detects_bullish_signal_bar_breaking_previous_high(self):
        pullback = self.make_pullback_structure("buy")

        previous = self.make_candle(
            10,
            "104",
            "105",
            "102",
            "104",
        )

        signal = self.make_candle(
            11,
            "104",
            "108",
            "103",
            "106",
        )

        detector = SignalBarDetector()

        result = detector.detect(
            pullback,
            previous,
            signal,
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(result.direction, "buy")
        self.assertEqual(result.signal_candle, signal)

    def test_detects_bearish_signal_bar_breaking_previous_low(self):
        pullback = self.make_pullback_structure("sell")

        previous = self.make_candle(
            10,
            "104",
            "106",
            "102",
            "104",
        )

        signal = self.make_candle(
            11,
            "103",
            "104",
            "98",
            "100",
        )

        detector = SignalBarDetector()

        result = detector.detect(
            pullback,
            previous,
            signal,
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(result.direction, "sell")
        self.assertEqual(result.signal_candle, signal)

    def test_bullish_signal_requires_break_of_previous_high(self):
        pullback = self.make_pullback_structure("buy")

        previous = self.make_candle(
            10,
            "104",
            "105",
            "102",
            "104",
        )

        # High breaks previous high.
        # Close does NOT need to remain above it.
        signal = self.make_candle(
            11,
            "104",
            "108",
            "103",
            "104.5",
        )

        detector = SignalBarDetector()

        result = detector.detect(
            pullback,
            previous,
            signal,
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(result.direction, "buy")
        self.assertEqual(result.signal_candle, signal)

    def test_bearish_signal_requires_break_of_previous_low(self):
        pullback = self.make_pullback_structure("sell")

        previous = self.make_candle(
            10,
            "104",
            "106",
            "102",
            "104",
        )

        # Low breaks previous low.
        # Close does NOT need to remain below it.
        signal = self.make_candle(
            11,
            "103",
            "105",
            "98",
            "103",
        )

        detector = SignalBarDetector()

        result = detector.detect(
            pullback,
            previous,
            signal,
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(result.direction, "sell")
        self.assertEqual(result.signal_candle, signal)

    def test_signal_candle_must_be_after_previous_candle(self):
        pullback = self.make_pullback_structure("buy")

        previous = self.make_candle(
            11,
            "104",
            "105",
            "102",
            "104",
        )

        signal = self.make_candle(
            10,
            "104",
            "108",
            "103",
            "106",
        )

        detector = SignalBarDetector()

        result = detector.detect(
            pullback,
            previous,
            signal,
        )

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_signal_bar_event_preserves_pullback_structure(self):
        pullback = self.make_pullback_structure("buy")

        previous = self.make_candle(
            10,
            "104",
            "105",
            "102",
            "104",
        )

        signal = self.make_candle(
            11,
            "104",
            "108",
            "103",
            "106",
        )

        detector = SignalBarDetector()

        result = detector.detect(
            pullback,
            previous,
            signal,
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertIs(result.pullback_structure, pullback)



================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_sp2l.py
