import unittest
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import Mock

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.key_bar import KeyBarEvent
from strategies.p_gap import PGapEvent
from strategies.pullback_structure import PullbackStructureEvent
from strategies.second_leg import SecondLegEvent
from strategies.signal_bar import SignalBarEvent
from strategies.sp2l import SP2LStateMachine
from strategies.sp2l_state import SP2LSetup, SP2LState
from strategies.spike_event import SpikeEvent
from strategies.spike_measurements import SpikeMeasurements
from strategies.structure import StructurePoint


BASE_TIME = datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0)


def make_candle(
    minute: int,
    open_price: float = 100.0,
    high: float = 101.0,
    low: float = 99.0,
    close: float = 100.5,
) -> Candle:
    return Candle(
        timestamp=BASE_TIME + timedelta(minutes=minute),
        open=open_price,
        high=high,
        low=low,
        close=close,
    )


def make_spike(candle: Candle) -> SpikeEvent:
    measurements = SpikeMeasurements(
        direction="buy",
        total_range=2.0,
        body=1.5,
        upper_wick=0.2,
        lower_wick=0.3,
        body_ratio_of_range=0.75,
        closing_wick_ratio=0.10,
        average_previous_range=1.0,
        average_previous_body=0.8,
        range_ratio=2.0,
        body_ratio=1.875,
        previous_high=99.0,
        previous_low=98.0,
        high_breakout_distance=2.0,
        low_breakout_distance=0.0,
        close_breakout_distance=1.5,
        is_spike=True,
        breakout_distance=1.5,
    )

    return SpikeEvent(
        breakout_candle=candle,
        direction="buy",
        measurements=measurements,
    )


def make_p_gap(
    spike: SpikeEvent,
    pre_breakout: Candle,
    follow_through: Candle,
) -> PGapEvent:
    return PGapEvent(
        direction="buy",
        pre_breakout_candle=pre_breakout,
        breakout_candle=spike.breakout_candle,
        follow_through_candle=follow_through,
    )


def make_second_leg(
    p_gap: PGapEvent,
    reference: Candle,
    anchor: Candle,
) -> SecondLegEvent:
    return SecondLegEvent(
        direction="buy",
        p_gap=p_gap,
        pullback_candle=anchor,
        reference_candle=reference,
    )


def make_pullback_structure(
    second_leg: SecondLegEvent,
    anchor: Candle,
) -> PullbackStructureEvent:
    structure_points = [
        StructurePoint(
            type="lower_high",
            candle=make_candle(
                25,
                open_price=99.0,
                high=100.0,
                low=98.5,
                close=99.5,
            ),
            price=100.0,
        ),
        StructurePoint(
            type="lower_high",
            candle=make_candle(
                30,
                open_price=98.5,
                high=99.5,
                low=97.8,
                close=99.0,
            ),
            price=99.5,
        ),
        StructurePoint(
            type="lower_high",
            candle=make_candle(
                35,
                open_price=98.0,
                high=99.0,
                low=97.2,
                close=98.5,
            ),
            price=99.0,
        ),
    ]

    return PullbackStructureEvent(
        direction="buy",
        second_leg=second_leg,
        anchor_candle=anchor,
        structure_points=structure_points,
    )


def make_signal_bar(
    pullback_structure: PullbackStructureEvent,
    candle: Candle,
) -> SignalBarEvent:
    return SignalBarEvent(
        direction="buy",
        pullback_structure=pullback_structure,
        signal_candle=candle,
    )


def make_key_bar(
    signal_bar: SignalBarEvent,
    candle: Candle,
) -> KeyBarEvent:
    return KeyBarEvent(
        direction="buy",
        signal_bar=signal_bar,
        key_candle=candle,
    )


class TestSP2LStateMachine(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.spike_detector = Mock()
        self.spike_detector.config = SimpleNamespace(
            lookback=2
        )

        self.p_gap_detector = Mock()
        self.second_leg_detector = Mock()
        self.pullback_structure_detector = Mock()
        self.signal_bar_detector = Mock()
        self.key_bar_detector = Mock()
        self.swing_processor = Mock()

        self.machine = SP2LStateMachine(
            spike_detector=self.spike_detector,
            p_gap_detector=self.p_gap_detector,
            second_leg_detector=self.second_leg_detector,
            pullback_structure_detector=(
                self.pullback_structure_detector
            ),
            signal_bar_detector=self.signal_bar_detector,
            key_bar_detector=self.key_bar_detector,
            swing_processor=self.swing_processor,
        )

    def _enter_spike_validated(self):
        candle_0 = make_candle(0)
        candle_1 = make_candle(5)
        spike_candle = make_candle(10)

        spike = make_spike(spike_candle)

        self.spike_detector.detect_event.return_value = spike

        self.machine.process(candle_0)
        self.machine.process(candle_1)
        self.machine.process(spike_candle)

        return candle_1, spike_candle, spike

    def _enter_pullback(self):
        pre_breakout, spike_candle, spike = (
            self._enter_spike_validated()
        )

        follow_through = make_candle(15)

        p_gap = make_p_gap(
            spike=spike,
            pre_breakout=pre_breakout,
            follow_through=follow_through,
        )

        self.p_gap_detector.detect.return_value = p_gap

        self.machine.process(follow_through)

        return (
            pre_breakout,
            spike_candle,
            follow_through,
            spike,
            p_gap,
        )

    def _enter_compression(self):
        (
            pre_breakout,
            spike_candle,
            follow_through,
            spike,
            p_gap,
        ) = self._enter_pullback()

        anchor = make_candle(
            20,
            open_price=102.0,
            high=103.0,
            low=100.0,
            close=101.5,
        )

        second_leg = make_second_leg(
            p_gap=p_gap,
            reference=follow_through,
            anchor=anchor,
        )

        self.second_leg_detector.detect.return_value = (
            second_leg
        )

        self.machine.process(anchor)

        structure_candle = make_candle(40)

        pullback_structure = make_pullback_structure(
            second_leg=second_leg,
            anchor=anchor,
        )

        self.pullback_structure_detector.detect.return_value = (
            pullback_structure
        )

        self.machine.process(structure_candle)

        return (
            pre_breakout,
            spike_candle,
            follow_through,
            anchor,
            structure_candle,
            spike,
            p_gap,
            second_leg,
            pullback_structure,
        )

    def test_starts_in_idle(self):
        self.assertEqual(
            self.machine.state,
            SP2LState.IDLE,
        )

    def test_no_spike_keeps_machine_idle(self):
        self.spike_detector.detect_event.return_value = None

        self.machine.process(make_candle(0))
        self.machine.process(make_candle(5))
        self.machine.process(make_candle(10))

        self.assertEqual(
            self.machine.state,
            SP2LState.IDLE,
        )

        self.assertIsNone(
            self.machine.pending_spike
        )

    def test_spike_moves_machine_to_spike_validated(self):
        candle_1, spike_candle, spike = (
            self._enter_spike_validated()
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            self.machine.state,
            SP2LState.SPIKE_VALIDATED,
        )

        self.assertIs(
            self.machine.pending_spike,
            spike,
        )

        self.assertIs(
            self.machine._pre_breakout_candle,
            candle_1,
        )

    def test_failed_p_gap_resets_machine(self):
        self._enter_spike_validated()

        self.p_gap_detector.detect.return_value = None

        self.machine.process(make_candle(15))

        self.assertEqual(
            self.machine.state,
            SP2LState.IDLE,
        )

        self.assertIsNone(
            self.machine.pending_spike
        )

        self.assertIsNone(
            self.machine.p_gap
        )

    def test_valid_p_gap_moves_to_pullback(self):
        (
            pre_breakout,
            spike_candle,
            follow_through,
            spike,
            p_gap,
        ) = self._enter_pullback()

        self.assertEqual(
            self.machine.state,
            SP2LState.PULLBACK,
        )

        self.assertIs(
            self.machine.p_gap,
            p_gap,
        )

        self.p_gap_detector.detect.assert_called_once_with(
            spike_event=spike,
            pre_breakout_candle=pre_breakout,
            follow_through_candle=follow_through,
        )

    def test_second_leg_creates_anchor(self):
        (
            _,
            _,
            follow_through,
            _,
            p_gap,
        ) = self._enter_pullback()

        anchor = make_candle(
            20,
            open_price=102.0,
            high=103.0,
            low=100.0,
            close=101.5,
        )

        second_leg = make_second_leg(
            p_gap=p_gap,
            reference=follow_through,
            anchor=anchor,
        )

        self.second_leg_detector.detect.return_value = (
            second_leg
        )

        self.machine.process(anchor)

        self.assertEqual(
            self.machine.state,
            SP2LState.PULLBACK,
        )

        self.assertIs(
            self.machine.second_leg,
            second_leg,
        )

        self.second_leg_detector.detect.assert_called_once_with(
            p_gap_event=p_gap,
            reference_candle=follow_through,
            pullback_candle=anchor,
        )

    def test_valid_pullback_structure_moves_to_compression(self):
        (
            _,
            _,
            _,
            _,
            _,
            _,
            _,
            second_leg,
            pullback_structure,
        ) = self._enter_compression()

        self.assertEqual(
            self.machine.state,
            SP2LState.COMPRESSION_VALID,
        )

        self.assertIs(
            self.machine.pullback_structure,
            pullback_structure,
        )

        self.assertIs(
            self.machine.pullback_structure.second_leg,
            second_leg,
        )

        self.pullback_structure_detector.detect.assert_called_once()
        
    def test_compression_violation_returns_to_pullback(self):
        (
            _,
            _,
            _,
            anchor,
            _,
            _,
            _,
            second_leg,
            _,
        ) = self._enter_compression()

        self.assertEqual(
            self.machine.state,
            SP2LState.COMPRESSION_VALID,
        )

        # The current compression sequence is broken,
        # but this candle does NOT violate the original Anchor.
        violating_candle = make_candle(
            45,
            open_price=101.0,
            high=102.0,
            low=99.0,
            close=100.0,
        )

        # The detector reports that the existing compression
        # sequence is no longer valid.
        self.pullback_structure_detector.detect.return_value = None

        self.machine.process(violating_candle)

        self.assertEqual(
            self.machine.state,
            SP2LState.PULLBACK,
        )

        self.assertIs(
            self.machine.second_leg,
            second_leg,
        )

        self.assertIsNone(
            self.machine.pullback_structure,
        )
    def test_anchor_invalidation_resets_pullback(self):
        self._enter_pullback()

        anchor = make_candle(
            20,
            open_price=102.0,
            high=103.0,
            low=100.0,
            close=101.5,
        )

        second_leg = make_second_leg(
            p_gap=self.machine.p_gap,
            reference=make_candle(15),
            anchor=anchor,
        )

        self.machine._second_leg = second_leg

        invalidating_candle = make_candle(
            25,
            open_price=102.0,
            high=103.1,
            low=100.5,
            close=102.5,
        )

        self.machine.process(invalidating_candle)

        self.assertEqual(
            self.machine.state,
            SP2LState.IDLE,
        )

        self.assertIsNone(
            self.machine.second_leg
        )

    def test_non_anchor_violation_keeps_pullback_alive(self):
        (
            _,
            _,
            follow_through,
            _,
            p_gap,
        ) = self._enter_pullback()

        anchor = make_candle(
            20,
            open_price=102.0,
            high=103.0,
            low=100.0,
            close=101.5,
        )

        second_leg = make_second_leg(
            p_gap=p_gap,
            reference=follow_through,
            anchor=anchor,
        )

        self.machine._second_leg = second_leg

        # This breaks the current compression sequence,
        # but remains below the original Anchor high of 103.
        violating_candle = make_candle(
            25,
            open_price=101.0,
            high=102.0,
            low=99.0,
            close=100.0,
        )

        self.pullback_structure_detector.detect.return_value = None

        self.machine.process(violating_candle)

        self.assertEqual(
            self.machine.state,
            SP2LState.PULLBACK,
        )

        self.assertIs(
            self.machine.second_leg,
            second_leg,
        )

        # A fresh compression sequence can now form
        # under the same original Anchor.
        new_sequence_candle = make_candle(
            30,
            open_price=99.0,
            high=101.0,
            low=97.0,
            close=100.0,
        )

        pullback_structure = make_pullback_structure(
            second_leg=second_leg,
            anchor=anchor,
        )

        self.pullback_structure_detector.detect.return_value = (
            pullback_structure
        )

        self.machine.process(new_sequence_candle)

        self.assertEqual(
            self.machine.state,
            SP2LState.COMPRESSION_VALID,
        )

        self.assertIs(
            self.machine.pullback_structure,
            pullback_structure,
        )

    def test_eighth_consecutive_structure_point_invalidates_setup(self):
        (
            _,
            _,
            follow_through,
            _,
            p_gap,
        ) = self._enter_pullback()

        anchor = make_candle(
            20,
            open_price=102.0,
            high=103.0,
            low=100.0,
            close=101.5,
        )

        second_leg = make_second_leg(
            p_gap=p_gap,
            reference=follow_through,
            anchor=anchor,
        )

        self.machine._second_leg = second_leg

        invalidating_candle = make_candle(
            40,
            open_price=98.0,
            high=97.5,
            low=96.0,
            close=97.0,
        )

        self.pullback_structure_detector.detect.return_value = None

        self.machine.process(invalidating_candle)

        self.assertEqual(
            self.machine.state,
            SP2LState.PULLBACK,
        )

        self.assertIs(
            self.machine.second_leg,
            second_leg,
        )

        # The detector is responsible for rejecting the 8th
        # consecutive structural point. The state machine should
        # therefore remain alive under the same Anchor.
        self.assertIsNone(
            self.machine.pullback_structure,
        )


    def test_signal_bar_moves_to_waiting_for_key_bar(self):
        (
            _,
            _,
            _,
            _,
            _,
            _,
            _,
            _,
            pullback_structure,
        ) = self._enter_compression()

        signal_candle = make_candle(45)

        signal_bar = make_signal_bar(
            pullback_structure,
            signal_candle,
        )

        self.signal_bar_detector.detect.return_value = (
            signal_bar
        )

        self.machine.process(signal_candle)

        self.assertEqual(
            self.machine.state,
            SP2LState.WAITING_FOR_KEY_BAR,
        )

        self.assertIs(
            self.machine.signal_bar,
            signal_bar,
        )

    def test_failed_key_bar_resets_machine(self):
        signal_bar = Mock(spec=SignalBarEvent)

        self.machine.state = (
            SP2LState.WAITING_FOR_KEY_BAR
        )
        self.machine._signal_bar = signal_bar

        self.key_bar_detector.detect.return_value = None

        result = self.machine.process(
            make_candle(45)
        )

        self.assertIsNone(result)

        self.assertEqual(
            self.machine.state,
            SP2LState.IDLE,
        )

        self.assertIsNone(
            self.machine.signal_bar
        )

    def test_valid_key_bar_returns_complete_setup(self):
        (
            _,
            _,
            _,
            _,
            _,
            spike,
            p_gap,
            _,
            pullback_structure,
        ) = self._enter_compression()

        signal_candle = make_candle(45)
        key_candle = make_candle(50)

        signal_bar = make_signal_bar(
            pullback_structure,
            signal_candle,
        )

        key_bar = make_key_bar(
            signal_bar,
            key_candle,
        )

        self.signal_bar_detector.detect.return_value = (
            signal_bar
        )
        self.key_bar_detector.detect.return_value = (
            key_bar
        )

        self.machine.process(signal_candle)

        result = self.machine.process(key_candle)

        self.assertIsInstance(
            result,
            SP2LSetup,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            result.direction,
            "buy",
        )

        self.assertIs(
            result.spike,
            spike,
        )

        self.assertIs(
            result.p_gap,
            p_gap,
        )

        self.assertIs(
            result.pullback_structure,
            pullback_structure,
        )

        self.assertIs(
            result.signal_bar,
            signal_bar,
        )

        self.assertIs(
            result.key_bar,
            key_bar,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            self.machine.state,
            SP2LState.IDLE,
        )

    def test_confirmed_key_bar_produces_only_one_setup(self):
        signal_bar = Mock(spec=SignalBarEvent)

        key_bar = Mock(spec=KeyBarEvent)
        key_bar.direction = "buy"

        self.machine.state = (
            SP2LState.WAITING_FOR_KEY_BAR
        )

        self.machine._pending_spike = Mock(
            spec=SpikeEvent
        )
        self.machine._p_gap = Mock(
            spec=PGapEvent
        )
        self.machine._pullback_structure = Mock(
            spec=PullbackStructureEvent
        )
        self.machine._signal_bar = signal_bar

        self.key_bar_detector.detect.return_value = (
            key_bar
        )

        first_result = self.machine.process(
            make_candle(45)
        )

        second_result = self.machine.process(
            make_candle(50)
        )

        self.assertIsInstance(
            first_result,
            SP2LSetup,
        )

        self.assertIsNone(
            second_result
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            self.machine.state,
            SP2LState.IDLE,
        )

    def test_state_machine_never_executes_a_trade(self):
        result = self.machine.process(
            make_candle(0)
        )

        self.assertIsNone(result)

        self.assertFalse(
            hasattr(self.machine, "account")
        )

        self.assertFalse(
            hasattr(self.machine, "risk_manager")
        )

        self.assertFalse(
            hasattr(self.machine, "pending_order")
        )


================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_sp2l_engine.py
================================================================================

import unittest
from unittest.mock import Mock
from datetime import datetime, timedelta


from backtest.engine import BacktestEngine
from config.instrument import XAUUSD
from config.trading import TRADING_CONFIG
from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.sp2l_strategy import SP2LStrategy
from unittest.mock import Mock

from backtest.signal import Signal
from strategies.sp2l_strategy import SP2LStrategy



BASE_TIME = datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0)
def make_candle(
    minute: int,
    open_price: float = 100.0,
    high: float = 101.0,
    low: float = 99.0,
    close: float = 100.5,
) -> Candle:
    return Candle(
        timestamp=BASE_TIME + timedelta(minutes=minute),
        open=open_price,
        high=high,
        low=low,
        close=close,
    )

class TestSP2LEngineIntegration(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_engine_accepts_sp2l_strategy(self):
        candles = [
            Candle(
                timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
                open=4000.0,
                high=4001.0,
                low=3999.0,
                close=4000.5,
            ),
            Candle(
                timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
                open=4000.5,
                high=4002.0,
                low=4000.0,
                close=4001.5,
            ),
        ]

        strategy = SP2LStrategy(
            state_machine=unittest.mock.Mock()
        )

        strategy.state_machine.process.return_value = None

        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=candles,
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=TRADING_CONFIG,
            stop_loss_distance=100,
            take_profit_distance=200,
        )

        result = engine.run(strategy)

        self.assertEqual(
            len(result.completed_trades),
            0,
        )

        self.assertIsNone(
            result.open_trade,
        )

    def test_sp2l_strategy_converts_buy_setup_to_buy_signal(self):
        state_machine = Mock()

        state_machine.process.return_value = Mock(
            direction="buy"
        )

        strategy = SP2LStrategy(
            state_machine=state_machine
        )

        candle = Candle(
            timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
            open=4000.0,
            high=4001.0,
            low=3999.0,
            close=4000.5,
        )

        signal = strategy.on_candle(candle)

        self.assertEqual(
            signal,
            Signal.BUY,
        )

        state_machine.process.assert_called_once_with(
            candle
        )

    def test_sp2l_buy_signal_executes_on_next_candle(self):
        state_machine = Mock()

        state_machine.process.side_effect = [
            None,
            Mock(direction="buy"),
            None,
        ]

        strategy = SP2LStrategy(
            state_machine=state_machine
        )

        candles = [
            Candle(
                timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
                open=4000.0,
                high=4001.0,
                low=3999.0,
                close=4000.5,
            ),
            Candle(
                timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
                open=4010.0,
                high=4011.0,
                low=4009.0,
                close=4010.5,
            ),
            Candle(
                timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 10),
                open=4020.0,
                high=4020.1,
                low=4019.95,
                close=4020.05,
            ),
        ]

        engine = BacktestEngine(
            candles=candles,
            instrument=XAUUSD,
            trading_config=TRADING_CONFIG,
            stop_loss_distance=100,
            take_profit_distance=200,
        )

        result = engine.run(strategy)

        self.assertIsNotNone(
            result.open_trade
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            result.open_trade.signal_time,
            candles[1].timestamp,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            result.open_trade.entry_time,
            candles[2].timestamp,
        )
    


================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_spike.py
================================================================================

import unittest
from decimal import Decimal
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.spike import SpikeDetector, SpikeDetectorConfig


class TestSpikeDetector(unittest.TestCase):

    def make_candle(
        self,
        minute: int,
        open_price: float,
        high: float,
        low: float,
        close: float,
    ) -> Candle:
        return Candle(
            timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 9, minute),
            open=open_price,
            high=high,
            low=low,
            close=close,
        )

    def test_insufficient_history_returns_none(self):
        detector = SpikeDetector(
            SpikeDetectorConfig(lookback=5)
        )

        candles = [
            self.make_candle(
                minute=i * 5,
                open_price=100,
                high=101,
                low=99,
                close=100.5,
            )
            for i in range(5)
        ]

        result = detector.detect(candles)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_large_breakout_candle_is_spike(self):
        detector = SpikeDetector(
            SpikeDetectorConfig(lookback=5)
        )

        candles = [
            self.make_candle(
                minute=i * 5,
                open_price=100 + i * 0.2,
                high=101 + i * 0.2,
                low=99 + i * 0.2,
                close=100.5 + i * 0.2,
            )
            for i in range(5)
        ]

        candles.append(
            self.make_candle(
                minute=25,
                open_price=101,
                high=105,
                low=100.8,
                close=104.8,
            )
        )

        result = detector.detect(candles)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertTrue(result.is_spike)
        self.assertEqual(result.direction, "buy")

    def test_large_candle_without_breakout_is_not_spike(self):
        detector = SpikeDetector(
            SpikeDetectorConfig(lookback=5)
        )

        candles = [
            self.make_candle(
                minute=i * 5,
                open_price=100,
                high=101,
                low=99,
                close=100.5,
            )
            for i in range(5)
        ]

        # Large candle, but it remains inside the previous range.
        candles.append(
            self.make_candle(
                minute=25,
                open_price=99.5,
                high=101,
                low=98.5,
                close=100.8,
            )
        )

        result = detector.detect(candles)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertFalse(result.is_spike)

    def test_small_candle_after_small_context_is_not_spike(self):
        detector = SpikeDetector(
            SpikeDetectorConfig(lookback=5)
        )

        candles = [
            self.make_candle(
                minute=i * 5,
                open_price=100,
                high=101,
                low=99,
                close=100.5,
            )
            for i in range(6)
        ]

        result = detector.detect(candles)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertFalse(result.is_spike)

    def test_bearish_breakout_is_detected(self):
        detector = SpikeDetector(
            SpikeDetectorConfig(lookback=5)
        )

        candles = [
            self.make_candle(
                minute=i * 5,
                open_price=100,
                high=101,
                low=99,
                close=99.5,
            )
            for i in range(5)
        ]

        candles.append(
            self.make_candle(
                minute=25,
                open_price=99,
                high=99.2,
                low=94.8,
                close=95,
            )
        )

        result = detector.detect(candles)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertTrue(result.is_spike)
        self.assertEqual(result.direction, "sell")

    def test_large_closing_side_wick_blocks_spike(self):
        detector = SpikeDetector(
            SpikeDetectorConfig(lookback=5)
        )

        candles = [
            self.make_candle(
                minute=i * 5,
                open_price=100,
                high=101,
                low=99,
                close=100.5,
            )
            for i in range(5)
        ]

        candles.append(
            self.make_candle(
                minute=25,
                open_price=101,
                high=105,
                low=100.8,
                close=101.5,
            )
        )

        result = detector.detect(candles)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertFalse(result.is_spike)

    def test_measurements_are_relative_to_previous_candles(self):
        detector = SpikeDetector(
            SpikeDetectorConfig(lookback=5)
        )

        candles = [
            self.make_candle(
                minute=i * 5,
                open_price=100,
                high=101,
                low=99,
                close=100.5,
            )
            for i in range(5)
        ]

        candles.append(
            self.make_candle(
                minute=25,
                open_price=100.5,
                high=104,
                low=100,
                close=103.8,
            )
        )

        result = detector.detect(candles)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)

        self.assertGreater(
            result.range_ratio,
            Decimal("1"),
        )

        self.assertGreater(
            result.body_ratio,
            Decimal("1"),
        )

        self.assertGreater(
            result.breakout_distance,
            Decimal("0"),
        )

    def test_detector_is_timeframe_agnostic(self):
        detector = SpikeDetector(
            SpikeDetectorConfig(lookback=3)
        )

        m5_candles = [
            self.make_candle(
                minute=i * 5,
                open_price=100,
                high=101,
                low=99,
                close=100.5,
            )
            for i in range(3)
        ]

        m5_candles.append(
            self.make_candle(
                minute=15,
                open_price=100.5,
                high=104,
                low=100.3,
                close=103.8,
            )
        )

        result = detector.detect(m5_candles)

        self.assertTrue(result.is_spike)

        # The detector itself has no M5/M15-specific logic.
        # A caller can feed it M15 candles using the same interface.
        self.assertEqual(result.direction, "buy")


    def test_intrabar_and_close_breakouts_are_measured_separately(self):
        detector = SpikeDetector(
            SpikeDetectorConfig(lookback=5)
        )

        candles = [
            self.make_candle(
                minute=i * 5,
                open_price=100,
                high=101,
                low=99,
                close=100.5,
            )
            for i in range(5)
        ]

        # High breaks the previous range, but close remains inside it.
        candles.append(
            self.make_candle(
                minute=25,
                open_price=100,
                high=102,
                low=99.8,
                close=100.5,
            )
        )

        result = detector.detect(candles)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)

        self.assertGreater(
            result.high_breakout_distance,
            Decimal("0"),
        )

        self.assertLessEqual(
            result.close_breakout_distance,
            Decimal("0"),
        )

    def test_bearish_intrabar_and_close_breakouts_are_measured(self):
        detector = SpikeDetector(
            SpikeDetectorConfig(lookback=5)
        )

        candles = [
            self.make_candle(
                minute=i * 5,
                open_price=100,
                high=101,
                low=99,
                close=99.5,
            )
            for i in range(5)
        ]

        # Low breaks the previous range and the close also breaks it.
        candles.append(
            self.make_candle(
                minute=25,
                open_price=99.5,
                high=99.7,
                low=96,
                close=96.2,
            )
        )

        result = detector.detect(candles)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)

        self.assertGreater(
            result.low_breakout_distance,
            Decimal("0"),
        )

        self.assertGreater(
            result.close_breakout_distance,
            Decimal("0"),
        )


================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_spike_event.py
================================================================================

import unittest
from datetime import datetime
from decimal import Decimal

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.spike import SpikeDetector
from strategies.spike_event import SpikeEvent


class TestSpikeEvent(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.detector = SpikeDetector()

    def make_candle(
        self,
        minute: int,
        open_price: str,
        high: str,
        low: str,
        close: str,
    ) -> Candle:
        return Candle(
            timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, minute),
            open=Decimal(open_price),
            high=Decimal(high),
            low=Decimal(low),
            close=Decimal(close),
        )

    def test_creates_event_for_valid_bullish_spike(self):
        candles = [
            self.make_candle(0, "100", "101", "99", "100.5"),
            self.make_candle(5, "100.2", "101.2", "99.8", "100.8"),
            self.make_candle(10, "100.4", "101.4", "100", "101"),
            self.make_candle(15, "100.6", "101.6", "100.2", "101.2"),
            self.make_candle(20, "100.8", "101.8", "100.4", "101.4"),
            self.make_candle(25, "101", "105", "100.8", "104.8"),
        ]

        event = self.detector.detect_event(candles)

        self.assertIsNotNone(event)
        self.assertIsInstance(event, SpikeEvent)

        assert event is not None

        self.assertEqual(event.direction, "buy")
        self.assertEqual(event.breakout_candle, candles[-1])
        self.assertTrue(event.measurements.is_spike)

    def test_returns_none_when_no_spike_exists(self):
        candles = [
            self.make_candle(0, "100", "101", "99", "100.5"),
            self.make_candle(5, "100.2", "101.2", "99.8", "100.8"),
            self.make_candle(10, "100.4", "101.4", "100", "101"),
            self.make_candle(15, "100.6", "101.6", "100.2", "101.2"),
            self.make_candle(20, "100.8", "101.8", "100.4", "101.4"),
            self.make_candle(25, "101", "102", "100.8", "101.8"),
        ]

        event = self.detector.detect_event(candles)

        self.assertIsNone(event)

    def test_event_preserves_spike_measurements(self):
        candles = [
            self.make_candle(0, "100", "101", "99", "100.5"),
            self.make_candle(5, "100.2", "101.2", "99.8", "100.8"),
            self.make_candle(10, "100.4", "101.4", "100", "101"),
            self.make_candle(15, "100.6", "101.6", "100.2", "101.2"),
            self.make_candle(20, "100.8", "101.8", "100.4", "101.4"),
            self.make_candle(25, "101", "105", "100.8", "104.8"),
        ]

        measurements = self.detector.detect(candles)
        event = self.detector.detect_event(candles)

        self.assertIsNotNone(event)
        assert event is not None

        self.assertEqual(event.measurements, measurements)

FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_structure.py

import unittest
from datetime import datetime

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.structure import (
    detect_higher_low,
    detect_lower_high,
)
from strategies.swing import SwingPoint


class TestStructure(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.candle_1 = Candle(
            timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
            open=100.0,
            high=105.0,
            low=95.0,
            close=102.0,
        )

        self.candle_2 = Candle(
            timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
            open=100.0,
            high=103.0,
            low=97.0,
            close=101.0,
        )

    def test_detects_higher_low(self):
        previous = SwingPoint(
            type="low",
            candle=self.candle_1,
            price=95,
        )

        current = SwingPoint(
            type="low",
            candle=self.candle_2,
            price=97,
        )

        result = detect_higher_low(previous, current)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(result.type, "higher_low")
        self.assertEqual(result.candle, self.candle_2)
        self.assertEqual(result.price, 97)

    def test_lower_low_is_not_higher_low(self):
        previous = SwingPoint(
            type="low",
            candle=self.candle_1,
            price=95,
        )

        current = SwingPoint(
            type="low",
            candle=self.candle_2,
            price=93,
        )

        result = detect_higher_low(previous, current)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_equal_lows_are_not_higher_low(self):
        previous = SwingPoint(
            type="low",
            candle=self.candle_1,
            price=95,
        )

        current = SwingPoint(
            type="low",
            candle=self.candle_2,
            price=95,
        )

        result = detect_higher_low(previous, current)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_different_swing_types_are_not_higher_low(self):
        previous = SwingPoint(
            type="high",
            candle=self.candle_1,
            price=105,
        )

        current = SwingPoint(
            type="low",
            candle=self.candle_2,
            price=97,
        )

        result = detect_higher_low(previous, current)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_detects_lower_high(self):
        previous = SwingPoint(
            type="high",
            candle=self.candle_1,
            price=105,
        )

        current = SwingPoint(
            type="high",
            candle=self.candle_2,
            price=103,
        )

        result = detect_lower_high(previous, current)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(result.type, "lower_high")
        self.assertEqual(result.candle, self.candle_2)
        self.assertEqual(result.price, 103)

    def test_higher_high_is_not_lower_high(self):
        previous = SwingPoint(
            type="high",
            candle=self.candle_1,
            price=105,
        )

        current = SwingPoint(
            type="high",
            candle=self.candle_2,
            price=107,
        )

        result = detect_lower_high(previous, current)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_equal_highs_are_not_lower_high(self):
        previous = SwingPoint(
            type="high",
            candle=self.candle_1,
            price=105,
        )

        current = SwingPoint(
            type="high",
            candle=self.candle_2,
            price=105,
        )

        result = detect_lower_high(previous, current)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_different_swing_types_are_not_lower_high(self):
        previous = SwingPoint(
            type="low",
            candle=self.candle_1,
            price=95,
        )

        current = SwingPoint(
            type="high",
            candle=self.candle_2,
            price=103,
        )

        result = detect_lower_high(previous, current)

        self.assertIsNone(result)



================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_structure_sequence.py
================================================================================

import unittest
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.structure_sequence import StructureSequenceDetector
from strategies.swing import SwingPoint
from strategies.swing_history import SwingHistory


class TestStructureSequenceDetector(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.history = SwingHistory()
        self.detector = StructureSequenceDetector(
            self.history
        )

        self.start = datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0)

    def candle(self, offset: int) -> Candle:
        return Candle(
            timestamp=self.start + timedelta(minutes=5 * offset),
            open=100.0,
            high=110.0,
            low=90.0,
            close=100.0,
        )

    def low(self, offset: int, price: int) -> SwingPoint:
        return SwingPoint(
            type="low",
            candle=self.candle(offset),
            price=price,
        )

    def high(self, offset: int, price: int) -> SwingPoint:
        return SwingPoint(
            type="high",
            candle=self.candle(offset),
            price=price,
        )

    def test_three_higher_lows(self):
        points = [
            self.low(0, 100),
            self.low(1, 105),
            self.low(2, 110),
            self.low(3, 115),
        ]

        for point in points:
            self.history.add(point)

        result = self.detector.higher_low_sequence(3)

        self.assertEqual(len(result), 3)
        self.assertEqual(
            [point.price for point in result],
            [105, 110, 115],
        )

    def test_three_lower_highs(self):
        points = [
            self.high(0, 150),
            self.high(1, 145),
            self.high(2, 140),
            self.high(3, 135),
        ]

        for point in points:
            self.history.add(point)

        result = self.detector.lower_high_sequence(3)

        self.assertEqual(len(result), 3)
        self.assertEqual(
            [point.price for point in result],
            [145, 140, 135],
        )

    def test_insufficient_higher_lows(self):
        points = [
            self.low(0, 100),
            self.low(1, 105),
            self.low(2, 110),
        ]

        for point in points:
            self.history.add(point)

        result = self.detector.higher_low_sequence(3)

        self.assertEqual(result, [])

    def test_insufficient_lower_highs(self):
        points = [
            self.high(0, 150),
            self.high(1, 145),
            self.high(2, 140),
        ]

        for point in points:
            self.history.add(point)

        result = self.detector.lower_high_sequence(3)

        self.assertEqual(result, [])

    def test_sequence_resets_after_broken_lower_high(self):
        points = [
            self.high(0, 150),
            self.high(1, 145),
            self.high(2, 140),
            self.high(3, 142),
            self.high(4, 135),
            self.high(5, 130),
        ]

        for point in points:
            self.history.add(point)

        result = self.detector.lower_high_sequence(2)

        self.assertEqual(len(result), 2)
        self.assertEqual(
            [point.price for point in result],
            [135, 130],
        )


    def test_sequence_resets_after_broken_lower_high(self):
        points = [
            self.high(0, 150),
            self.high(1, 145),
            self.high(2, 140),
            self.high(3, 142),
            self.high(4, 135),
            self.high(5, 130),
        ]

        for point in points:
            self.history.add(point)

        result = self.detector.lower_high_sequence(2)

        self.assertEqual(len(result), 2)
        self.assertEqual(
            [point.price for point in result],
            [135, 130],
        )


    def test_higher_low_sequence_ignores_highs(self):
        points = [
            self.low(0, 100),
            self.high(1, 150),
            self.low(2, 105),
            self.high(3, 145),
            self.low(4, 110),
        ]

        for point in points:
            self.history.add(point)

        result = self.detector.higher_low_sequence(2)

        self.assertEqual(len(result), 2)
        self.assertEqual(
            [point.price for point in result],
            [105, 110],
        )

    def test_lower_high_sequence_ignores_lows(self):
        points = [
            self.high(0, 150),
            self.low(1, 100),
            self.high(2, 145),
            self.low(3, 105),
            self.high(4, 140),
        ]

        for point in points:
            self.history.add(point)

        result = self.detector.lower_high_sequence(2)

        self.assertEqual(len(result), 2)
        self.assertEqual(
            [point.price for point in result],
            [145, 140],
        )

    def test_zero_minimum_length_is_invalid(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            self.detector.higher_low_sequence(0)

        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            self.detector.lower_high_sequence(0)

    def test_negative_minimum_length_is_invalid(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            self.detector.higher_low_sequence(-1)

        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            self.detector.lower_high_sequence(-1)

import unittest
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.swing import SwingDetector


class TestSwingDetector(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.detector = SwingDetector()

        self.start = datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0)

    def candle(
        self,
        offset: int,
        high: str,
        low: str,
    ) -> Candle:
        return Candle(
            timestamp=self.start + timedelta(minutes=5 * offset),
            open=float(low),
            high=float(high),
            low=float(low),
            close=float(low),
        )

    def test_requires_three_candles(self):
        candles = [
            self.candle(0, "100", "99"),
            self.candle(1, "101", "100"),
        ]

        result = self.detector.detect(candles)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_detects_swing_high(self):
        candles = [
            self.candle(0, "100", "98"),
            self.candle(1, "105", "99"),
            self.candle(2, "102", "100"),
        ]

        result = self.detector.detect(candles)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(result.type, "high")
        self.assertEqual(result.candle.timestamp, candles[1].timestamp)
        self.assertEqual(result.price, Decimal("105"))

    def test_detects_swing_low(self):
        candles = [
            self.candle(0, "105", "100"),
            self.candle(1, "103", "95"),
            self.candle(2, "102", "98"),
        ]

        result = self.detector.detect(candles)

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(result.type, "low")
        self.assertEqual(result.candle.timestamp, candles[1].timestamp)
        self.assertEqual(result.price, Decimal("95"))

    def test_does_not_detect_swing_high(self):
        candles = [
            self.candle(0, "100", "98"),
            self.candle(1, "103", "99"),
            self.candle(2, "105", "100"),
        ]

        result = self.detector.detect(candles)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_does_not_detect_swing_low(self):
        candles = [
            self.candle(0, "105", "100"),
            self.candle(1, "103", "98"),
            self.candle(2, "102", "95"),
        ]

        result = self.detector.detect(candles)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_equal_highs_cannot_form_swing_high(self):
        candles = [
            self.candle(0, "105", "100"),
            self.candle(1, "105", "99"),
            self.candle(2, "103", "100"),
        ]

        result = self.detector.detect(candles)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

    def test_equal_lows_cannot_form_swing_low(self):
        candles = [
            self.candle(0, "105", "100"),
            self.candle(1, "103", "98"),
            self.candle(2, "102", "98"),
        ]

        result = self.detector.detect(candles)

        self.assertIsNone(result)

FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_swing_history.py

import unittest
from datetime import datetime

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.swing import SwingPoint
from strategies.swing_history import SwingHistory


class TestSwingHistory(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.history = SwingHistory()

        self.candle_1 = Candle(
            timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0),
            open=100.0,
            high=105.0,
            low=95.0,
            close=102.0,
        )

        self.candle_2 = Candle(
            timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 5),
            open=102.0,
            high=110.0,
            low=100.0,
            close=108.0,
        )

        self.candle_3 = Candle(
            timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 10),
            open=108.0,
            high=109.0,
            low=97.0,
            close=100.0,
        )

    def high(self, candle, price):
        return SwingPoint(
            type="high",
            candle=candle,
            price=price,
        )

    def low(self, candle, price):
        return SwingPoint(
            type="low",
            candle=candle,
            price=price,
        )

    def test_history_starts_empty(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.history.points, [])
        self.assertEqual(self.history.highs(), [])
        self.assertEqual(self.history.lows(), [])
        self.assertIsNone(self.history.latest_high())
        self.assertIsNone(self.history.latest_low())

    def test_adds_swing_point(self):
        point = self.high(self.candle_1, 105)

        self.history.add(point)

        self.assertEqual(self.history.points, [point])

    def test_returns_highs_only(self):
        high_1 = self.high(self.candle_1, 105)
        low_1 = self.low(self.candle_2, 100)
        high_2 = self.high(self.candle_3, 109)

        self.history.add(high_1)
        self.history.add(low_1)
        self.history.add(high_2)

        self.assertEqual(
            self.history.highs(),
            [high_1, high_2],
        )

    def test_returns_lows_only(self):
        high_1 = self.high(self.candle_1, 105)
        low_1 = self.low(self.candle_2, 100)
        low_2 = self.low(self.candle_3, 97)

        self.history.add(high_1)
        self.history.add(low_1)
        self.history.add(low_2)

        self.assertEqual(
            self.history.lows(),
            [low_1, low_2],
        )

    def test_returns_latest_high(self):
        high_1 = self.high(self.candle_1, 105)
        high_2 = self.high(self.candle_2, 110)

        self.history.add(high_1)
        self.history.add(high_2)

        self.assertEqual(
            self.history.latest_high(),
            high_2,
        )

    def test_returns_latest_low(self):
        low_1 = self.low(self.candle_1, 95)
        low_2 = self.low(self.candle_2, 100)

        self.history.add(low_1)
        self.history.add(low_2)

        self.assertEqual(
            self.history.latest_low(),
            low_2,
        )

    def test_points_are_returned_as_a_copy(self):
        point = self.high(self.candle_1, 105)

        self.history.add(point)

        points = self.history.points
        points.clear()

        self.assertEqual(
            self.history.points,
            [point],
        )

FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_swing_processor.py

import unittest
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal

from models.candle import Candle
from strategies.swing_processor import SwingProcessor


class TestSwingProcessor(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.processor = SwingProcessor()
        self.start = datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0)

    def candle(
        self,
        offset: int,
        high: str,
        low: str,
    ) -> Candle:
        return Candle(
            timestamp=self.start + timedelta(minutes=5 * offset),
            open=float(low),
            high=float(high),
            low=float(low),
            close=float(low),
        )

    def test_waits_for_three_candles(self):
        result = self.processor.process(
            self.candle(0, "100", "98")
        )

        self.assertIsNone(result)

        result = self.processor.process(
            self.candle(1, "105", "99")
        )

        self.assertIsNone(result)

        self.assertEqual(
            self.processor.history.points,
            [],
        )

    def test_confirms_middle_candle_as_swing_high(self):
        self.processor.process(
            self.candle(0, "100", "98")
        )
        self.processor.process(
            self.candle(1, "105", "99")
        )

        result = self.processor.process(
            self.candle(2, "102", "100")
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(result.type, "high")
        self.assertEqual(
            result.candle.timestamp,
            self.start + timedelta(minutes=5),
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            result.price,
            Decimal("105"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            self.processor.history.points,
            [result],
        )

    def test_confirms_middle_candle_as_swing_low(self):
        self.processor.process(
            self.candle(0, "105", "100")
        )
        self.processor.process(
            self.candle(1, "103", "95")
        )

        result = self.processor.process(
            self.candle(2, "102", "98")
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(result.type, "low")
        self.assertEqual(
            result.candle.timestamp,
            self.start + timedelta(minutes=5),
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            result.price,
            Decimal("95"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            self.processor.history.points,
            [result],
        )

    def test_non_swing_does_not_modify_history(self):
        candles = [
            self.candle(0, "100", "98"),
            self.candle(1, "103", "99"),
            self.candle(2, "105", "100"),
        ]

        for candle in candles:
            result = self.processor.process(candle)

        self.assertIsNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(
            self.processor.history.points,
            [],
        )

    def test_processes_multiple_confirmed_swings(self):
        candles = [
            self.candle(0, "100", "98"),
            self.candle(1, "105", "99"),
            self.candle(2, "102", "100"),
            self.candle(3, "101", "97"),
            self.candle(4, "103", "99"),
        ]

        results = []

        for candle in candles:
            result = self.processor.process(candle)

            if result is not None:
                results.append(result)

        self.assertEqual(len(results), 2)

        self.assertEqual(
            results[0].type,
            "high",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            results[0].price,
            Decimal("105"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            results[1].type,
            "low",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            results[1].price,
            Decimal("97"),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            self.processor.history.points,
            results,
        )

    def test_confirmed_swing_is_one_candle_behind_current_candle(self):
        self.processor.process(
            self.candle(0, "100", "98")
        )
        self.processor.process(
            self.candle(1, "105", "99")
        )

        result = self.processor.process(
            self.candle(2, "102", "100")
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)

        self.assertEqual(
            result.candle.timestamp,
            self.start + timedelta(minutes=5),
        )

        self.assertNotEqual(
            result.candle.timestamp,
            self.start + timedelta(minutes=10),
        )



================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-gpt\tests\test_timeframe.py

import unittest
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from models.candle import Candle
from models.timeframe import M15Builder


def make_candle(
    hour: int,
    minute: int,
    open_: float,
    high: float,
    low: float,
    close: float,
) -> Candle:
    return Candle(
        timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, hour, minute),
        open=open_,
        high=high,
        low=low,
        close=close,
    )


class TestM15Builder(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_aligned_candles_create_m15_candle(self):
        builder = M15Builder()

        self.assertIsNone(
            builder.update(
                make_candle(9, 0, 100, 105, 99, 103)
            )
        )

        self.assertIsNone(
            builder.update(
                make_candle(9, 5, 103, 108, 102, 107)
            )
        )

        result = builder.update(
            make_candle(9, 10, 107, 110, 104, 109)
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)

    def test_misaligned_candles_are_ignored_until_next_boundary(self):
        builder = M15Builder()

        self.assertIsNone(
            builder.update(
                make_candle(9, 5, 100, 105, 99, 103)
            )
        )

        self.assertIsNone(
            builder.update(
                make_candle(9, 10, 103, 108, 102, 107)
            )
        )

        # 09:15 is the first valid M15 starting point.
        self.assertIsNone(
            builder.update(
                make_candle(9, 15, 107, 110, 104, 109)
            )
        )

        self.assertIsNone(
            builder.update(
                make_candle(9, 20, 109, 112, 108, 111)
            )
        )

        result = builder.update(
            make_candle(9, 25, 111, 115, 110, 114)
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(
            result.timestamp,
            datetime(2026, 1, 1, 9, 15),
        )

    def test_m15_ohlc_is_aggregated_correctly(self):
        builder = M15Builder()

        builder.update(
            make_candle(9, 0, 100, 105, 99, 103)
        )
        builder.update(
            make_candle(9, 5, 103, 108, 102, 107)
        )

        result = builder.update(
            make_candle(9, 10, 107, 110, 104, 109)
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            result.timestamp,
            datetime(2026, 1, 1, 9, 0),
        )
        self.assertEqual(result.open, 100)
        self.assertEqual(result.high, 110)
        self.assertEqual(result.low, 99)
        self.assertEqual(result.close, 109)

    def test_gap_invalidates_partial_group(self):
        builder = M15Builder()

        # Start valid group.
        builder.update(
            make_candle(9, 15, 100, 105, 99, 103)
        )

        builder.update(
            make_candle(9, 20, 103, 108, 102, 107)
        )

        # 09:25 is missing. 09:30 arrives instead.
        self.assertIsNone(
            builder.update(
                make_candle(9, 30, 107, 110, 104, 109)
            )
        )

        # The previous partial group must not be completed.
        self.assertEqual(len(builder.candles), 0)

    def test_after_gap_next_aligned_candle_can_start_new_group(self):
        builder = M15Builder()

        builder.update(
            make_candle(9, 15, 100, 105, 99, 103)
        )

        builder.update(
            make_candle(9, 20, 103, 108, 102, 107)
        )

        # Gap: 09:25 missing.
        builder.update(
            make_candle(9, 30, 107, 110, 104, 109)
        )

        # 09:30 starts a fresh group.
        builder.update(
            make_candle(9, 35, 109, 112, 108, 111)
        )

        result = builder.update(
            make_candle(9, 40, 111, 115, 110, 114)
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)
        self.assertEqual(
            result.timestamp,
            datetime(2026, 1, 1, 9, 30),
        )

    def test_invalid_spacing_does_not_create_m15_candle(self):
        builder = M15Builder()

        builder.update(
            make_candle(9, 0, 100, 105, 99, 103)
        )

        # 09:10 is not the expected second candle.
        self.assertIsNone(
            builder.update(
                make_candle(9, 10, 103, 108, 102, 107)
            )
        )

        self.assertEqual(len(builder.candles), 0)

    def test_hour_boundary_is_valid(self):
        builder = M15Builder()

        builder.update(
            make_candle(9, 45, 100, 105, 99, 103)
        )

        builder.update(
            make_candle(9, 50, 103, 108, 102, 107)
        )

        result = builder.update(
            make_candle(9, 55, 107, 110, 104, 109)
        )

        self.assertIsNotNone(result)

        self.assertEqual(
            result.timestamp,
            datetime(2026, 1, 1, 9, 45),
        )

    def test_m15_history_is_limited_to_max_history(self):
        builder = M15Builder(max_history=2)

        start = datetime(2026, 1, 1, 9, 0)

        for block in range(3):
            for offset in range(3):
                timestamp = start + timedelta(
                    minutes=15 * block + 5 * offset
                )

                builder.update(
                    Candle(
                        timestamp=timestamp,
                        open=100 + block,
                        high=105 + block,
                        low=95 + block,
                        close=102 + block,
                    )
                )

        self.assertEqual(len(builder.candles), 2)

        self.assertEqual(
            builder.candles[0].timestamp,
            datetime(2026, 1, 1, 9, 15),
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            builder.candles[1].timestamp,
            datetime(2026, 1, 1, 9, 30),
        )
