import csv
import math
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from collections import defaultdict
from bisect import bisect_right

# ============================================================
# SP2L EURUSD M1 BACKTESTER
# ============================================================
#
# CSV format (NO HEADER):
#
# date<TAB>time<TAB>open<TAB>high<TAB>low<TAB>close<TAB>volume
#
# Example:
# 2026.01.05    10:00:00    1.17000    1.17020    1.16980    1.17010    123
#
# Put EURUSD1.csv in the same folder as this Python file.
#
# ============================================================


# ============================================================
# USER SETTINGS
# ============================================================

SYMBOL = "EURUSD"

if SYMBOL == "EURUSD":

    CSV_FILE = "EURUSD1.csv"

    CONTRACT_SIZE = 100_000

    PRICE_STEP = 0.00001

    # 30 pipets = 0.00030
    MIN_SL_DISTANCE = 30 * PRICE_STEP

elif SYMBOL == "XAUUSD":

    CSV_FILE = "XAUUSD1.csv"

    # 1 lot = 100 oz
    CONTRACT_SIZE = 100

    PRICE_STEP = 0.01

    # 30 points = $0.30
    MIN_SL_DISTANCE = 30 * PRICE_STEP

else:

    raise ValueError(
        f"Unsupported symbol: {SYMBOL}"
    )


STARTING_BALANCE = 2500.0

RISK_PERCENT = 1.0

LEVERAGE = 2000

# Minimum SL distance in pipets
MIN_SL_PIPETS = 30

# Maximum SL distance:
# 50% of ADR
ADR_PERIOD = 20
MAX_SL_ADR_PERCENT = 50.0

# Trading windows
TRADING_WINDOWS = [
    ("10:00", "12:30"),
    ("15:30", "16:30"),
]

# ============================================================
# LOSS LIMITS
# ============================================================

# Maximum number of consecutive losing trades allowed
# in each trading window.
#
# Example:
# 2 means after 2 consecutive losses in that window,
# no more trades will be taken in that window.
#
# Set to None to disable a limit.

MAX_CONSECUTIVE_LOSSES_WINDOW_1 = 10
MAX_CONSECUTIVE_LOSSES_WINDOW_2 = 10

# Maximum total number of losing trades allowed per day.
#
# Example:
# 3 means once 3 losing trades have occurred that day,
# no more trades will be taken for the rest of that day.
#
# Set to None to disable the daily limit.

MAX_LOSSES_PER_DAY = 15

# TP levels
TP1_R = 1.0
TP2_R = 2.0
TP3_R = 3.0

# Take half at TP2
TP2_CLOSE_PERCENT = 0.50

# Costs
# Set these to zero initially.
# They can be changed later.
SPREAD_PIPETS = 1
COMMISSION_PER_LOT = 8.0


# If True, print every trade
PRINT_TRADES = True

# If True, print detected setups that were rejected
PRINT_REJECTED_SETUPS = False

MIN_LOTS_TO_OPEN = 0.02
BROKER_MIN_LOTS = 0.01
BROKER_LOT_STEP = 0.01

# ============================================================
# DATA STRUCTURE
# ============================================================

class Candle:
    def __init__(self, dt, open_price, high, low, close, volume):
        self.dt = dt
        self.open = open_price
        self.high = high
        self.low = low
        self.close = close
        self.volume = volume

    def __repr__(self):
        return (
            f"Candle({self.dt}, "
            f"O={self.open}, H={self.high}, "
            f"L={self.low}, C={self.close})"
        )


class Trade:
    def __init__(
        self,
        direction,
        setup_time,
        entry_time,
        entry,
        sl,
        tp1,
        tp2,
        tp3,
        risk_amount,
        lots,
        c1_time,
        c2_time,
        c3_time,
    ):
        self.direction = direction
        self.setup_time = setup_time
        self.entry_time = entry_time

        self.entry = entry
        self.sl = sl
        self.tp1 = tp1
        self.tp2 = tp2
        self.tp3 = tp3

        self.risk_amount = risk_amount
        self.lots = lots

        self.c1_time = c1_time
        self.c2_time = c2_time
        self.c3_time = c3_time

        self.tp1_hit = False
        self.tp2_hit = False
        self.sl_hit = False

        self.exit_time = None
        self.exit_price = None

        self.pnl = 0.0
        self.pnl_r = 0.0

        self.close_reason = None


# ============================================================
# LOAD CSV
# ============================================================

def load_data(filename):

    candles = []

    with open(filename, "r", newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:

        reader = csv.reader(f, delimiter=",")

        for row_number, row in enumerate(reader, start=1):

            if not row:
                continue

            if len(row) < 7:
                print(f"Skipping malformed row {row_number}: {row}")
                continue

            date_str = row[0].strip()
            time_str = row[1].strip()

            try:
                dt = parse_datetime(date_str, time_str)

                open_price = float(row[2])
                high = float(row[3])
                low = float(row[4])
                close = float(row[5])
                volume = float(row[6])

            except ValueError as e:
                print(f"Skipping row {row_number}: {e}")
                continue

            candles.append(
                Candle(
                    dt,
                    open_price,
                    high,
                    low,
                    close,
                    volume
                )
            )

    candles.sort(key=lambda x: x.dt)

    return candles


def parse_datetime(date_str, time_str):

    formats = [
        "%Y.%m.%d %H:%M:%S",
        "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S",
        "%Y/%m/%d %H:%M:%S",

        "%Y.%m.%d %H:%M",
        "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M",
        "%Y/%m/%d %H:%M",
    ]

    value = f"{date_str} {time_str}"

    for fmt in formats:
        try:
            return datetime.strptime(value, fmt)
        except ValueError:
            pass

    raise ValueError(
        f"Unknown date/time format: {date_str} {time_str}"
    )


# ============================================================
# PIPET DETECTION
# ============================================================

def detect_pipet_size(candles):

    prices = []

    for c in candles:
        prices.extend([
            c.open,
            c.high,
            c.low,
            c.close
        ])

    decimals = 0

    for price in prices[:10000]:

        text = f"{price:.10f}".rstrip("0")

        if "." in text:
            decimal_count = len(text.split(".")[1])
            decimals = max(decimals, decimal_count)

    if decimals <= 2:
        return 0.01

    if decimals == 3:
        return 0.001

    if decimals == 4:
        return 0.0001

    return 0.00001


# ============================================================
# TRADING WINDOW
# ============================================================

def time_to_minutes(t):

    return t.hour * 60 + t.minute


def is_in_trading_window(dt):

    current = time_to_minutes(dt.time())

    for start_str, end_str in TRADING_WINDOWS:

        start_h, start_m = map(int, start_str.split(":"))
        end_h, end_m = map(int, end_str.split(":"))

        start = start_h * 60 + start_m
        end = end_h * 60 + end_m

        if start <= current <= end:
            return True

    return False

def get_trading_window_index(dt):
    """
    Return the index of the trading window containing dt.

    Returns:
        0 -> first trading window
        1 -> second trading window
        None -> outside all trading windows
    """

    current = time_to_minutes(dt.time())

    for index, (start_str, end_str) in enumerate(TRADING_WINDOWS):

        start_h, start_m = map(int, start_str.split(":"))
        end_h, end_m = map(int, end_str.split(":"))

        start = start_h * 60 + start_m
        end = end_h * 60 + end_m

        if start <= current <= end:
            return index

    return None

# ============================================================
# DAILY DATA / ADR
# ============================================================

def calculate_daily_ranges(candles):

    daily = defaultdict(list)

    for c in candles:
        daily[c.dt.date()].append(c)

    daily_ranges = {}

    for date, day_candles in daily.items():

        high = max(c.high for c in day_candles)
        low = min(c.low for c in day_candles)

        daily_ranges[date] = high - low

    return daily_ranges


def calculate_adr_for_date(current_date, daily_ranges):

    dates = sorted(
        d for d in daily_ranges.keys()
        if d < current_date
    )

    if len(dates) < ADR_PERIOD:
        return None

    previous_dates = dates[-ADR_PERIOD:]

    ranges = [
        daily_ranges[d]
        for d in previous_dates
    ]

    return sum(ranges) / len(ranges)


# ============================================================
# POSITION SIZE
# ============================================================

def calculate_position_size(
    balance,
    entry,
    sl,
    pipet_size
):

    target_risk = balance * (
        RISK_PERCENT / 100.0
    )

    sl_distance = abs(entry - sl)

    if sl_distance <= 0:
        return 0.0, 0.0

    # Position size required to risk exactly 1%.
    lots_by_risk = target_risk / (
        sl_distance * CONTRACT_SIZE
    )

    # Maximum position size allowed by account leverage.
    if LEVERAGE <= 0:
        return 0.0, 0.0

    lots_by_margin = (
        balance * LEVERAGE
    ) / (
        entry * CONTRACT_SIZE
    )

#    lots = min(
#        lots_by_risk,
#        lots_by_margin
#    )

    lots = min(
        round(lots_by_risk, 2),
        round(lots_by_margin, 2)
    )

    # Actual monetary risk at the selected position size.
    actual_risk = (
        sl_distance *
        CONTRACT_SIZE *
        lots
    )

    return lots, actual_risk


# ============================================================
# SPREAD
# ============================================================

def apply_spread(entry, direction, pipet_size):

    spread = SPREAD_PIPETS * pipet_size

    if direction == "BUY":
        return entry + spread

    return entry - spread


# ============================================================
# SP2L DETECTION
# ============================================================

def detect_bullish_setup(c1, c2, c3):

    # C2 must break C1 high and close above it.
    if c2.high <= c1.high:
        return False

    if c2.close <= c1.high:
        return False

    # C3 must remain above C1 high.
    if c3.low <= c1.high:
        return False

    return True


def detect_bearish_setup(c1, c2, c3):

    # C2 must break C1 low and close below it.
    if c2.low >= c1.low:
        return False

    if c2.close >= c1.low:
        return False

    # C3 must remain below C1 low.
    if c3.high >= c1.low:
        return False

    return True


# ============================================================
# ENTRY SEARCH
# ============================================================

def find_bullish_entry(
    candles,
    start_index,
    c1,
    c2,
    c3,
    max_sl_distance
):

    previous_low = c3.low

    for i in range(start_index, len(candles)):

        candle = candles[i]

        # If the setup moves into a different trading day,
        # don't keep the old setup alive indefinitely.
        if candle.dt.date() != c3.dt.date():
            return None

        # If outside trading window, no entry.
        if not is_in_trading_window(candle.dt):
            continue

        # Entry occurs when price comes below previous
        # candle's low.
        if candle.low < previous_low:

            entry = previous_low
            sl = c1.low

            distance = entry - sl

            # Must be positive.
            if distance <= 0:
                return None

            # Minimum SL.
            min_distance = MIN_SL_PIPETS * PIPET_SIZE

            if distance < min_distance:

                if PRINT_REJECTED_SETUPS:
                    print(
                        "Rejected bullish setup: "
                        f"SL too small ({distance / PIPET_SIZE:.1f} pipets)"
                    )

                return None

            # Maximum SL.
            if distance > max_sl_distance:

                if PRINT_REJECTED_SETUPS:
                    print(
                        "Rejected bullish setup: "
                        f"SL too large ({distance / PIPET_SIZE:.1f} pipets)"
                    )

                return None

            return i, entry, sl

        # The trigger moves to this candle's low.
        previous_low = candle.low

    return None


def find_bearish_entry(
    candles,
    start_index,
    c1,
    c2,
    c3,
    max_sl_distance
):

    previous_high = c3.high

    for i in range(start_index, len(candles)):

        candle = candles[i]

        if candle.dt.date() != c3.dt.date():
            return None

        if not is_in_trading_window(candle.dt):
            continue

        # Entry when price trades above previous candle high.
        if candle.high > previous_high:

            entry = previous_high
            sl = c1.high

            distance = sl - entry

            if distance <= 0:
                return None

            min_distance = MIN_SL_PIPETS * PIPET_SIZE

            if distance < min_distance:

                if PRINT_REJECTED_SETUPS:
                    print(
                        "Rejected bearish setup: "
                        f"SL too small ({distance / PIPET_SIZE:.1f} pipets)"
                    )

                return None

            if distance > max_sl_distance:

                if PRINT_REJECTED_SETUPS:
                    print(
                        "Rejected bearish setup: "
                        f"SL too large ({distance / PIPET_SIZE:.1f} pipets)"
                    )

                return None

            return i, entry, sl

        previous_high = candle.high

    return None


# ============================================================
# TRADE SIMULATION
# ============================================================

def simulate_trade(
    trade,
    candles,
    entry_index,
    balance
):

    remaining_fraction = 1.0

    entry = trade.entry

    sl = trade.sl
    tp1 = trade.tp1
    tp2 = trade.tp2
    tp3 = trade.tp3

    risk = trade.risk_amount

    # We begin with 100% of the position.
    #
    # TP1:
    #     Reference only. No profit is taken.
    #
    # TP2:
    #     Close 50%.
    #
    # TP3:
    #     Close remaining 50%.

    for i in range(entry_index, len(candles)):

        candle = candles[i]

        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # END OF DAY
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        if candle.dt.date() != trade.entry_time.date():

            trade.exit_time = candles[i - 1].dt
            trade.exit_price = candles[i - 1].close
            trade.close_reason = "END_OF_DAY"

            pnl = calculate_pnl(
                trade.direction,
                entry,
                trade.exit_price,
                trade.lots,
                remaining_fraction
            )

            trade.pnl += pnl

            remaining_fraction = 0.0

            break

        # ====================================================
        # BUY
        # ====================================================

        if trade.direction == "BUY":

            # ------------------------------------------------
            # SL
            #
            # SL gets priority over TP levels if both are
            # touched in the same candle.
            # ------------------------------------------------

            if candle.low <= sl:

                trade.sl_hit = True
                trade.exit_time = candle.dt
                trade.exit_price = sl
                trade.close_reason = "SL"

                pnl = calculate_pnl(
                    "BUY",
                    entry,
                    sl,
                    trade.lots,
                    remaining_fraction
                )

                trade.pnl += pnl

                remaining_fraction = 0.0

                break

            # ------------------------------------------------
            # TP1
            #
            # TP1 is now ONLY a reference.
            # We do NOT close anything here.
            # ------------------------------------------------

            if not trade.tp1_hit and candle.high >= tp1:

                trade.tp1_hit = True

            # ------------------------------------------------
            # TP2
            #
            # Close 50%.
            # ------------------------------------------------

            if (
                not trade.tp2_hit
                and candle.high >= tp2
            ):

                trade.tp2_hit = True

                pnl = calculate_pnl(
                    "BUY",
                    entry,
                    tp2,
                    trade.lots,
                    TP2_CLOSE_PERCENT
                )

                trade.pnl += pnl

                remaining_fraction -= TP2_CLOSE_PERCENT

            # ------------------------------------------------
            # TP3
            #
            # Close whatever remains.
            # ------------------------------------------------

            if (
                trade.tp2_hit
                and candle.high >= tp3
            ):

                pnl = calculate_pnl(
                    "BUY",
                    entry,
                    tp3,
                    trade.lots,
                    remaining_fraction
                )

                trade.pnl += pnl

                remaining_fraction = 0.0

                trade.exit_time = candle.dt
                trade.exit_price = tp3
                trade.close_reason = "TP3"

                break

        # ====================================================
        # SELL
        # ====================================================

        else:

            # ------------------------------------------------
            # SL
            # ------------------------------------------------

            if candle.high >= sl:

                trade.sl_hit = True
                trade.exit_time = candle.dt
                trade.exit_price = sl
                trade.close_reason = "SL"

                pnl = calculate_pnl(
                    "SELL",
                    entry,
                    sl,
                    trade.lots,
                    remaining_fraction
                )

                trade.pnl += pnl

                remaining_fraction = 0.0

                break

            # ------------------------------------------------
            # TP1
            #
            # Reference only.
            # ------------------------------------------------

            if (
                not trade.tp1_hit
                and candle.low <= tp1
            ):

                trade.tp1_hit = True

            # ------------------------------------------------
            # TP2
            #
            # Close 50%.
            # ------------------------------------------------

            if (
                not trade.tp2_hit
                and candle.low <= tp2
            ):

                trade.tp2_hit = True

                pnl = calculate_pnl(
                    "SELL",
                    entry,
                    tp2,
                    trade.lots,
                    TP2_CLOSE_PERCENT
                )

                trade.pnl += pnl

                remaining_fraction -= TP2_CLOSE_PERCENT

            # ------------------------------------------------
            # TP3
            #
            # Close remaining position.
            # ------------------------------------------------

            if (
                trade.tp2_hit
                and candle.low <= tp3
            ):

                pnl = calculate_pnl(
                    "SELL",
                    entry,
                    tp3,
                    trade.lots,
                    remaining_fraction
                )

                trade.pnl += pnl

                remaining_fraction = 0.0

                trade.exit_time = candle.dt
                trade.exit_price = tp3
                trade.close_reason = "TP3"

                break

    else:

        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # END OF DATA
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        last_candle = candles[-1]

        trade.exit_time = last_candle.dt
        trade.exit_price = last_candle.close
        trade.close_reason = "END_OF_DATA"

        pnl = calculate_pnl(
            trade.direction,
            entry,
            trade.exit_price,
            trade.lots,
            remaining_fraction
        )

        trade.pnl += pnl

        remaining_fraction = 0.0

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # COMMISSION
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    commission = (
        trade.lots *
        COMMISSION_PER_LOT
    )

    trade.pnl -= commission

    # Calculate R AFTER commission so the reported R
    # corresponds to actual net P&L.
    if trade.risk_amount > 0:
        trade.pnl_r = (
            trade.pnl /
            trade.risk_amount
        )
    else:
        trade.pnl_r = 0.0

    return trade


def calculate_pnl(
    direction,
    entry,
    exit_price,
    lots,
    fraction
):

    if direction == "BUY":
        price_change = exit_price - entry

    else:
        price_change = entry - exit_price

    return (
        price_change *
        CONTRACT_SIZE *
        lots *
        fraction
    )





def aggregate_to_m5(candles):
    """
    Convert M1 candles into M5 candles.
    """

    m5 = []

    current_bucket = None
    bucket_candles = []

    for candle in candles:

        # Floor minute to nearest 5-minute boundary
        bucket = candle.dt.replace(
            minute=(candle.dt.minute // 5) * 5,
            second=0,
            microsecond=0
        )

        if current_bucket is None:
            current_bucket = bucket

        if bucket != current_bucket:

            if bucket_candles:
                m5.append(
                    Candle(
                        current_bucket,
                        bucket_candles[0].open,
                        max(c.high for c in bucket_candles),
                        min(c.low for c in bucket_candles),
                        bucket_candles[-1].close,
                        sum(c.volume for c in bucket_candles)
                    )
                )

            current_bucket = bucket
            bucket_candles = []

        bucket_candles.append(candle)

    # Last bucket
    if bucket_candles:

        m5.append(
            Candle(
                current_bucket,
                bucket_candles[0].open,
                max(c.high for c in bucket_candles),
                min(c.low for c in bucket_candles),
                bucket_candles[-1].close,
                sum(c.volume for c in bucket_candles)
            )
        )

    return m5


def calculate_ema(values, period):

    if len(values) < period:
        return [None] * len(values)

    ema = [None] * len(values)

    # Initial EMA = SMA
    initial_sma = sum(values[:period]) / period
    ema[period - 1] = initial_sma

    multiplier = 2 / (period + 1)

    for i in range(period, len(values)):

        ema[i] = (
            (values[i] - ema[i - 1]) *
            multiplier
            + ema[i - 1]
        )

    return ema


def build_m5_trend_data(candles):

    m5 = aggregate_to_m5(candles)

    closes = [
        candle.close
        for candle in m5
    ]

    ema20 = calculate_ema(
        closes,
        20
    )

    trend_data = []

    for i, (candle, ema) in enumerate(
        zip(m5, ema20)
    ):

        if i == 0:
            previous_ema = None
        else:
            previous_ema = ema20[i - 1]

        trend_data.append(
            (
                candle.dt,
                candle.close,
                ema,
                previous_ema
            )
        )

    return trend_data


def get_m5_trend(entry_time, trend_data):
    """
    Determine M5 trend using the most recently COMPLETED
    M5 candle before entry_time.

    Trend requires BOTH:
        BUY:
            M5 close > EMA20
            EMA20 rising

        SELL:
            M5 close < EMA20
            EMA20 falling

    Returns:
        'BULLISH'
        'BEARISH'
        None
    """

    previous = None

    for dt, close, ema20, previous_ema20 in trend_data:

        if dt >= entry_time:
            break

        if ema20 is None or previous_ema20 is None:
            continue

        previous = (
            close,
            ema20,
            previous_ema20
        )

    if previous is None:
        return None

    close, ema20, previous_ema20 = previous

    if (
        close > ema20
        and ema20 > previous_ema20
    ):
        return "BULLISH"

    if (
        close < ema20
        and ema20 < previous_ema20
    ):
        return "BEARISH"

    return None


def get_m5_trend_at_c3( c3_time, trend_data ):

    return get_m5_trend(
        c3_time + timedelta(minutes=5),
        trend_data
    )

# ============================================================
# MAIN BACKTEST
# ============================================================

def run_backtest(candles):

    global PIPET_SIZE

    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("SP2L BACKTEST")
    print("=" * 70)

    print(f"Candles: {len(candles):,}")
    print(f"Start:   {candles[0].dt}")
    print(f"End:     {candles[-1].dt}")

    daily_ranges = calculate_daily_ranges(candles)

    # Build M5 EMA20 trend data
    trend_data = build_m5_trend_data(candles)

    balance = STARTING_BALANCE
    equity_curve = []

    trades = []

    rejected = 0

    # ========================================================
    # LOSS LIMIT TRACKING
    # ========================================================

    # Consecutive losses in each trading window.
    consecutive_losses = [0] * len(TRADING_WINDOWS)

    # Total losing trades for the current day.
    daily_losses = 0

    # Date currently being tracked.
    current_loss_date = None

    # We use a loop that advances past the trade after one is found.
    i = 0

    while i < len(candles) - 3:

        c1 = candles[i]
        c2 = candles[i + 1]
        c3 = candles[i + 2]

        # ====================================================
        # NEW DAY
        # ====================================================

        if current_loss_date != c1.dt.date():

            current_loss_date = c1.dt.date()

            daily_losses = 0

            # Consecutive-loss limits are per window,
            # so they start fresh each day as well.
            consecutive_losses = [0] * len(TRADING_WINDOWS)

        # Setup must be formed inside a trading window.
        #
        # We require the third candle to be in the window as well.
        if not (
            is_in_trading_window(c1.dt)
            and is_in_trading_window(c2.dt)
            and is_in_trading_window(c3.dt)
        ):
            i += 1
            continue

        # ====================================================
        # DAILY LOSS LIMIT
        # ====================================================

        if (
            MAX_LOSSES_PER_DAY is not None
            and daily_losses >= MAX_LOSSES_PER_DAY
        ):

            i += 1
            continue

        adr = calculate_adr_for_date(
            c3.dt.date(),
            daily_ranges
        )

        if adr is None:
            i += 1
            continue

        max_sl_distance = (
            adr *
            MAX_SL_ADR_PERCENT /
            100.0
        )

        # ====================================================
        # BULLISH
        # ====================================================

        if detect_bullish_setup(c1, c2, c3):

            result = find_bullish_entry(
                candles,
                i + 3,
                c1,
                c2,
                c3,
                max_sl_distance
            )

            if result is not None:

                entry_index, raw_entry, sl = result

                # ========================================================
                # WINDOW LOSS LIMIT
                # ========================================================

                entry_window = get_trading_window_index(
                    candles[entry_index].dt
                )

                if entry_window is None:
                    rejected += 1
                    i += 1
                    continue

                if entry_window == 0:
                    max_consecutive_losses = (
                        MAX_CONSECUTIVE_LOSSES_WINDOW_1
                    )

                elif entry_window == 1:
                    max_consecutive_losses = (
                        MAX_CONSECUTIVE_LOSSES_WINDOW_2
                    )

                else:
                    max_consecutive_losses = None

                if (
                    max_consecutive_losses is not None
                    and consecutive_losses[entry_window]
                    >= max_consecutive_losses
                ):

                    rejected += 1
                    i += 1
                    continue

                # --------------------------------------------------------
                # M5 TREND FILTER
                #
                # Trend must be bullish when C3 completes AND
                # still bullish when the M1 entry occurs.
                # --------------------------------------------------------

                trend_at_c3 = get_m5_trend_at_c3(
                    c3.dt,
                    trend_data
                )

                trend_at_entry = get_m5_trend(
                    candles[entry_index].dt,
                    trend_data
                )

                if (
                        trend_at_c3 != "BULLISH"
                        or trend_at_entry != "BULLISH"
                ):
                    rejected += 1
                    i += 1
                    continue


                entry = apply_spread(
                    raw_entry,
                    "BUY",
                    PIPET_SIZE
                )

                lots, risk_amount = calculate_position_size(
                    balance,
                    entry,
                    sl,
                    PIPET_SIZE
                )

                if lots >= MIN_LOTS_TO_OPEN:

                    distance = entry - sl

                    tp1 = entry + distance * TP1_R
                    tp2 = entry + distance * TP2_R
                    tp3 = entry + distance * TP3_R

                    trade = Trade(
                        "BUY",
                        c3.dt,
                        candles[entry_index].dt,
                        entry,
                        sl,
                        tp1,
                        tp2,
                        tp3,
                        risk_amount,
                        lots,
                        c1.dt,
                        c2.dt,
                        c3.dt
                    )

                    trade = simulate_trade(
                        trade,
                        candles,
                        entry_index,
                        balance
                    )

                    balance += trade.pnl

                    trades.append(trade)

                    # ====================================================
                    # UPDATE LOSS COUNTERS
                    # ====================================================

                    trade_window = get_trading_window_index(
                        trade.entry_time
                    )

                    if trade.pnl < 0:

                        daily_losses += 1

                        if trade_window is not None:
                            consecutive_losses[trade_window] += 1

                    else:

                        if trade_window is not None:
                            consecutive_losses[trade_window] = 0

                    equity_curve.append(
                        (trade.exit_time, balance)
                    )

                    if PRINT_TRADES:
                        print_trade(
                            len(trades),
                            trade,
                            balance
                        )

                    # Don't allow overlapping trades.
                    i = entry_index + 1
                    continue

                else:
                    print(f"Trade rejected: {lots:.4f} lots")
                    rejected += 1

        # ====================================================
        # BEARISH
        # ====================================================

        if detect_bearish_setup(c1, c2, c3):

            result = find_bearish_entry(
                candles,
                i + 3,
                c1,
                c2,
                c3,
                max_sl_distance
            )

            if result is not None:

                entry_index, raw_entry, sl = result

                # ========================================================
                # WINDOW LOSS LIMIT
                # ========================================================

                entry_window = get_trading_window_index(
                    candles[entry_index].dt
                )

                if entry_window is None:
                    rejected += 1
                    i += 1
                    continue

                if entry_window == 0:
                    max_consecutive_losses = (
                        MAX_CONSECUTIVE_LOSSES_WINDOW_1
                    )

                elif entry_window == 1:
                    max_consecutive_losses = (
                        MAX_CONSECUTIVE_LOSSES_WINDOW_2
                    )

                else:
                    max_consecutive_losses = None

                if (
                    max_consecutive_losses is not None
                    and consecutive_losses[entry_window]
                    >= max_consecutive_losses
                ):

                    rejected += 1
                    i += 1
                    continue

                # --------------------------------------------------------
                # M5 TREND FILTER
                #
                # Trend must be bearish when C3 completes AND
                # still bearish when the M1 entry occurs.
                # --------------------------------------------------------

                trend_at_c3 = get_m5_trend_at_c3(
                    c3.dt,
                    trend_data
                )

                trend_at_entry = get_m5_trend(
                    candles[entry_index].dt,
                    trend_data
                )

                if (
                        trend_at_c3 != "BEARISH"
                        or trend_at_entry != "BEARISH"
                ):
                    rejected += 1
                    i += 1
                    continue

                entry = apply_spread(
                    raw_entry,
                    "SELL",
                    PIPET_SIZE
                )

                lots, risk_amount = calculate_position_size(
                    balance,
                    entry,
                    sl,
                    PIPET_SIZE
                )

                if lots >= MIN_LOTS_TO_OPEN:

                    distance = sl - entry

                    tp1 = entry - distance * TP1_R
                    tp2 = entry - distance * TP2_R
                    tp3 = entry - distance * TP3_R

                    trade = Trade(
                        "SELL",
                        c3.dt,
                        candles[entry_index].dt,
                        entry,
                        sl,
                        tp1,
                        tp2,
                        tp3,
                        risk_amount,
                        lots,
                        c1.dt,
                        c2.dt,
                        c3.dt
                    )

                    trade = simulate_trade(
                        trade,
                        candles,
                        entry_index,
                        balance
                    )

                    balance += trade.pnl

                    trades.append(trade)

                    # ====================================================
                    # UPDATE LOSS COUNTERS
                    # ====================================================

                    trade_window = get_trading_window_index(
                        trade.entry_time
                    )

                    if trade.pnl < 0:

                        # Daily losing trade count
                        daily_losses += 1

                        # Consecutive losing trades in this window
                        if trade_window is not None:
                            consecutive_losses[trade_window] += 1

                    else:

                        # A non-losing trade breaks the consecutive
                        # losing-trade sequence for its window.
                        if trade_window is not None:
                            consecutive_losses[trade_window] = 0

                    equity_curve.append(
                        (trade.exit_time, balance)
                    )

                    if PRINT_TRADES:
                        print_trade(
                            len(trades),
                            trade,
                            balance
                        )

                    i = entry_index + 1
                    continue

                else:
                    print(f"Trade rejected: {lots:.4f} lots")
                    rejected += 1

        i += 1

    return trades, equity_curve, balance


# ============================================================
# TRADE PRINTING
# ============================================================

def print_trade(number, trade, balance):

    print(
        f"\nTrade #{number}"
    )

    print(
        f"  {trade.direction} | "
        f"Entry: {trade.entry_time}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Entry={trade.entry:.5f} "
        f"SL={trade.sl:.5f} "
        f"TP1={trade.tp1:.5f} "
        f"TP2={trade.tp2:.5f} "
        f"TP3={trade.tp3:.5f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Lots={trade.lots:.4f} "
        f"Risk=${trade.risk_amount:.2f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Result={trade.close_reason} "
        f"PnL=${trade.pnl:.2f} "
        f"R={trade.pnl_r:.2f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Balance=${balance:.2f}"
    )



# ============================================================
# STATISTICS
# ============================================================

def calculate_statistics(trades):

    if not trades:
        return {}

    wins = [
        t for t in trades
        if t.pnl > 0
    ]

    losses = [
        t for t in trades
        if t.pnl < 0
    ]

    total_profit = sum(
        t.pnl for t in wins
    )

    total_loss = abs(sum(
        t.pnl for t in losses
    ))

    net_profit = sum(
        t.pnl for t in trades
    )

    win_rate = (
        len(wins) /
        len(trades) *
        100
    )

    profit_factor = (
        total_profit / total_loss
        if total_loss > 0
        else math.inf
    )

    expectancy = (
        net_profit / len(trades)
    )

    average_r = (
        sum(t.pnl_r for t in trades) /
        len(trades)
    )

    return {
        "total_trades": len(trades),
        "wins": len(wins),
        "losses": len(losses),
        "win_rate": win_rate,
        "gross_profit": total_profit,
        "gross_loss": total_loss,
        "net_profit": net_profit,
        "profit_factor": profit_factor,
        "expectancy": expectancy,
        "average_r": average_r,
    }

def print_outcome_breakdown(trades):

    if not trades:
        print("\nNo trades to analyze.")
        return

    sl_before_tp2 = 0
    tp2_then_sl = 0
    tp2_then_tp3 = 0
    end_of_day_after_tp2 = 0
    end_of_day_before_tp2 = 0
    end_of_data_after_tp2 = 0
    end_of_data_before_tp2 = 0
    other = 0

    for trade in trades:

        if trade.close_reason == "SL":

            if trade.tp2_hit:
                tp2_then_sl += 1
            else:
                sl_before_tp2 += 1

        elif trade.close_reason == "TP3":

            tp2_then_tp3 += 1

        elif trade.close_reason == "END_OF_DAY":

            if trade.tp2_hit:
                end_of_day_after_tp2 += 1
            else:
                end_of_day_before_tp2 += 1

        elif trade.close_reason == "END_OF_DATA":

            if trade.tp2_hit:
                end_of_data_after_tp2 += 1
            else:
                end_of_data_before_tp2 += 1

        else:
            other += 1

    total = len(trades)

    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("TRADE OUTCOME BREAKDOWN")
    print("=" * 70)

    print(
        f"{'Outcome':<30}"
        f"{'Trades':>10}"
        f"{'Percent':>12}"
    )

    print("-" * 70)

    def show(name, count):

        percentage = (
            count / total * 100
            if total > 0
            else 0
        )

        print(
            f"{name:<30}"
            f"{count:>10}"
            f"{percentage:>11.2f}%"
        )

    show(
        "SL before TP2",
        sl_before_tp2
    )

    show(
        "TP2 -> SL",
        tp2_then_sl
    )

    show(
        "TP2 -> TP3",
        tp2_then_tp3
    )

    show(
        "EOD after TP2",
        end_of_day_after_tp2
    )

    show(
        "EOD before TP2",
        end_of_day_before_tp2
    )

    show(
        "End of data after TP2",
        end_of_data_after_tp2
    )

    show(
        "End of data before TP2",
        end_of_data_before_tp2
    )

    show(
        "Other",
        other
    )

    print("-" * 70)

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Theoretical R contribution of the clean outcomes
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    print()
    print("R-MULTIPLE CONTRIBUTION")
    print("-" * 70)

    # SL before TP2:
    # Entire position loses 1R.
    r_sl = -1.0 * sl_before_tp2

    # TP2 -> SL:
    # 50% was closed at +2R = +1R.
    # Remaining 50% loses 1R = -0.5R.
    # Net = +0.5R.
    r_tp2_sl = 0.5 * tp2_then_sl

    # TP2 -> TP3:
    # 50% at +2R = +1R.
    # 50% at +3R = +1.5R.
    # Net = +2.5R.
    r_tp3 = 2.5 * tp2_then_tp3

    print(
        f"SL before TP2:          "
        f"{r_sl:+.2f}R"
    )

    print(
        f"TP2 -> SL:              "
        f"{r_tp2_sl:+.2f}R"
    )

    print(
        f"TP2 -> TP3:             "
        f"{r_tp3:+.2f}R"
    )

    print("=" * 70)


def print_direction_breakdown(trades):

    if not trades:
        return

    buys = [
        t for t in trades
        if t.direction == "BUY"
    ]

    sells = [
        t for t in trades
        if t.direction == "SELL"
    ]

    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("BUY vs SELL BREAKDOWN")
    print("=" * 70)

    def analyze(name, group):

        if not group:
            print(f"\n{name}: No trades")
            return

        wins = sum(
            1 for t in group
            if t.pnl > 0
        )

        losses = sum(
            1 for t in group
            if t.pnl < 0
        )

        pnl = sum(
            t.pnl for t in group
        )

        total_r = sum(
            t.pnl_r for t in group
        )

        avg_r = (
            total_r / len(group)
        )

        win_rate = (
            wins / len(group) * 100
        )

        gross_profit = sum(
            t.pnl for t in group
            if t.pnl > 0
        )

        gross_loss = abs(sum(
            t.pnl for t in group
            if t.pnl < 0
        ))

        profit_factor = (
            gross_profit / gross_loss
            if gross_loss > 0
            else float("inf")
        )

        sl_before_tp1 = sum(
            1 for t in group
            if t.close_reason == "SL"
            and not t.tp1_hit
        )

        tp1_tp2 = sum(
            1 for t in group
            if t.close_reason == "TP2"
        )

        tp1_sl = sum(
            1 for t in group
            if t.close_reason == "SL"
            and t.tp1_hit
        )

        print()
        print(f"{name}")
        print("-" * 70)

        print(
            f"Trades:                 {len(group)}"
        )

        print(
            f"Wins:                   {wins}"
        )

        print(
            f"Losses:                 {losses}"
        )

        print(
            f"Win rate:               {win_rate:.2f}%"
        )

        print(
            f"Profit factor:          "
            f"{profit_factor:.3f}"
            if profit_factor != float("inf")
            else
            "Profit factor:          INF"
        )

        print(
            f"Net PnL:                ${pnl:,.2f}"
        )

        print(
            f"Total R:                {total_r:+.2f}R"
        )

        print(
            f"Average R/trade:        {avg_r:+.3f}R"
        )

        print(
            f"SL before TP1:          {sl_before_tp1}"
        )

        print(
            f"TP1 -> SL:              {tp1_sl}"
        )

        print(
            f"TP1 -> TP2:             {tp1_tp2}"
        )

    analyze("BUY", buys)
    analyze("SELL", sells)

    print("=" * 70)


def calculate_drawdown(
    equity_curve,
    starting_balance
):

    if not equity_curve:
        return 0.0, 0.0

    peak = starting_balance
    max_drawdown = 0.0
    max_drawdown_percent = 0.0

    for _, equity in equity_curve:

        if equity > peak:
            peak = equity

        drawdown = peak - equity

        if drawdown > max_drawdown:
            max_drawdown = drawdown

            if peak > 0:
                max_drawdown_percent = (
                    drawdown / peak * 100
                )

    return max_drawdown, max_drawdown_percent


def print_statistics(
    trades,
    equity_curve,
    final_balance
):

    stats = calculate_statistics(trades)

    max_dd, max_dd_percent = calculate_drawdown(
        equity_curve,
        STARTING_BALANCE
    )

    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("BACKTEST RESULTS")
    print("=" * 70)

    print(
        f"Starting balance:       "
        f"${STARTING_BALANCE:,.2f}"
    )

    print(
        f"Final balance:          "
        f"${final_balance:,.2f}"
    )

    print(
        f"Net profit:             "
        f"${stats.get('net_profit', 0):,.2f}"
    )

    print(
        f"Return:                 "
        f"{(final_balance / STARTING_BALANCE - 1) * 100:.2f}%"
    )

    print()
    print(
        f"Total trades:           "
        f"{stats.get('total_trades', 0)}"
    )

    print(
        f"Wins:                   "
        f"{stats.get('wins', 0)}"
    )

    print(
        f"Losses:                 "
        f"{stats.get('losses', 0)}"
    )

    print(
        f"Win rate:               "
        f"{stats.get('win_rate', 0):.2f}%"
    )

    pf = stats.get(
        "profit_factor",
        0
    )

    if math.isinf(pf):
        print("Profit factor:          INF")
    else:
        print(
            f"Profit factor:          "
            f"{pf:.3f}"
        )

    print(
        f"Expectancy/trade:       "
        f"${stats.get('expectancy', 0):.2f}"
    )

    print(
        f"Average R/trade:        "
        f"{stats.get('average_r', 0):.3f}R"
    )

    print()
    print(
        f"Maximum drawdown:       "
        f"${max_dd:,.2f}"
    )

    print(
        f"Maximum drawdown:       "
        f"{max_dd_percent:.2f}%"
    )

    print("=" * 70)

def export_trades(
    trades,
    filename="SP2L_trades.csv"
):

    with open(
        filename,
        "w",
        newline="",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:

        writer = csv.writer(f)

        writer.writerow([
            "direction",
            "setup_time",
            "entry_time",
            "entry",
            "sl",
            "tp1",
            "tp2",
            "tp3",
            "lots",
            "risk",
            "tp1_hit",
            "tp2_hit",
            "sl_hit",
            "exit_time",
            "exit_price",
            "close_reason",
            "pnl",
            "pnl_R",
            "c1_time",
            "c2_time",
            "c3_time",
        ])

        for t in trades:

            writer.writerow([
                t.direction,
                t.setup_time,
                t.entry_time,
                f"{t.entry:.5f}",
                f"{t.sl:.5f}",
                f"{t.tp1:.5f}",
                f"{t.tp2:.5f}",
                f"{t.tp3:.5f}",
                f"{t.lots:.6f}",
                f"{t.risk_amount:.2f}",
                t.tp1_hit,
                t.tp2_hit,
                t.sl_hit,
                t.exit_time,
                f"{t.exit_price:.5f}",
                t.close_reason,
                f"{t.pnl:.2f}",
                f"{t.pnl_r:.4f}",
                t.c1_time,
                t.c2_time,
                t.c3_time,
            ])

    print()
    print(
        f"Trade log exported to: {filename}"
    )

import pandas as pd
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import seaborn as sns

def visualize_sp2l_history(csv_path="sp2l_trade_history.csv"):
    try:
        df = pd.read_csv(csv_path)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        print(f"Error: Could not find '{csv_path}'.")
        return

    if df.empty:
        print("Trade history is empty.")
        return

    # Prepare timeline and drawdown metrics
    df['Entry_Time'] = pd.to_datetime(df['Entry_Time'])
    df['Cum_PnL'] = df['Net_PnL'].cumsum()
    df['Peak_PnL'] = df['Cum_PnL'].cummax()
    df['Drawdown'] = df['Cum_PnL'] - df['Peak_PnL']

    # Initialize plotting environment
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(15, 10))
    fig.suptitle('SP2L Strategy - Trade History Visual Analysis', fontsize=16, fontweight='bold')

    # 1. Equity Curve
    axes[0, 0].plot(df['Entry_Time'], df['Cum_PnL'], color='green', linewidth=2)
    axes[0, 0].set_title('Cumulative Equity Curve')
    axes[0, 0].set_ylabel('Net PnL ($)')
    axes[0, 0].grid(True, alpha=0.3)
    axes[0, 0].tick_params(axis='x', rotation=45)

    # 2. Underwater / Drawdown Curve
    axes[0, 1].fill_between(df['Entry_Time'], df['Drawdown'], 0, color='red', alpha=0.4)
    axes[0, 1].plot(df['Entry_Time'], df['Drawdown'], color='darkred', linewidth=1)
    axes[0, 1].set_title('Drawdown Curve')
    axes[0, 1].set_ylabel('Drawdown ($)')
    axes[0, 1].grid(True, alpha=0.3)
    axes[0, 1].tick_params(axis='x', rotation=45)

    # 3. PnL by Pullback Length
    pb_pnl = df.groupby('Pullback_Candles')['Net_PnL'].sum().reset_index()
    sns.barplot(
        x='Pullback_Candles', y='Net_PnL', data=pb_pnl, 
        ax=axes[1, 0], hue='Pullback_Candles', palette='viridis', legend=False
    )
    axes[1, 0].set_title('Net PnL by Pullback Candles')
    axes[1, 0].set_ylabel('Net PnL ($)')
    axes[1, 0].set_xlabel('Number of Candles')

    # 4. Initial Entries vs 2X Scale-In
    scale_pnl = df.groupby('Scale_In')['Net_PnL'].sum().reset_index()
    scale_pnl['Scale_In'] = scale_pnl['Scale_In'].map({False: 'Initial Entry', True: '2X Scale-In'})
    sns.barplot(
        x='Scale_In', y='Net_PnL', data=scale_pnl, 
        ax=axes[1, 1], hue='Scale_In', palette='coolwarm', legend=False
    )
    axes[1, 1].set_title('Initial vs Scale-In Performance')
    axes[1, 1].set_ylabel('Net PnL ($)')
    axes[1, 1].set_xlabel('Entry Type')

    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.show()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    visualize_sp2l_history()

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-v2\main.py
