import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import List, Optional
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np

# ==============================================================================
# 1. BROKER, RISK & STRATEGY CONFIGURATION (M1 SCALPING)
# ==============================================================================
INITIAL_BALANCE = 10000.0  
RISK_PER_TRADE_PCT = 0.01  
MAX_DAILY_LOSS_PCT = 0.03  
MAX_WEEKLY_LOSS_PCT = 0.06  

SPREAD = 0.25  
COMMISSION_PER_LOT = 5.0  
CONTRACT_SIZE = 100.0  
LOT_STEP = 0.01  
MIN_LOT = 0.01  

# M1 Specific Rules (from SP2L Transcript)
EMA_PERIOD = 60            
MAX_LIMIT_WAIT_CANDLES = 3 
FIB_MIN_RETRACE = 0.382    
FIB_MAX_RETRACE = 0.618    

# Context Filters
SESSION_START_HOUR = 9     # London Open
SESSION_END_HOUR = 18      # NY Session End
MAX_EMA_DISTANCE = 2.50    # Prevents trading 3rd/4th pushes (E-GAPs)

# ==============================================================================
# 2. DATA STRUCTURES
# ==============================================================================
@dataclass
class PendingOrder:
    setup_id: int
    direction: str
    limit_price: float
    stop_loss: float
    take_profit: float
    setup_reason: str
    expiry_index: int      
    leg1_height: float

@dataclass
class Trade:
    trade_id: int
    setup_id: int
    direction: str  
    entry_time: datetime
    entry_price: float
    stop_loss: float
    take_profit: float
    lot_size: float
    commission: float
    setup_reason: str = ''       
    is_scale_in: bool = False
    exit_time: Optional[datetime] = None
    exit_price: Optional[float] = None
    gross_pnl: float = 0.0
    net_pnl: float = 0.0
    exit_reason: str = '' 

# ==============================================================================
# 3. SP2L M1 BACKTESTING ENGINE
# ==============================================================================
class SP2LM1Backtester:

    def __init__(self, data_path: str):
        self.data_path = data_path
        self.df = self._load_data()

        self.balance = INITIAL_BALANCE
        self.equity = INITIAL_BALANCE

        self.current_day = None
        self.current_week = None
        self.daily_start_balance = INITIAL_BALANCE
        self.weekly_start_balance = INITIAL_BALANCE
        self.daily_loss_locked = False
        self.weekly_loss_locked = False

        self.trades: List[Trade] = []
        self.active_trades: List[Trade] = []
        self.pending_order: Optional[PendingOrder] = None
        
        self.trade_counter = 0
        self.setup_counter = 0

    def _load_data(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
        col_names = ['date', 'time', 'open', 'high', 'low', 'close', 'volume']
        df = pd.read_csv(self.data_path, header=None, names=col_names)
        df['datetime'] = pd.to_datetime(df['date'] + ' ' + df['time'], format='%Y.%m.%d %H:%M')
        df.sort_values('datetime', inplace=True)
        df.reset_index(drop=True, inplace=True)
        df['ema_60'] = df['close'].ewm(span=EMA_PERIOD, adjust=False).mean()
        return df

    def _update_calendar_limits(self, current_dt: datetime):
        day = current_dt.date()
        week = (current_dt.year, current_dt.isocalendar()[1])

        if self.current_day != day:
            self.current_day = day
            self.daily_start_balance = self.balance
            self.daily_loss_locked = False

        if self.current_week != week:
            self.current_week = week
            self.weekly_start_balance = self.balance
            self.weekly_loss_locked = False

        if (self.daily_start_balance - self.balance) / self.daily_start_balance >= MAX_DAILY_LOSS_PCT:
            self.daily_loss_locked = True
        if (self.weekly_start_balance - self.balance) / self.weekly_start_balance >= MAX_WEEKLY_LOSS_PCT:
            self.weekly_loss_locked = True

    def _calculate_lot_size(self, entry_price: float, sl_price: float) -> Optional[float]:
        sl_distance = abs(entry_price - sl_price)
        if sl_distance <= 0: return None
        max_risk_dollars = self.balance * RISK_PER_TRADE_PCT
        loss_per_lot = (sl_distance * CONTRACT_SIZE) + COMMISSION_PER_LOT
        raw_lot = max_risk_dollars / loss_per_lot
        lot_size = round(math.floor(raw_lot / LOT_STEP) * LOT_STEP, 2)
        return lot_size if lot_size >= MIN_LOT else None

    def _process_active_trades(self, bar: pd.Series):
        if not self.active_trades:
            return

        remaining_trades = []
        scaled_in_setup_ids = {t.setup_id for t in self.active_trades if t.is_scale_in}

        for trade in self.active_trades:
            trade_closed = False

            if trade.direction == 'BUY':
                bid_low = bar['low'] - (SPREAD / 2.0)
                bid_high = bar['high'] - (SPREAD / 2.0)

                # 2X Scale-In Logic
                halfway_price = trade.entry_price - ((trade.entry_price - trade.stop_loss) / 2)
                if bid_low <= halfway_price and trade.setup_id not in scaled_in_setup_ids:
                    lot_size = self._calculate_lot_size(halfway_price, trade.stop_loss)
                    if lot_size:
                        self.trade_counter += 1
                        scale_trade = Trade(
                            trade_id=self.trade_counter, setup_id=trade.setup_id,
                            direction='BUY', entry_time=bar['datetime'],
                            entry_price=halfway_price, stop_loss=trade.stop_loss,
                            take_profit=trade.take_profit, lot_size=lot_size,
                            commission=lot_size * COMMISSION_PER_LOT,
                            setup_reason='2X Scale-In (Bullish)', is_scale_in=True
                        )
                        remaining_trades.append(scale_trade)
                        scaled_in_setup_ids.add(trade.setup_id)

                if bid_low <= trade.stop_loss:
                    trade.exit_price = trade.stop_loss
                    trade.exit_reason = 'SL'
                    trade_closed = True
                elif bid_high >= trade.take_profit:
                    trade.exit_price = trade.take_profit
                    trade.exit_reason = 'TP'
                    trade_closed = True

            elif trade.direction == 'SELL':
                ask_high = bar['high'] + (SPREAD / 2.0)
                ask_low = bar['low'] + (SPREAD / 2.0)

                # 2X Scale-In Logic
                halfway_price = trade.entry_price + ((trade.stop_loss - trade.entry_price) / 2)
                if ask_high >= halfway_price and trade.setup_id not in scaled_in_setup_ids:
                    lot_size = self._calculate_lot_size(halfway_price, trade.stop_loss)
                    if lot_size:
                        self.trade_counter += 1
                        scale_trade = Trade(
                            trade_id=self.trade_counter, setup_id=trade.setup_id,
                            direction='SELL', entry_time=bar['datetime'],
                            entry_price=halfway_price, stop_loss=trade.stop_loss,
                            take_profit=trade.take_profit, lot_size=lot_size,
                            commission=lot_size * COMMISSION_PER_LOT,
                            setup_reason='2X Scale-In (Bearish)', is_scale_in=True
                        )
                        remaining_trades.append(scale_trade)
                        scaled_in_setup_ids.add(trade.setup_id)

                if ask_high >= trade.stop_loss:
                    trade.exit_price = trade.stop_loss
                    trade.exit_reason = 'SL'
                    trade_closed = True
                elif ask_low <= trade.take_profit:
                    trade.exit_price = trade.take_profit
                    trade.exit_reason = 'TP'
                    trade_closed = True

            if trade_closed:
                trade.exit_time = bar['datetime']
                price_diff = (trade.exit_price - trade.entry_price) if trade.direction == 'BUY' else (trade.entry_price - trade.exit_price)
                trade.gross_pnl = price_diff * trade.lot_size * CONTRACT_SIZE
                trade.net_pnl = trade.gross_pnl - trade.commission
                self.balance += trade.net_pnl
                self.trades.append(trade)
            else:
                remaining_trades.append(trade)

        self.active_trades = remaining_trades

    def export_trades_to_csv(self, filename: str = "sp2l_trade_history.csv"):
        """Exports the trade history to a CSV file for manual review."""
        if not self.trades:
            return
        trade_records = []
        for t in self.trades:
            trade_records.append({
                'Trade_ID': t.trade_id, 'Setup_ID': t.setup_id, 'Direction': t.direction,
                'Scale_In': t.is_scale_in, 'Setup_Reason': t.setup_reason,
                'Entry_Time': t.entry_time, 'Entry_Price': round(t.entry_price, 2), 
                'Stop_Loss': round(t.stop_loss, 2), 'Take_Profit': round(t.take_profit, 2), 
                'Lot_Size': t.lot_size, 'Exit_Time': t.exit_time, 
                'Exit_Price': round(t.exit_price, 2) if t.exit_price else None,
                'Exit_Reason': t.exit_reason, 'Net_PnL': round(t.net_pnl, 2)
            })
        pd.DataFrame(trade_records).to_csv(filename, index=False)
        print(f"\n[✓] Exported {len(self.trades)} trades to '{filename}'")

    def run(self):
        print(f'Starting SP2L M1 Limit-Order Engine on {len(self.df)} candles...')
        lookback = EMA_PERIOD

        for i in range(lookback, len(self.df)):
            bar = self.df.iloc[i]
            self._update_calendar_limits(bar['datetime'])
            self._process_active_trades(bar)

            if self.active_trades or self.daily_loss_locked or self.weekly_loss_locked:
                continue

            # Time Session Filter (London & NY)
            if not (SESSION_START_HOUR <= bar['datetime'].hour <= SESSION_END_HOUR):
                self.pending_order = None # Cancel pending orders outside of active session
                continue

            # Check Pending Limit Order Fill
            if self.pending_order:
                po = self.pending_order
                filled = False
                
                if po.direction == 'BUY':
                    ask_low = bar['low'] + (SPREAD / 2.0)
                    if ask_low <= po.limit_price:
                        filled = True
                elif po.direction == 'SELL':
                    bid_high = bar['high'] - (SPREAD / 2.0)
                    if bid_high >= po.limit_price:
                        filled = True
                        
                if filled:
                    lot_size = self._calculate_lot_size(po.limit_price, po.stop_loss)
                    if lot_size:
                        self.trade_counter += 1
                        trade = Trade(
                            trade_id=self.trade_counter, setup_id=po.setup_id, direction=po.direction,
                            entry_time=bar['datetime'], entry_price=po.limit_price,
                            stop_loss=po.stop_loss, take_profit=po.take_profit,
                            lot_size=lot_size, commission=lot_size * COMMISSION_PER_LOT,
                            setup_reason=po.setup_reason
                        )
                        self.active_trades.append(trade)
                        self._process_active_trades(bar)
                    self.pending_order = None
                    continue
                elif i >= po.expiry_index:
                    self.pending_order = None 
                    continue

            if self.pending_order is not None:
                continue 

            # Detect New SP2L Setup (Spike + P-GAP + Fib Check)
            c_0 = self.df.iloc[i-3] 
            c_1 = self.df.iloc[i-2] 
            c_2 = self.df.iloc[i-1] 

            # A) BULLISH SETUP
            is_uptrend = c_2['close'] > c_2['ema_60']
            is_breakout_bull = c_1['close'] > c_0['high'] and c_1['close'] > c_1['open']
            is_pgap_bull = c_2['low'] > c_0['high']
            
            if is_uptrend and is_breakout_bull and is_pgap_bull:
                spike_origin = min(c_0['low'], c_1['low'])
                
                # E-GAP Filter (Must originate near EMA)
                if abs(spike_origin - c_0['ema_60']) <= MAX_EMA_DISTANCE:
                    spike_peak = c_2['high']
                    leg1_height = spike_peak - spike_origin
                    gap_boundary = c_0['high']

                    if leg1_height > 0:
                        retracement_pct = (spike_peak - gap_boundary) / leg1_height
                        if FIB_MIN_RETRACE <= retracement_pct <= FIB_MAX_RETRACE:
                            self.setup_counter += 1
                            entry_price = gap_boundary + (SPREAD / 2.0)
                            stop_loss = spike_origin - (SPREAD / 2.0)
                            
                            self.pending_order = PendingOrder(
                                setup_id=self.setup_counter, direction='BUY', 
                                limit_price=entry_price, stop_loss=stop_loss, 
                                take_profit=entry_price + leg1_height, 
                                setup_reason=f'Bull P-GAP ({retracement_pct*100:.1f}% Fib)',
                                expiry_index=i + MAX_LIMIT_WAIT_CANDLES, leg1_height=leg1_height
                            )
                            continue

            # B) BEARISH SETUP
            is_downtrend = c_2['close'] < c_2['ema_60']
            is_breakout_bear = c_1['close'] < c_0['low'] and c_1['close'] < c_1['open']
            is_pgap_bear = c_2['high'] < c_0['low']

            if is_downtrend and is_breakout_bear and is_pgap_bear:
                spike_origin = max(c_0['high'], c_1['high'])
                
                # E-GAP Filter (Must originate near EMA)
                if abs(spike_origin - c_0['ema_60']) <= MAX_EMA_DISTANCE:
                    spike_peak = c_2['low']
                    leg1_height = spike_origin - spike_peak
                    gap_boundary = c_0['low']

                    if leg1_height > 0:
                        retracement_pct = (gap_boundary - spike_peak) / leg1_height
                        if FIB_MIN_RETRACE <= retracement_pct <= FIB_MAX_RETRACE:
                            self.setup_counter += 1
                            entry_price = gap_boundary - (SPREAD / 2.0)
                            stop_loss = spike_origin + (SPREAD / 2.0)
                            
                            self.pending_order = PendingOrder(
                                setup_id=self.setup_counter, direction='SELL', 
                                limit_price=entry_price, stop_loss=stop_loss, 
                                take_profit=entry_price - leg1_height,
                                setup_reason=f'Bear P-GAP ({retracement_pct*100:.1f}% Fib)',
                                expiry_index=i + MAX_LIMIT_WAIT_CANDLES, leg1_height=leg1_height
                            )

        self.export_trades_to_csv()
        self.generate_deep_analysis_report()

    def generate_deep_analysis_report(self):
        if not self.trades:
            print('No trades executed during the backtest period.')
            return

        df = pd.DataFrame([{
            'Direction': t.direction, 'Scale_In': t.is_scale_in, 
            'Entry_Time': t.entry_time, 'Exit_Time': t.exit_time,
            'Net_PnL': t.net_pnl, 'Setup_Reason': t.setup_reason
        } for t in self.trades])
        
        wins = df[df['Net_PnL'] > 0]
        losses = df[df['Net_PnL'] <= 0]
        
        gross_profit = wins['Net_PnL'].sum()
        gross_loss = abs(losses['Net_PnL'].sum())
        profit_factor = (gross_profit / gross_loss) if gross_loss > 0 else float('inf')
        win_rate = (len(wins) / len(df)) * 100

        avg_win = wins['Net_PnL'].mean() if not wins.empty else 0
        avg_loss = abs(losses['Net_PnL'].mean()) if not losses.empty else 0
        expectancy = ((win_rate / 100) * avg_win) - (((100 - win_rate) / 100) * avg_loss)

        df['Cum_PnL'] = df['Net_PnL'].cumsum()
        max_dd_dollars = (df['Cum_PnL'].cummax() - df['Cum_PnL']).max()

        print("\n" + "="*60)
        print("    SP2L M1 LIMIT-ORDER PERFORMANCE REPORT (WITH 2X)    ")
        print("="*60)
        print(f"Total Trades         : {len(df)}")
        print(f"Net Profit           : ${df['Net_PnL'].sum():,.2f}")
        print(f"Profit Factor        : {profit_factor:.2f}")
        print(f"Win Rate             : {win_rate:.2f}% ({len(wins)}W / {len(losses)}L)")
        print("-" * 60)
        print(f"Average Win          : ${avg_win:,.2f}")
        print(f"Average Loss         : ${-avg_loss:,.2f}")
        print(f"Expectancy Per Trade : ${expectancy:,.2f} (Expected Value)")
        print(f"Max Dollar Drawdown  : ${max_dd_dollars:,.2f}")
        
        print("-" * 60)
        print(" >> SEGMENTED PERFORMANCE (INITIAL vs 2X SCALE-IN)")
        initial_trades = df[df['Scale_In'] == False]
        scale_trades = df[df['Scale_In'] == True]
        for label, subset in [("Initial Entries", initial_trades), ("2X Scale-Ins ", scale_trades)]:
            if not subset.empty:
                s_wr = (len(subset[subset['Net_PnL'] > 0]) / len(subset)) * 100
                s_pnl = subset['Net_PnL'].sum()
                print(f" {label}: {len(subset):3} trades | WR: {s_wr:5.1f}% | Net: ${s_pnl:,.2f}")
        print("="*60 + "\n")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    engine = SP2LM1Backtester('XAUUSD1.csv')
    engine.run()

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-v2\qvb_filtered_engine.py
================================================================================

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import List, Optional
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np

# ==============================================================================
# 1. QUANTITATIVE CONFIGURATION WITH EQUILIBRIUM FILTER
# ==============================================================================
INITIAL_BALANCE = 10000.0  
RISK_PER_TRADE_PCT = 0.01  
MAX_DAILY_LOSS_PCT = 0.03  
MAX_WEEKLY_LOSS_PCT = 0.06

SPREAD = 0.25  
COMMISSION_PER_LOT = 5.0  
CONTRACT_SIZE = 100.0  
LOT_STEP = 0.01  
MIN_LOT = 0.01  

DONCHIAN_PERIOD = 60       
EMA_PERIOD = 200           
EQUILIBRIUM_EMA_PERIOD = 60  # Added 60 EMA equilibrium baseline
ATR_PERIOD = 14            
ATR_SMA_PERIOD = 50        
TRAILING_ATR_MULT = 2.5    

SESSION_START_HOUR = 8     
SESSION_END_HOUR = 17      

@dataclass
class Trade:
    trade_id: int
    direction: str  
    entry_time: datetime
    entry_price: float
    initial_stop: float
    current_stop: float
    lot_size: float
    commission: float
    exit_time: Optional[datetime] = None
    exit_price: Optional[float] = None
    gross_pnl: float = 0.0
    net_pnl: float = 0.0
    exit_reason: str = '' 

class FilteredQVBBacktester:
    def __init__(self, data_path: str):
        self.data_path = data_path
        self.df = self._prepare_data()
        self.balance = INITIAL_BALANCE
        
        self.current_day = None
        self.current_week = None
        self.daily_start_balance = INITIAL_BALANCE
        self.weekly_start_balance = INITIAL_BALANCE
        self.daily_loss_locked = False
        self.weekly_loss_locked = False

        self.trades: List[Trade] = []
        self.active_trade: Optional[Trade] = None
        self.trade_counter = 0

    def _prepare_data(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
        col_names = ['datetime_str', 'open', 'high', 'low', 'close', 'volume']
        df = pd.read_csv(self.data_path, header=None, names=col_names)
        df['datetime'] = pd.to_datetime(df['datetime_str'], format='%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')
        df.drop(columns=['datetime_str'], inplace=True)
        df.sort_values('datetime', inplace=True)
        df.reset_index(drop=True, inplace=True)
        
        # Macro Institutional Baseline & Equilibrium Price
        df['ema_200'] = df['close'].ewm(span=EMA_PERIOD, adjust=False).mean()
        df['equilibrium_ema_60'] = df['close'].ewm(span=EQUILIBRIUM_EMA_PERIOD, adjust=False).mean()
        
        # Donchian Channels
        df['donchian_high_prev'] = df['high'].shift(1).rolling(window=DONCHIAN_PERIOD).max()
        df['donchian_low_prev'] = df['low'].shift(1).rolling(window=DONCHIAN_PERIOD).min()
        
        # ATR Volatility Expansion
        high_low = df['high'] - df['low']
        high_close = np.abs(df['high'] - df['close'].shift(1))
        low_close = np.abs(df['low'] - df['close'].shift(1))
        true_range = pd.concat([high_low, high_close, low_close], axis=1).max(axis=1)
        df['atr'] = true_range.ewm(span=ATR_PERIOD, adjust=False).mean()
        df['atr_sma'] = df['atr'].rolling(window=ATR_SMA_PERIOD).mean()
        
        return df

    def _update_calendar_limits(self, current_dt: datetime):
        day = current_dt.date()
        week = (current_dt.year, current_dt.isocalendar()[1])

        if self.current_day != day:
            self.current_day = day
            self.daily_start_balance = self.balance
            self.daily_loss_locked = False

        if self.current_week != week:
            self.current_week = week
            self.weekly_start_balance = self.balance
            self.weekly_loss_locked = False

        if (self.daily_start_balance - self.balance) / self.daily_start_balance >= MAX_DAILY_LOSS_PCT:
            self.daily_loss_locked = True
        if (self.weekly_start_balance - self.balance) / self.weekly_start_balance >= MAX_WEEKLY_LOSS_PCT:
            self.weekly_loss_locked = True

    def _calculate_lot_size(self, entry_price: float, sl_price: float) -> Optional[float]:
        sl_distance = abs(entry_price - sl_price)
        if sl_distance <= 0: return None
        loss_per_lot = (sl_distance * CONTRACT_SIZE) + COMMISSION_PER_LOT
        raw_lot = (self.balance * RISK_PER_TRADE_PCT) / loss_per_lot
        lot_size = round(math.floor(raw_lot / LOT_STEP) * LOT_STEP, 2)
        return lot_size if lot_size >= MIN_LOT else None

    def export_trades_to_csv(self, filename: str = "qvb_filtered_history.csv"):
        if not self.trades: return
        trade_records = [{
            'Trade_ID': t.trade_id, 'Direction': t.direction,
            'Entry_Time': t.entry_time, 'Entry_Price': round(t.entry_price, 2), 
            'Exit_Stop': round(t.current_stop, 2), 'Lot_Size': t.lot_size, 
            'Exit_Time': t.exit_time, 'Exit_Price': round(t.exit_price, 2), 
            'Net_PnL': round(t.net_pnl, 2)
        } for t in self.trades]
        pd.DataFrame(trade_records).to_csv(filename, index=False)
        print(f"\n[✓] Exported trade records to '{filename}'")

    def run(self):
        print(f'Starting Filtered QVB Engine on {len(self.df)} candles...')
        
        start_idx = max(DONCHIAN_PERIOD, EMA_PERIOD, ATR_SMA_PERIOD, EQUILIBRIUM_EMA_PERIOD) + 2

        for i in range(start_idx, len(self.df)):
            curr_bar = self.df.iloc[i]
            prev_bar = self.df.iloc[i-1]
            
            self._update_calendar_limits(curr_bar['datetime'])
            
            # 1. Evaluate Exits & Trailing Stops
            if self.active_trade:
                trade = self.active_trade
                trade_closed = False
                
                if trade.direction == 'BUY':
                    bid_low = curr_bar['low'] - (SPREAD / 2.0)
                    if bid_low <= trade.current_stop:
                        trade.exit_price = min(trade.current_stop, curr_bar['open'] - (SPREAD / 2.0))
                        trade_closed = True
                    else:
                        new_stop = curr_bar['close'] - (curr_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                        if new_stop > trade.current_stop:
                            trade.current_stop = new_stop

                elif trade.direction == 'SELL':
                    ask_high = curr_bar['high'] + (SPREAD / 2.0)
                    if ask_high >= trade.current_stop:
                        trade.exit_price = max(trade.current_stop, curr_bar['open'] + (SPREAD / 2.0))
                        trade_closed = True
                    else:
                        new_stop = curr_bar['close'] + (curr_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                        if new_stop < trade.current_stop:
                            trade.current_stop = new_stop

                if trade_closed:
                    trade.exit_time = curr_bar['datetime']
                    trade.exit_reason = 'TRAILING_SL'
                    price_diff = (trade.exit_price - trade.entry_price) if trade.direction == 'BUY' else (trade.entry_price - trade.exit_price)
                    trade.gross_pnl = price_diff * trade.lot_size * CONTRACT_SIZE
                    trade.net_pnl = trade.gross_pnl - trade.commission
                    self.balance += trade.net_pnl
                    self.trades.append(trade)
                    self.active_trade = None
                    
                continue 

            # 2. Calendar & Session Filters
            if self.daily_loss_locked or self.weekly_loss_locked:
                continue
            if not (SESSION_START_HOUR <= curr_bar['datetime'].hour <= SESSION_END_HOUR):
                continue

            # 3. Signals with Equilibrium Filter
            is_volatility_expanding = prev_bar['atr'] > prev_bar['atr_sma']
            
            # Equilibrium alignment check: ensure price isn't overly overextended from the 60 EMA equilibrium baseline
            price_near_equilibrium_long = prev_bar['close'] >= prev_bar['equilibrium_ema_60']
            price_near_equilibrium_short = prev_bar['close'] <= prev_bar['equilibrium_ema_60']

            # LONG ENTRY
            if (prev_bar['close'] > prev_bar['donchian_high_prev']) and \
               (prev_bar['close'] > prev_bar['ema_200']) and \
               price_near_equilibrium_long and \
               is_volatility_expanding:
                
                entry_price = curr_bar['open'] + (SPREAD / 2.0)
                initial_stop = entry_price - (prev_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                
                lot_size = self._calculate_lot_size(entry_price, initial_stop)
                if lot_size:
                    self.trade_counter += 1
                    self.active_trade = Trade(
                        trade_id=self.trade_counter, direction='BUY',
                        entry_time=curr_bar['datetime'], entry_price=entry_price,
                        initial_stop=initial_stop, current_stop=initial_stop,
                        lot_size=lot_size, commission=lot_size * COMMISSION_PER_LOT
                    )
                    
                    if (curr_bar['low'] - (SPREAD / 2.0)) <= initial_stop:
                        self.active_trade.exit_time = curr_bar['datetime']
                        self.active_trade.exit_price = initial_stop
                        self.active_trade.exit_reason = 'SAME_BAR_SL'
                        price_diff = self.active_trade.exit_price - self.active_trade.entry_price
                        self.active_trade.gross_pnl = price_diff * lot_size * CONTRACT_SIZE
                        self.active_trade.net_pnl = self.active_trade.gross_pnl - self.active_trade.commission
                        self.balance += self.active_trade.net_pnl
                        self.trades.append(self.active_trade)
                        self.active_trade = None
                    else:
                        new_stop = curr_bar['close'] - (curr_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                        if new_stop > initial_stop:
                            self.active_trade.current_stop = new_stop
                            
            # SHORT ENTRY
            elif (prev_bar['close'] < prev_bar['donchian_low_prev']) and \
                 (prev_bar['close'] < prev_bar['ema_200']) and \
                 price_near_equilibrium_short and \
                 is_volatility_expanding:
                
                entry_price = curr_bar['open'] - (SPREAD / 2.0)
                initial_stop = entry_price + (prev_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                
                lot_size = self._calculate_lot_size(entry_price, initial_stop)
                if lot_size:
                    self.trade_counter += 1
                    self.active_trade = Trade(
                        trade_id=self.trade_counter, direction='SELL',
                        entry_time=curr_bar['datetime'], entry_price=entry_price,
                        initial_stop=initial_stop, current_stop=initial_stop,
                        lot_size=lot_size, commission=lot_size * COMMISSION_PER_LOT
                    )
                    
                    if (curr_bar['high'] + (SPREAD / 2.0)) >= initial_stop:
                        self.active_trade.exit_time = curr_bar['datetime']
                        self.active_trade.exit_price = initial_stop
                        self.active_trade.exit_reason = 'SAME_BAR_SL'
                        price_diff = self.active_trade.entry_price - self.active_trade.exit_price
                        self.active_trade.gross_pnl = price_diff * lot_size * CONTRACT_SIZE
                        self.active_trade.net_pnl = self.active_trade.gross_pnl - self.active_trade.commission
                        self.balance += self.active_trade.net_pnl
                        self.trades.append(self.active_trade)
                        self.active_trade = None
                    else:
                        new_stop = curr_bar['close'] + (curr_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                        if new_stop < initial_stop:
                            self.active_trade.current_stop = new_stop

        self.export_trades_to_csv()
        self.generate_report()

    def generate_report(self):
        if not self.trades:
            print('No trades executed.')
            return

        df = pd.DataFrame([{'Net_PnL': t.net_pnl, 'Direction': t.direction} for t in self.trades])
        wins = df[df['Net_PnL'] > 0]
        losses = df[df['Net_PnL'] <= 0]
        
        win_rate = (len(wins) / len(df)) * 100
        profit_factor = wins['Net_PnL'].sum() / abs(losses['Net_PnL'].sum()) if not losses.empty else float('inf')
        
        avg_win = wins['Net_PnL'].mean() if not wins.empty else 0
        avg_loss = abs(losses['Net_PnL'].mean()) if not losses.empty else 0
        expectancy = ((win_rate / 100) * avg_win) - (((100 - win_rate) / 100) * avg_loss)
        net_return_pct = ((self.balance - INITIAL_BALANCE) / INITIAL_BALANCE) * 100.0

        print("\n" + "="*60)
        print("   FILTERED EQUILIBRIUM QVB PERFORMANCE REPORT      ")
        print("="*60)
        print(f"Starting Balance     : ${INITIAL_BALANCE:,.2f}")
        print(f"Final Balance        : ${self.balance:,.2f}")
        print(f"Net Return           : {net_return_pct:.2f}%")
        print("-" * 60)
        print(f"Total Trades         : {len(df)}")
        print(f"Net Profit           : ${df['Net_PnL'].sum():,.2f}")
        print(f"Profit Factor        : {profit_factor:.2f}")
        print(f"Win Rate             : {win_rate:.2f}%")
        print(f"Average Win          : ${avg_win:,.2f}")
        print(f"Average Loss         : ${-avg_loss:,.2f}")
        print(f"Expectancy Per Trade : ${expectancy:,.2f}")
        print("="*60 + "\n")
if __name__ == '__main__':
    engine = FilteredQVBBacktester('XAUUSD5_alternate.csv')
    engine.run()

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-v2\qvb_oos_test.py
================================================================================

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import List, Optional
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np

# ==============================================================================
# CONFIGURATION
# ==============================================================================
INITIAL_BALANCE = 10000.0  
RISK_PER_TRADE_PCT = 0.01  
MAX_DAILY_LOSS_PCT = 0.03  
MAX_WEEKLY_LOSS_PCT = 0.06

SPREAD = 0.25  
COMMISSION_PER_LOT = 5.0  
CONTRACT_SIZE = 100.0  
LOT_STEP = 0.01  
MIN_LOT = 0.01  

# Winning Parameters from Grid Search
DONCHIAN_PERIOD = 60       
EMA_PERIOD = 200           
TRAILING_ATR_MULT = 2.5    

ATR_PERIOD = 14            
ATR_SMA_PERIOD = 50        

SESSION_START_HOUR = 8     
SESSION_END_HOUR = 17      

# Split Ratio (e.g., first 70% In-Sample, last 30% Out-of-Sample)
OOS_SPLIT_RATIO = 0.70     

@dataclass
class Trade:
    trade_id: int
    direction: str  
    entry_time: datetime
    entry_price: float
    initial_stop: float
    current_stop: float
    lot_size: float
    commission: float
    exit_time: Optional[datetime] = None
    exit_price: Optional[float] = None
    gross_pnl: float = 0.0
    net_pnl: float = 0.0
    exit_reason: str = '' 

class OOSQVBBacktester:
    def __init__(self, df_subset: pd.DataFrame):
        self.df = self._prepare_data(df_subset.copy())
        self.balance = INITIAL_BALANCE
        
        self.current_day = None
        self.current_week = None
        self.daily_start_balance = INITIAL_BALANCE
        self.weekly_start_balance = INITIAL_BALANCE
        self.daily_loss_locked = False
        self.weekly_loss_locked = False

        self.trades: List[Trade] = []
        self.active_trade: Optional[Trade] = None
        self.trade_counter = 0

    def _prepare_data(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        df['ema_200'] = df['close'].ewm(span=EMA_PERIOD, adjust=False).mean()
        df['donchian_high_prev'] = df['high'].shift(1).rolling(window=DONCHIAN_PERIOD).max()
        df['donchian_low_prev'] = df['low'].shift(1).rolling(window=DONCHIAN_PERIOD).min()
        
        high_low = df['high'] - df['low']
        high_close = np.abs(df['high'] - df['close'].shift(1))
        low_close = np.abs(df['low'] - df['close'].shift(1))
        true_range = pd.concat([high_low, high_close, low_close], axis=1).max(axis=1)
        df['atr'] = true_range.ewm(span=ATR_PERIOD, adjust=False).mean()
        df['atr_sma'] = df['atr'].rolling(window=ATR_SMA_PERIOD).mean()
        return df

    def _update_calendar_limits(self, current_dt: datetime):
        day = current_dt.date()
        week = (current_dt.year, current_dt.isocalendar()[1])

        if self.current_day != day:
            self.current_day = day
            self.daily_start_balance = self.balance
            self.daily_loss_locked = False

        if self.current_week != week:
            self.current_week = week
            self.weekly_start_balance = self.balance
            self.weekly_loss_locked = False

        if (self.daily_start_balance - self.balance) / self.daily_start_balance >= MAX_DAILY_LOSS_PCT:
            self.daily_loss_locked = True
        if (self.weekly_start_balance - self.balance) / self.weekly_start_balance >= MAX_WEEKLY_LOSS_PCT:
            self.weekly_loss_locked = True

    def _calculate_lot_size(self, entry_price: float, sl_price: float) -> Optional[float]:
        sl_distance = abs(entry_price - sl_price)
        if sl_distance <= 0: return None
        loss_per_lot = (sl_distance * CONTRACT_SIZE) + COMMISSION_PER_LOT
        raw_lot = (self.balance * RISK_PER_TRADE_PCT) / loss_per_lot
        lot_size = round(math.floor(raw_lot / LOT_STEP) * LOT_STEP, 2)
        return lot_size if lot_size >= MIN_LOT else None

    def run(self):
        start_idx = max(DONCHIAN_PERIOD, EMA_PERIOD, ATR_SMA_PERIOD) + 2
        if start_idx >= len(self.df):
            print("Subset too small for indicators.")
            return

        for i in range(start_idx, len(self.df)):
            curr_bar = self.df.iloc[i]
            prev_bar = self.df.iloc[i-1]
            
            self._update_calendar_limits(curr_bar['datetime'])
            
            # 1. Manage Exits
            if self.active_trade:
                trade = self.active_trade
                trade_closed = False
                
                if trade.direction == 'BUY':
                    bid_low = curr_bar['low'] - (SPREAD / 2.0)
                    if bid_low <= trade.current_stop:
                        trade.exit_price = min(trade.current_stop, curr_bar['open'] - (SPREAD / 2.0))
                        trade_closed = True
                    else:
                        new_stop = curr_bar['close'] - (curr_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                        if new_stop > trade.current_stop:
                            trade.current_stop = new_stop

                elif trade.direction == 'SELL':
                    ask_high = curr_bar['high'] + (SPREAD / 2.0)
                    if ask_high >= trade.current_stop:
                        trade.exit_price = max(trade.current_stop, curr_bar['open'] + (SPREAD / 2.0))
                        trade_closed = True
                    else:
                        new_stop = curr_bar['close'] + (curr_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                        if new_stop < trade.current_stop:
                            trade.current_stop = new_stop

                if trade_closed:
                    trade.exit_time = curr_bar['datetime']
                    trade.exit_reason = 'TRAILING_SL'
                    price_diff = (trade.exit_price - trade.entry_price) if trade.direction == 'BUY' else (trade.entry_price - trade.exit_price)
                    trade.gross_pnl = price_diff * trade.lot_size * CONTRACT_SIZE
                    trade.net_pnl = trade.gross_pnl - trade.commission
                    self.balance += trade.net_pnl
                    self.trades.append(trade)
                    self.active_trade = None
                    
                continue

            if self.daily_loss_locked or self.weekly_loss_locked: continue
            if not (SESSION_START_HOUR <= curr_bar['datetime'].hour <= SESSION_END_HOUR): continue

            is_volatility_expanding = prev_bar['atr'] > prev_bar['atr_sma']
            
            # 2. Entries (Strict T-1)
            if (prev_bar['close'] > prev_bar['donchian_high_prev']) and (prev_bar['close'] > prev_bar['ema_200']) and is_volatility_expanding:
                entry_price = curr_bar['open'] + (SPREAD / 2.0)
                initial_stop = entry_price - (prev_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                
                lot_size = self._calculate_lot_size(entry_price, initial_stop)
                if lot_size:
                    self.trade_counter += 1
                    self.active_trade = Trade(
                        trade_id=self.trade_counter, direction='BUY', entry_time=curr_bar['datetime'], 
                        entry_price=entry_price, initial_stop=initial_stop, current_stop=initial_stop,
                        lot_size=lot_size, commission=lot_size * COMMISSION_PER_LOT
                    )
                    
                    if (curr_bar['low'] - (SPREAD / 2.0)) <= initial_stop:
                        self.active_trade.exit_time = curr_bar['datetime']
                        self.active_trade.exit_price = initial_stop
                        self.active_trade.exit_reason = 'SAME_BAR_SL'
                        price_diff = self.active_trade.exit_price - self.active_trade.entry_price
                        self.active_trade.gross_pnl = price_diff * lot_size * CONTRACT_SIZE
                        self.active_trade.net_pnl = self.active_trade.gross_pnl - self.active_trade.commission
                        self.balance += self.active_trade.net_pnl
                        self.trades.append(self.active_trade)
                        self.active_trade = None
                    else:
                        new_stop = curr_bar['close'] - (curr_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                        if new_stop > initial_stop: self.active_trade.current_stop = new_stop
                            
            elif (prev_bar['close'] < prev_bar['donchian_low_prev']) and (prev_bar['close'] < prev_bar['ema_200']) and is_volatility_expanding:
                entry_price = curr_bar['open'] - (SPREAD / 2.0)
                initial_stop = entry_price + (prev_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                
                lot_size = self._calculate_lot_size(entry_price, initial_stop)
                if lot_size:
                    self.trade_counter += 1
                    self.active_trade = Trade(
                        trade_id=self.trade_counter, direction='SELL', entry_time=curr_bar['datetime'], 
                        entry_price=entry_price, initial_stop=initial_stop, current_stop=initial_stop,
                        lot_size=lot_size, commission=lot_size * COMMISSION_PER_LOT
                    )
                    
                    if (curr_bar['high'] + (SPREAD / 2.0)) >= initial_stop:
                        self.active_trade.exit_time = curr_bar['datetime']
                        self.active_trade.exit_price = initial_stop
                        self.active_trade.exit_reason = 'SAME_BAR_SL'
                        price_diff = self.active_trade.entry_price - self.active_trade.exit_price
                        self.active_trade.gross_pnl = price_diff * lot_size * CONTRACT_SIZE
                        self.active_trade.net_pnl = self.active_trade.gross_pnl - self.active_trade.commission
                        self.balance += self.active_trade.net_pnl
                        self.trades.append(self.active_trade)
                        self.active_trade = None
                    else:
                        new_stop = curr_bar['close'] + (curr_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                        if new_stop < initial_stop: self.active_trade.current_stop = new_stop

        self.generate_report()

    def generate_report(self):
        if not self.trades:
            print('No trades executed in this period.')
            return

        df = pd.DataFrame([{'Net_PnL': t.net_pnl} for t in self.trades])
        wins = df[df['Net_PnL'] > 0]
        losses = df[df['Net_PnL'] <= 0]
        
        win_rate = (len(wins) / len(df)) * 100
        profit_factor = wins['Net_PnL'].sum() / abs(losses['Net_PnL'].sum()) if not losses.empty else float('inf')
        avg_win = wins['Net_PnL'].mean() if not wins.empty else 0
        avg_loss = abs(losses['Net_PnL'].mean()) if not losses.empty else 0
        expectancy = ((win_rate / 100) * avg_win) - (((100 - win_rate) / 100) * avg_loss)
        net_return_pct = ((self.balance - INITIAL_BALANCE) / INITIAL_BALANCE) * 100.0

        print(f"Starting Balance     : ${INITIAL_BALANCE:,.2f}")
        print(f"Final Balance        : ${self.balance:,.2f}")
        print(f"Net Return           : {net_return_pct:.2f}%")
        print(f"Total Trades         : {len(df)}")
        print(f"Net Profit           : ${df['Net_PnL'].sum():,.2f}")
        print(f"Profit Factor        : {profit_factor:.2f}")
        print(f"Win Rate             : {win_rate:.2f}%")
        print(f"Expectancy Per Trade : ${expectancy:,.2f}")
        print("-" * 60)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    col_names = ['date', 'time', 'open', 'high', 'low', 'close', 'volume']
    df_raw = pd.read_csv('XAUUSD5.csv', header=None, names=col_names)
    df_raw['datetime'] = pd.to_datetime(df_raw['date'] + ' ' + df_raw['time'], format='%Y.%m.%d %H:%M')
    df_raw.sort_values('datetime', inplace=True)
    df_raw.reset_index(drop=True, inplace=True)

    split_idx = int(len(df_raw) * OOS_SPLIT_RATIO)
    df_is = df_raw.iloc[:split_idx].copy()
    df_oos = df_raw.iloc[split_idx:].copy()

    print("="*60)
    print(f"IN-SAMPLE (IS) PERIOD: {df_is['datetime'].min()} to {df_is['datetime'].max()}")
    print("="*60)
    engine_is = OOSQVBBacktester(df_is)
    engine_is.run()

    print("\n" + "="*60)
    print(f"OUT-OF-SAMPLE (OOS) BLIND TEST: {df_oos['datetime'].min()} to {df_oos['datetime'].max()}")
    print("="*60)
    engine_oos = OOSQVBBacktester(df_oos)
    engine_oos.run()

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-v2\qvb_optimizer.py
================================================================================

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import List, Optional, Dict
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
import itertools

# ==============================================================================
# 1. FIXED QUANTITATIVE CONFIGURATION
# ==============================================================================
INITIAL_BALANCE = 10000.0  
RISK_PER_TRADE_PCT = 0.01  
MAX_DAILY_LOSS_PCT = 0.03  
MAX_WEEKLY_LOSS_PCT = 0.06

SPREAD = 0.25  
COMMISSION_PER_LOT = 5.0  
CONTRACT_SIZE = 100.0  
LOT_STEP = 0.01  
MIN_LOT = 0.01  

ATR_PERIOD = 14            
ATR_SMA_PERIOD = 50        
SESSION_START_HOUR = 8     
SESSION_END_HOUR = 17      

# ==============================================================================
# 2. DATA STRUCTURES
# ==============================================================================
@dataclass
class Trade:
    trade_id: int
    direction: str  
    entry_time: datetime
    entry_price: float
    initial_stop: float
    current_stop: float
    lot_size: float
    commission: float
    exit_time: Optional[datetime] = None
    exit_price: Optional[float] = None
    gross_pnl: float = 0.0
    net_pnl: float = 0.0
    exit_reason: str = '' 

# ==============================================================================
# 3. DYNAMIC T-1 QUANTITATIVE ENGINE
# ==============================================================================
class StrictQVBBacktester:
    def __init__(self, df_raw: pd.DataFrame, donchian_period: int, ema_period: int, trailing_atr_mult: float):
        self.donchian_period = donchian_period
        self.ema_period = ema_period
        self.trailing_atr_mult = trailing_atr_mult
        
        self.df = self._prepare_data(df_raw.copy())
        self.balance = INITIAL_BALANCE
        
        self.current_day = None
        self.current_week = None
        self.daily_start_balance = INITIAL_BALANCE
        self.weekly_start_balance = INITIAL_BALANCE
        self.daily_loss_locked = False
        self.weekly_loss_locked = False

        self.trades: List[Trade] = []
        self.active_trade: Optional[Trade] = None
        self.trade_counter = 0

    def _prepare_data(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        df['ema_200'] = df['close'].ewm(span=self.ema_period, adjust=False).mean()
        df['donchian_high_prev'] = df['high'].shift(1).rolling(window=self.donchian_period).max()
        df['donchian_low_prev'] = df['low'].shift(1).rolling(window=self.donchian_period).min()
        
        high_low = df['high'] - df['low']
        high_close = np.abs(df['high'] - df['close'].shift(1))
        low_close = np.abs(df['low'] - df['close'].shift(1))
        true_range = pd.concat([high_low, high_close, low_close], axis=1).max(axis=1)
        df['atr'] = true_range.ewm(span=ATR_PERIOD, adjust=False).mean()
        df['atr_sma'] = df['atr'].rolling(window=ATR_SMA_PERIOD).mean()
        
        return df

    def _update_calendar_limits(self, current_dt: datetime):
        day = current_dt.date()
        week = (current_dt.year, current_dt.isocalendar()[1])

        if self.current_day != day:
            self.current_day = day
            self.daily_start_balance = self.balance
            self.daily_loss_locked = False

        if self.current_week != week:
            self.current_week = week
            self.weekly_start_balance = self.balance
            self.weekly_loss_locked = False

        if (self.daily_start_balance - self.balance) / self.daily_start_balance >= MAX_DAILY_LOSS_PCT:
            self.daily_loss_locked = True
        if (self.weekly_start_balance - self.balance) / self.weekly_start_balance >= MAX_WEEKLY_LOSS_PCT:
            self.weekly_loss_locked = True

    def _calculate_lot_size(self, entry_price: float, sl_price: float) -> Optional[float]:
        sl_distance = abs(entry_price - sl_price)
        if sl_distance <= 0: return None
        loss_per_lot = (sl_distance * CONTRACT_SIZE) + COMMISSION_PER_LOT
        raw_lot = (self.balance * RISK_PER_TRADE_PCT) / loss_per_lot
        lot_size = round(math.floor(raw_lot / LOT_STEP) * LOT_STEP, 2)
        return lot_size if lot_size >= MIN_LOT else None

    def run(self) -> Dict:
        start_idx = max(self.donchian_period, self.ema_period, ATR_SMA_PERIOD) + 2

        for i in range(start_idx, len(self.df)):
            curr_bar = self.df.iloc[i]
            prev_bar = self.df.iloc[i-1]
            
            self._update_calendar_limits(curr_bar['datetime'])
            
            if self.active_trade:
                trade = self.active_trade
                trade_closed = False
                
                if trade.direction == 'BUY':
                    bid_low = curr_bar['low'] - (SPREAD / 2.0)
                    if bid_low <= trade.current_stop:
                        trade.exit_price = min(trade.current_stop, curr_bar['open'] - (SPREAD / 2.0))
                        trade_closed = True
                    else:
                        new_stop = curr_bar['close'] - (curr_bar['atr'] * self.trailing_atr_mult)
                        if new_stop > trade.current_stop:
                            trade.current_stop = new_stop

                elif trade.direction == 'SELL':
                    ask_high = curr_bar['high'] + (SPREAD / 2.0)
                    if ask_high >= trade.current_stop:
                        trade.exit_price = max(trade.current_stop, curr_bar['open'] + (SPREAD / 2.0))
                        trade_closed = True
                    else:
                        new_stop = curr_bar['close'] + (curr_bar['atr'] * self.trailing_atr_mult)
                        if new_stop < trade.current_stop:
                            trade.current_stop = new_stop

                if trade_closed:
                    trade.exit_time = curr_bar['datetime']
                    trade.exit_reason = 'TRAILING_SL'
                    price_diff = (trade.exit_price - trade.entry_price) if trade.direction == 'BUY' else (trade.entry_price - trade.exit_price)
                    trade.gross_pnl = price_diff * trade.lot_size * CONTRACT_SIZE
                    trade.net_pnl = trade.gross_pnl - trade.commission
                    self.balance += trade.net_pnl
                    self.trades.append(trade)
                    self.active_trade = None
                    
                continue

            if self.daily_loss_locked or self.weekly_loss_locked: continue
            if not (SESSION_START_HOUR <= curr_bar['datetime'].hour <= SESSION_END_HOUR): continue

            is_volatility_expanding = prev_bar['atr'] > prev_bar['atr_sma']
            
            if (prev_bar['close'] > prev_bar['donchian_high_prev']) and (prev_bar['close'] > prev_bar['ema_200']) and is_volatility_expanding:
                entry_price = curr_bar['open'] + (SPREAD / 2.0)
                initial_stop = entry_price - (prev_bar['atr'] * self.trailing_atr_mult)
                
                lot_size = self._calculate_lot_size(entry_price, initial_stop)
                if lot_size:
                    self.trade_counter += 1
                    self.active_trade = Trade(
                        trade_id=self.trade_counter, direction='BUY', entry_time=curr_bar['datetime'], 
                        entry_price=entry_price, initial_stop=initial_stop, current_stop=initial_stop,
                        lot_size=lot_size, commission=lot_size * COMMISSION_PER_LOT
                    )
                    
                    if (curr_bar['low'] - (SPREAD / 2.0)) <= initial_stop:
                        self.active_trade.exit_time = curr_bar['datetime']
                        self.active_trade.exit_price = initial_stop
                        self.active_trade.exit_reason = 'SAME_BAR_SL'
                        price_diff = self.active_trade.exit_price - self.active_trade.entry_price
                        self.active_trade.gross_pnl = price_diff * lot_size * CONTRACT_SIZE
                        self.active_trade.net_pnl = self.active_trade.gross_pnl - self.active_trade.commission
                        self.balance += self.active_trade.net_pnl
                        self.trades.append(self.active_trade)
                        self.active_trade = None
                    else:
                        new_stop = curr_bar['close'] - (curr_bar['atr'] * self.trailing_atr_mult)
                        if new_stop > initial_stop: self.active_trade.current_stop = new_stop
                            
            elif (prev_bar['close'] < prev_bar['donchian_low_prev']) and (prev_bar['close'] < prev_bar['ema_200']) and is_volatility_expanding:
                entry_price = curr_bar['open'] - (SPREAD / 2.0)
                initial_stop = entry_price + (prev_bar['atr'] * self.trailing_atr_mult)
                
                lot_size = self._calculate_lot_size(entry_price, initial_stop)
                if lot_size:
                    self.trade_counter += 1
                    self.active_trade = Trade(
                        trade_id=self.trade_counter, direction='SELL', entry_time=curr_bar['datetime'], 
                        entry_price=entry_price, initial_stop=initial_stop, current_stop=initial_stop,
                        lot_size=lot_size, commission=lot_size * COMMISSION_PER_LOT
                    )
                    
                    if (curr_bar['high'] + (SPREAD / 2.0)) >= initial_stop:
                        self.active_trade.exit_time = curr_bar['datetime']
                        self.active_trade.exit_price = initial_stop
                        self.active_trade.exit_reason = 'SAME_BAR_SL'
                        price_diff = self.active_trade.entry_price - self.active_trade.exit_price
                        self.active_trade.gross_pnl = price_diff * lot_size * CONTRACT_SIZE
                        self.active_trade.net_pnl = self.active_trade.gross_pnl - self.active_trade.commission
                        self.balance += self.active_trade.net_pnl
                        self.trades.append(self.active_trade)
                        self.active_trade = None
                    else:
                        new_stop = curr_bar['close'] + (curr_bar['atr'] * self.trailing_atr_mult)
                        if new_stop < initial_stop: self.active_trade.current_stop = new_stop

        return self._generate_metrics()

    def _generate_metrics(self) -> Dict:
        if not self.trades:
            return {'Net_Profit': 0, 'Win_Rate': 0, 'Expectancy': 0, 'Trades': 0, 'Profit_Factor': 0}
        
        df = pd.DataFrame([{'Net_PnL': t.net_pnl} for t in self.trades])
        wins = df[df['Net_PnL'] > 0]
        losses = df[df['Net_PnL'] <= 0]
        
        win_rate = (len(wins) / len(df)) * 100
        gross_profit = wins['Net_PnL'].sum()
        gross_loss = abs(losses['Net_PnL'].sum())
        profit_factor = gross_profit / gross_loss if gross_loss > 0 else float('inf')
        
        avg_win = wins['Net_PnL'].mean() if not wins.empty else 0
        avg_loss = abs(losses['Net_PnL'].mean()) if not losses.empty else 0
        expectancy = ((win_rate / 100) * avg_win) - (((100 - win_rate) / 100) * avg_loss)
        
        return {
            'Donchian': self.donchian_period,
            'EMA': self.ema_period,
            'ATR_Mult': self.trailing_atr_mult,
            'Net_Profit': round(df['Net_PnL'].sum(), 2),
            'Trades': len(df),
            'Win_Rate': round(win_rate, 2),
            'Profit_Factor': round(profit_factor, 2),
            'Expectancy': round(expectancy, 2)
        }

# ==============================================================================
# 4. GRID SEARCH EXECUTION
# ==============================================================================
if __name__ == '__main__':
    print("Loading historical data for optimization...")
    col_names = ['date', 'time', 'open', 'high', 'low', 'close', 'volume']
    df_raw = pd.read_csv('XAUUSD5.csv', header=None, names=col_names)
    df_raw['datetime'] = pd.to_datetime(df_raw['date'] + ' ' + df_raw['time'], format='%Y.%m.%d %H:%M')
    df_raw.sort_values('datetime', inplace=True)
    df_raw.reset_index(drop=True, inplace=True)

    # Define Parameter Grid
    donchian_periods = [30, 40, 50, 60]
    ema_periods = [200]
    atr_multipliers = [2.0, 2.5, 3.0, 3.5]
    
    combinations = list(itertools.product(donchian_periods, ema_periods, atr_multipliers))
    print(f"Executing Grid Search across {len(combinations)} parameter combinations...\n")

    results = []
    for donchian, ema, atr_mult in combinations:
        engine = StrictQVBBacktester(df_raw, donchian, ema, atr_mult)
        metrics = engine.run()
        results.append(metrics)
        print(f"Tested Donchian: {donchian:2}, ATR Mult: {atr_mult:.1f} | Net: ${metrics['Net_Profit']:7.2f} | WR: {metrics['Win_Rate']:5.2f}% | Exp: ${metrics['Expectancy']:5.2f}")

    results_df = pd.DataFrame(results)
    results_df.sort_values(by='Expectancy', ascending=False, inplace=True)
    results_df.to_csv('qvb_optimization_results.csv', index=False)
    
    print("\n" + "="*60)
    print("                 TOP 5 ROBUST PARAMETER SETS                ")
    print("="*60)
    print(results_df.head(5).to_string(index=False))
    print("="*60)

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-v2\qvb_stress_test.py
================================================================================

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import List, Optional
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np

# ==============================================================================
# CONFIGURATION
# ==============================================================================
INITIAL_BALANCE = 10000.0  
RISK_PER_TRADE_PCT = 0.01  
MAX_DAILY_LOSS_PCT = 0.03  
MAX_WEEKLY_LOSS_PCT = 0.06

COMMISSION_PER_LOT = 5.0  
CONTRACT_SIZE = 100.0  
LOT_STEP = 0.01  
MIN_LOT = 0.01  

# Winning Parameters
DONCHIAN_PERIOD = 60       
EMA_PERIOD = 200           
TRAILING_ATR_MULT = 2.5    

ATR_PERIOD = 14            
ATR_SMA_PERIOD = 50        

SESSION_START_HOUR = 8     
SESSION_END_HOUR = 17      
OOS_SPLIT_RATIO = 0.70     

@dataclass
class Trade:
    trade_id: int
    direction: str  
    entry_time: datetime
    entry_price: float
    initial_stop: float
    current_stop: float
    lot_size: float
    commission: float
    exit_time: Optional[datetime] = None
    exit_price: Optional[float] = None
    gross_pnl: float = 0.0
    net_pnl: float = 0.0
    exit_reason: str = '' 

class StressTestQVBBacktester:
    def __init__(self, df_subset: pd.DataFrame, spread: float):
        self.spread = spread
        self.df = self._prepare_data(df_subset.copy())
        self.balance = INITIAL_BALANCE
        
        self.current_day = None
        self.current_week = None
        self.daily_start_balance = INITIAL_BALANCE
        self.weekly_start_balance = INITIAL_BALANCE
        self.daily_loss_locked = False
        self.weekly_loss_locked = False

        self.trades: List[Trade] = []
        self.active_trade: Optional[Trade] = None
        self.trade_counter = 0

    def _prepare_data(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        df['ema_200'] = df['close'].ewm(span=EMA_PERIOD, adjust=False).mean()
        df['donchian_high_prev'] = df['high'].shift(1).rolling(window=DONCHIAN_PERIOD).max()
        df['donchian_low_prev'] = df['low'].shift(1).rolling(window=DONCHIAN_PERIOD).min()
        
        high_low = df['high'] - df['low']
        high_close = np.abs(df['high'] - df['close'].shift(1))
        low_close = np.abs(df['low'] - df['close'].shift(1))
        true_range = pd.concat([high_low, high_close, low_close], axis=1).max(axis=1)
        df['atr'] = true_range.ewm(span=ATR_PERIOD, adjust=False).mean()
        df['atr_sma'] = df['atr'].rolling(window=ATR_SMA_PERIOD).mean()
        return df

    def _update_calendar_limits(self, current_dt: datetime):
        day = current_dt.date()
        week = (current_dt.year, current_dt.isocalendar()[1])

        if self.current_day != day:
            self.current_day = day
            self.daily_start_balance = self.balance
            self.daily_loss_locked = False

        if self.current_week != week:
            self.current_week = week
            self.weekly_start_balance = self.balance
            self.weekly_loss_locked = False

        if (self.daily_start_balance - self.balance) / self.daily_start_balance >= MAX_DAILY_LOSS_PCT:
            self.daily_loss_locked = True
        if (self.weekly_start_balance - self.balance) / self.weekly_start_balance >= MAX_WEEKLY_LOSS_PCT:
            self.weekly_loss_locked = True

    def _calculate_lot_size(self, entry_price: float, sl_price: float) -> Optional[float]:
        sl_distance = abs(entry_price - sl_price)
        if sl_distance <= 0: return None
        loss_per_lot = (sl_distance * CONTRACT_SIZE) + COMMISSION_PER_LOT
        raw_lot = (self.balance * RISK_PER_TRADE_PCT) / loss_per_lot
        lot_size = round(math.floor(raw_lot / LOT_STEP) * LOT_STEP, 2)
        return lot_size if lot_size >= MIN_LOT else None

    def run(self) -> dict:
        start_idx = max(DONCHIAN_PERIOD, EMA_PERIOD, ATR_SMA_PERIOD) + 2

        for i in range(start_idx, len(self.df)):
            curr_bar = self.df.iloc[i]
            prev_bar = self.df.iloc[i-1]
            
            self._update_calendar_limits(curr_bar['datetime'])
            
            # 1. Manage Exits
            if self.active_trade:
                trade = self.active_trade
                trade_closed = False
                
                if trade.direction == 'BUY':
                    bid_low = curr_bar['low'] - (self.spread / 2.0)
                    if bid_low <= trade.current_stop:
                        trade.exit_price = min(trade.current_stop, curr_bar['open'] - (self.spread / 2.0))
                        trade_closed = True
                    else:
                        new_stop = curr_bar['close'] - (curr_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                        if new_stop > trade.current_stop:
                            trade.current_stop = new_stop

                elif trade.direction == 'SELL':
                    ask_high = curr_bar['high'] + (self.spread / 2.0)
                    if ask_high >= trade.current_stop:
                        trade.exit_price = max(trade.current_stop, curr_bar['open'] + (self.spread / 2.0))
                        trade_closed = True
                    else:
                        new_stop = curr_bar['close'] + (curr_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                        if new_stop < trade.current_stop:
                            trade.current_stop = new_stop

                if trade_closed:
                    trade.exit_time = curr_bar['datetime']
                    trade.exit_reason = 'TRAILING_SL'
                    price_diff = (trade.exit_price - trade.entry_price) if trade.direction == 'BUY' else (trade.entry_price - trade.exit_price)
                    trade.gross_pnl = price_diff * trade.lot_size * CONTRACT_SIZE
                    trade.net_pnl = trade.gross_pnl - trade.commission
                    self.balance += trade.net_pnl
                    self.trades.append(trade)
                    self.active_trade = None
                    
                continue

            if self.daily_loss_locked or self.weekly_loss_locked: continue
            if not (SESSION_START_HOUR <= curr_bar['datetime'].hour <= SESSION_END_HOUR): continue

            is_volatility_expanding = prev_bar['atr'] > prev_bar['atr_sma']
            
            # 2. Entries
            if (prev_bar['close'] > prev_bar['donchian_high_prev']) and (prev_bar['close'] > prev_bar['ema_200']) and is_volatility_expanding:
                entry_price = curr_bar['open'] + (self.spread / 2.0)
                initial_stop = entry_price - (prev_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                
                lot_size = self._calculate_lot_size(entry_price, initial_stop)
                if lot_size:
                    self.trade_counter += 1
                    self.active_trade = Trade(
                        trade_id=self.trade_counter, direction='BUY', entry_time=curr_bar['datetime'], 
                        entry_price=entry_price, initial_stop=initial_stop, current_stop=initial_stop,
                        lot_size=lot_size, commission=lot_size * COMMISSION_PER_LOT
                    )
                    
                    if (curr_bar['low'] - (self.spread / 2.0)) <= initial_stop:
                        self.active_trade.exit_time = curr_bar['datetime']
                        self.active_trade.exit_price = initial_stop
                        self.active_trade.exit_reason = 'SAME_BAR_SL'
                        price_diff = self.active_trade.exit_price - self.active_trade.entry_price
                        self.active_trade.gross_pnl = price_diff * lot_size * CONTRACT_SIZE
                        self.active_trade.net_pnl = self.active_trade.gross_pnl - self.active_trade.commission
                        self.balance += self.active_trade.net_pnl
                        self.trades.append(self.active_trade)
                        self.active_trade = None
                    else:
                        new_stop = curr_bar['close'] - (curr_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                        if new_stop > initial_stop: self.active_trade.current_stop = new_stop
                            
            elif (prev_bar['close'] < prev_bar['donchian_low_prev']) and (prev_bar['close'] < prev_bar['ema_200']) and is_volatility_expanding:
                entry_price = curr_bar['open'] - (self.spread / 2.0)
                initial_stop = entry_price + (prev_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                
                lot_size = self._calculate_lot_size(entry_price, initial_stop)
                if lot_size:
                    self.trade_counter += 1
                    self.active_trade = Trade(
                        trade_id=self.trade_counter, direction='SELL', entry_time=curr_bar['datetime'], 
                        entry_price=entry_price, initial_stop=initial_stop, current_stop=initial_stop,
                        lot_size=lot_size, commission=lot_size * COMMISSION_PER_LOT
                    )
                    
                    if (curr_bar['high'] + (self.spread / 2.0)) >= initial_stop:
                        self.active_trade.exit_time = curr_bar['datetime']
                        self.active_trade.exit_price = initial_stop
                        self.active_trade.exit_reason = 'SAME_BAR_SL'
                        price_diff = self.active_trade.entry_price - self.active_trade.exit_price
                        self.active_trade.gross_pnl = price_diff * lot_size * CONTRACT_SIZE
                        self.active_trade.net_pnl = self.active_trade.gross_pnl - self.active_trade.commission
                        self.balance += self.active_trade.net_pnl
                        self.trades.append(self.active_trade)
                        self.active_trade = None
                    else:
                        new_stop = curr_bar['close'] + (curr_bar['atr'] * TRAILING_ATR_MULT)
                        if new_stop < initial_stop: self.active_trade.current_stop = new_stop

        return self._generate_metrics()

    def _generate_metrics(self) -> dict:
        if not self.trades:
            return {'Spread': self.spread, 'Net_Profit': 0, 'Win_Rate': 0, 'Profit_Factor': 0, 'Expectancy': 0}
        
        df = pd.DataFrame([{'Net_PnL': t.net_pnl} for t in self.trades])
        wins = df[df['Net_PnL'] > 0]
        losses = df[df['Net_PnL'] <= 0]
        
        win_rate = (len(wins) / len(df)) * 100
        gross_profit = wins['Net_PnL'].sum()
        gross_loss = abs(losses['Net_PnL'].sum())
        profit_factor = gross_profit / gross_loss if gross_loss > 0 else float('inf')
        
        avg_win = wins['Net_PnL'].mean() if not wins.empty else 0
        avg_loss = abs(losses['Net_PnL'].mean()) if not losses.empty else 0
        expectancy = ((win_rate / 100) * avg_win) - (((100 - win_rate) / 100) * avg_loss)
        
        return {
            'Spread': self.spread,
            'Net_Profit': round(df['Net_PnL'].sum(), 2),
            'Win_Rate': round(win_rate, 2),
            'Profit_Factor': round(profit_factor, 2),
            'Expectancy': round(expectancy, 2)
        }

if __name__ == '__main__':
    col_names = ['date', 'time', 'open', 'high', 'low', 'close', 'volume']
    df_raw = pd.read_csv('XAUUSD5.csv', header=None, names=col_names)
    df_raw['datetime'] = pd.to_datetime(df_raw['date'] + ' ' + df_raw['time'], format='%Y.%m.%d %H:%M')
    df_raw.sort_values('datetime', inplace=True)
    df_raw.reset_index(drop=True, inplace=True)

    # Isolate Out-of-Sample dataset
    split_idx = int(len(df_raw) * OOS_SPLIT_RATIO)
    df_oos = df_raw.iloc[split_idx:].copy()

    test_spreads = [0.25, 0.35, 0.45, 0.55, 0.65]
    results = []

    print("\n" + "="*65)
    print("      OOS SPREAD STRESS-TEST RESULTS (XAUUSD M5)       ")
    print("="*65)
    for s in test_spreads:
        tester = StressTestQVBBacktester(df_oos, spread=s)
        res = tester.run()
        results.append(res)
        print(f"Spread: ${s:4.2f} | Net Profit: ${res['Net_Profit']:8.2f} | PF: {res['Profit_Factor']:4.2f} | WR: {res['Win_Rate']:5.2f}% | Exp: ${res['Expectancy']:5.2f}")
    print("="*65)

================================================================================
FILE: trade-bot-v2\qvb_wfo.py
